# HS2Next bridge commands

Generated by `Tools/gen_commands_md.py` from a `help` dump (`Editor/BridgeHelpDump.cs` →
`Spike/out/bridge/help_dump.json`); do not edit by hand. To refresh it after a command change:

```
& Tools\unity_batch.ps1 -Method BridgeHelpDump.Run -NoGraphics
python -I Tools/gen_commands_md.py
```

Protocol 1; 265 commands (260 core, 5 bridge); 111 HS2-parity names; 107 experimental. `help` and `help {name}` answer the same facts at run time.

## Driving HS2Next from a script

HS2Next listens for automation requests while it runs (the Editor's play mode and every player): HTTP on `127.0.0.1`,
port 17322 by default (setting `bridge.port`, command line `-bridgePort N`). Only programs on the same computer can connect.
Use `127.0.0.1` in URLs: a `Host: localhost` header is answered with 400. The bridge is on by default and can be switched off
in File > Options > Automation, with `"bridge": {"enabled": false}` in the settings or with `-noBridge`.

**Requests.** `POST /` with a JSON object `{"cmd": "<name>", ...arguments}` (UTF-8, at most 16 MiB). An empty body is a
`ping`. Every reply is HTTP 200 with `{"ok": true, "cmd": "<name>", ...returned fields}` or
`{"ok": false, "error": "<message>", "code": "<code>", "detail": {...}}`; scripts branch on `code`, never on the message
text. The transport refuses a request before any command runs with an HTTP status: 400 (the body is not a JSON object),
401 (token missing or wrong), 403 (a browser request: any `Origin` or `Sec-Fetch-*` header, whatever its value; or a remote
address), 404 (a path other than `/`), 405 (a method other than POST), 413 (a body over 16 MiB). Commands run in order on
the app's main thread, one queue for all clients; `ping`, `help`, `bridge.version` and `log.tail` sent alone are answered at
once, also while the main thread is busy.

**Envelope members** that any request may carry besides the command's arguments: `id` (any JSON value, echoed in the reply),
`timeout` (seconds, at most 600; default 60 or the command's own), `waitFrames` (frames to wait after the command before the
reply), `strict` (`true`: an unknown argument is an error instead of a warning), `undo` (`false`: a change is made without an
undo entry) and, inside a `batch`, `as` (names a member's reply; later members refer to it as `"$name.field"`).

**Token.** The release build requires a token by default: send the header `X-HS2Next-Token` with the content of the file
`<UserData>/Bridge/token`, which the app writes anew at each start. Development builds and the Editor require none unless the
setting `bridge.token` asks for one.

**Finding a running app.** While it listens, the app writes `<UserData>/Bridge/endpoint.json` with `url`, `pid`, `startedAt`
and `tokenRequired`, and removes it when it stops. `<UserData>` is `%USERPROFILE%\AppData\LocalLow\stp3\HS2Next\UserData`
for an installed player, the `UserData` folder next to `HS2Next.exe` in portable mode, and the project's `UserData/` in the
Editor; `app.paths` reports the folders of the running app.

**First steps.** `ping` reports the app's readiness stages (wait until it is ready before loading anything); `help` lists the
groups and commands; `{"cmd": "help", "name": "<command>"}` returns one command's description as below; `bridge.version`
gives the protocol, Api and app versions. `batch` runs a list of requests in order and returns their replies.
`Tools/hs2n_bridge.py` is a small client (library and command line, Python standard library only) that finds the endpoint and
the token by the rules above:

```
python Tools/hs2n_bridge.py ping
python Tools/hs2n_bridge.py '{"cmd": "help", "name": "scene.load"}'
python Tools/hs2n_bridge.py '[{"cmd": "scene.new"}, {"cmd": "map.load", "no": 10}]'
```

**Error codes.**

| Code | Meaning |
|---|---|
| `bad_json` | the body is not a JSON object |
| `unknown_command` | no command of that name (`detail.candidates`: the nearest names) |
| `bad_args` | an argument is missing, of the wrong type or out of range (`detail.arg`, `detail.expected`) |
| `not_found` | a named thing (node, file, list row, help entry) does not exist |
| `wrong_kind` | the target exists but is of another kind (an item key given to a character command) |
| `not_ready` | the app is not ready for this command yet (`detail.extra.ready`: the stages) |
| `busy` | the bridge cannot take the request now (queue full, a re-entrant call) |
| `timeout` | the request did not complete within its timeout |
| `io_denied` | a file path points outside the allowed folders, or would overwrite a file without `overwrite` |
| `unsupported` | the feature is not available in this version or is switched off |
| `plugin_fault` | a plugin's handler failed |
| `internal` | a built-in handler failed unexpectedly |
| `unavailable` | the target exists but is not built (a character whose build failed or is pending) |

**What stays stable.** The protocol number changes only when the envelope changes. Within the 1.x versions every command
that is not marked *experimental* keeps its name, its arguments (name, type, whether required, declared default), its
returned fields and its flags: a new version may add commands, optional arguments and returned fields, and a renamed
command keeps its old name as an alias. Experimental commands (UI introspection, dumps, diagnostics) may change or go in any
version. Help texts are descriptions, not part of the promise, and error codes may be added but never change meaning.
Plugins add commands of their own; `help` lists them with the plugin's GUID as owner (see `Reference/PLUGINS.md`).

## Naming convention

- A name is `<group>.<verb or noun>` in camelCase (`chara.edit.hairBundle`, `scene.load`); the group is the subject: `scene`,
  `obj` (any node), `chara`, `chara.edit` (the Maker), `item`, `map`, `camera`, `light`, `route`, `fk`, `ik`, `pose`, `anime`,
  `effects`, `sound`, `content`, `app`, `ui`, `plugin`, `bridge`, `log`, `timeline` (the scene's Timeline), `overrides` (the
  runtime override layer), `record` (frame recording), `materials` (what material edits share), `constraint` (the scene's
  constraints).
- Reads are `state`, `get`, `list` or a noun; writes are `set` or a verb.
- **HS2-parity names** (marked *HS2 parity* below) keep HS2 Studio's established automation vocabulary: they are never renamed
  and keep HS2's argument names, defaults, reply members and number forms.
- The one-word names registered before this convention was written down stay as they are; new Core names follow the
  convention.
- A plugin's command names start with one of its manifest's `commandPrefixes`; its owner and help group are the plugin's GUID.
- A rename adds the old name as an alias (`CommandSpec.Alias`): the old name still reaches the command and its reply carries
  the warning `deprecated: use <name>`. No command has been renamed so far.

## Group: advanced pose

<a id="cmd-pose-blendshapes"></a>

### `pose.blendShapes` · *experimental*

The skinned renderers with blend shapes of a built character, with each shape's current weight and stored override. What this character lacks is listed by stale.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `renderer` | `string` |  |  | only this renderer's address: head/\<name>, body/\<name>, hair:\<k>/\<name>, clothes:\<k>/\<name> or acc:\<id>/\<name>, with #\<n> for the n-th of a repeated name |
| `part` | `string` |  |  | only this part: head, body, hair:\<k>, clothes:\<k> or acc:\<id> |
| `filter` | `string` |  |  | only shapes whose name holds this text, ignoring case; a renderer without one is left out |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `renderers` | `array<object>` | yes | {renderer (the address), part, name, protected (a part that keeps the mosaic in place), shapes: [{name, weight, override (null: none)}]} |
| `stale` | `array<string>` | yes | stored renderers this character lacks (the address), stored shapes a renderer lacks (\<address>:\<shape>) |

Example: `{"cmd": "pose.blendShapes", "key": "<id>", "renderer": "head/o_tooth"}`

<a id="cmd-pose-blendshapes-clear"></a>

### `pose.blendShapes.clear` · *experimental*

Removes blend-shape weights (one history entry): one shape of a renderer, a renderer's, or all of them.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `renderer` | `string` |  |  | one renderer's address |
| `shape` | `string` |  |  | with renderer: only this shape |
| `all` | `bool` |  |  | true: every weight of this character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `removed` | `int` | yes | weights removed |

Example: `{"all": true, "cmd": "pose.blendShapes.clear", "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-pose-blendshapes-set"></a>

### `pose.blendShapes.set` · *experimental*

Sets blend-shape weights of one renderer (one history entry). A weight wins over the face on every step; a weight given as null is removed, and the face or the mesh value shows again. A part that keeps the mosaic in place answers unsupported.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `renderer` | `string` | yes |  | the renderer's address: head/\<name>, body/\<name>, hair:\<k>/\<name>, clothes:\<k>/\<name> or acc:\<id>/\<name>, with #\<n> for the n-th of a repeated name |
| `shape` | `string` |  |  | one shape's name, with weight |
| `weight` | `float` |  |  | with shape: its weight, -200..300 (0..100 on a slider); null removes it |
| `weights` | `object` |  |  | several shapes instead of shape: {shape: weight}; null removes that weight |
| `keepMissing` | `bool` |  | `false` | store a weight for a renderer or a shape a built character lacks (it acts when that is there); without it such a target answers not_found |
| `merge` | `bool` |  | `false` | join the history entry of the previous edit of this renderer |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `renderer` | `string` | yes | the address |
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | the shapes whose weight changed |
| `removed` | `array<string>` | yes | the shapes whose weight left |

Example: `{"cmd": "pose.blendShapes.set", "key": "<id>", "renderer": "head/o_tooth", "shape": "<shape>", "weight": 50}`

<a id="cmd-pose-bones"></a>

### `pose.bones` · *experimental*

The bones of a built character that a correction can address, with their corrections. A correction whose bone this character lacks is listed by stale.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `part` | `string` |  |  | only this part: body, head, hair:\<k>, clothes:\<k> or acc:\<id> (default: every part) |
| `filter` | `string` |  |  | only bones whose name holds this text, ignoring case |
| `correctedOnly` | `bool` |  | `false` | only bones with a correction |
| `limit` | `int` |  | `2000` | at most this many rows |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bones` | `array<object>` | yes | {bone (the address), name, parent (its address, null at the top of a part), depth, part, fkGroup (null when the bone has no FK row), corrected, pos, rot, scale, shown (false: a later stage writes this bone, so the value does not show now), physics (a swing chain writes this bone), protected (a part that keeps the mosaic in place)} |
| `stale` | `array<string>` | yes | stored addresses that act on nothing here |
| `count` | `int` | yes | the bones that match, before the limit |

Example: `{"cmd": "pose.bones", "filter": "Mouth", "key": "<id>", "part": "head"}`

<a id="cmd-pose-bones-clear"></a>

### `pose.bones.clear` · *experimental*

Removes corrections (one history entry): one bone, several, or all of them.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `bone` | `string` |  |  | one address |
| `bones` | `array<string>` |  |  | several addresses |
| `all` | `bool` |  |  | true: every correction of this character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `removed` | `int` | yes | entries removed |

Example: `{"all": true, "cmd": "pose.bones.clear", "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-pose-bones-set"></a>

### `pose.bones.set` · *experimental*

Sets corrections of bones (one history entry): pos is added to the local position, the local rotation is multiplied by rot (Euler degrees) and each axis of the local scale by scale. Members left out keep their value; a correction that changes nothing removes the entry. A part that keeps the mosaic in place answers unsupported.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `bone` | `string` |  |  | one bone's address: its name, or hair:\<k>/\<name>, clothes:\<k>/\<name>, acc:\<id>/\<name> with #\<n> for the n-th of a repeated name |
| `pos` | `vec3` |  |  | with bone: the position offset, each component within +-1000 |
| `rot` | `euler` |  |  | with bone: the rotation offset, Euler degrees |
| `scale` | `vec3` |  |  | with bone: the scale factor, each component in 0.001..1000 |
| `bones` | `object` |  |  | several bones instead of bone: {address: {pos, rot, scale}}, each member optional |
| `keepMissing` | `bool` |  | `false` | store a correction for a bone a built character lacks (it acts when the bone is there); without it such a bone answers not_found |
| `merge` | `bool` |  | `false` | with one bone: join the history entry of the previous edit of this bone |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | the addresses whose correction changed |
| `removed` | `array<string>` | yes | the addresses whose entry left |
| `bones` | `object` | yes | {address: {pos, rot, scale}} of the named bones that have a correction now |

Example: `{"bone": "cf_J_Hand_Index02_L", "cmd": "pose.bones.set", "key": "<id>", "rot": [0, 0, 15]}`

<a id="cmd-pose-bust"></a>

### `pose.bust` · *experimental*

The two breast and two hip chains of a built character with their values: base as the card gives them, override as the scene stores them, values as they act.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `chains` | `array<object>` | yes | {chain (breastL, breastR, hipL, hipR), active, values, base, override (null: none)}; values and base: {enabled, gravity, force, damping, elasticity, stiffness, inert} |

Example: `{"cmd": "pose.bust", "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-pose-bust-clear"></a>

### `pose.bust.clear` · *experimental*

Removes the scene values of one breast or hip chain or of all four (one history entry); the card's values act again.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `chain` | `enum(breastL\|breastR\|hipL\|hipR)` |  |  | one chain |
| `all` | `bool` |  |  | true: all four |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `removed` | `int` | yes | chains cleared |

Example: `{"all": true, "cmd": "pose.bust.clear", "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-pose-bust-set"></a>

### `pose.bust.set` · *experimental*

Sets scene values of one breast or hip chain (one history entry). The card's values stay as they are; a member given as null goes back to the card's value.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `chain` | `enum(breastL\|breastR\|hipL\|hipR)` | yes |  | the chain |
| `enabled` | `bool` |  |  | false stops the chain; true never overrides the FK breast or the animation's switches |
| `gravity` | `vec3` |  |  | [x, y, z] |
| `force` | `vec3` |  |  | [x, y, z] |
| `damping` | `float` |  |  | 0..1 |
| `elasticity` | `float` |  |  | 0..1 |
| `stiffness` | `float` |  |  | 0..1 |
| `inert` | `float` |  |  | 0..1 |
| `merge` | `bool` |  | `false` | join the history entry of the previous edit of this chain |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `chain` | `string` | yes | the chain |
| `override` | `object` | yes | the members stored now (null: none) |
| `values` | `object` | yes | the values as they act (null when the character is not built or the body has no such chain) |

Example: `{"chain": "breastL", "cmd": "pose.bust.set", "damping": 0.3, "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-pose-chains"></a>

### `pose.chains` · *experimental*

The swing chains of a built character with their values: base as the prefab and the card give them, override as the scene stores them, values as they act. A stored chain this character lacks is listed by stale.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `chains` | `array<object>` | yes | {chain (the address), part, root, category, particles, active, noShake, values, base, override (null: none)}; values and base: {enabled, weight, damping, elasticity, stiffness, inert, radius, gravity, force, freezeAxis} |
| `stale` | `array<string>` | yes | stored addresses that act on nothing here |

Example: `{"cmd": "pose.chains", "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-pose-chains-clear"></a>

### `pose.chains.clear` · *experimental*

Removes the scene values of one swing chain or of all of them (one history entry); the card's values act again.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `chain` | `string` |  |  | one address |
| `all` | `bool` |  |  | true: every chain of this character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `removed` | `int` | yes | entries removed |

Example: `{"all": true, "cmd": "pose.chains.clear", "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-pose-chains-set"></a>

### `pose.chains.set` · *experimental*

Sets scene values of one swing chain (one history entry). They are read in front of the card's values, which stay as they are; a member given as null goes back to the card's value.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `chain` | `string` | yes |  | the chain's address: hair:\<k>/\<root>, clothes:\<k>/\<root>, acc:\<id>/\<root> or other/\<root>, with #\<n> for the n-th chain of that root in the part |
| `enabled` | `bool` |  |  | false stops the chain and its bones return to the rest pose; true does not override a card's no-shake |
| `weight` | `float` |  |  | 0..1 |
| `damping` | `float` |  |  | 0..1 |
| `elasticity` | `float` |  |  | 0..1 |
| `stiffness` | `float` |  |  | 0..1 |
| `inert` | `float` |  |  | 0..1 |
| `radius` | `float` |  |  | from 0 |
| `gravity` | `vec3` |  |  | [x, y, z] |
| `force` | `vec3` |  |  | [x, y, z] |
| `freezeAxis` | `int` |  |  | 0 none, 1 x, 2 y, 3 z |
| `merge` | `bool` |  | `false` | join the history entry of the previous edit of this chain |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `chain` | `string` | yes | the address |
| `override` | `object` | yes | the members stored now (null: none) |
| `values` | `object` | yes | the values as they act (null when the character is not built) |

Example: `{"chain": "hair:1/<root>", "cmd": "pose.chains.set", "damping": 0.2, "gravity": null, "key": "<id>"}`

## Group: app

<a id="cmd-app-options"></a>

### `app.options`

Runtime parity switches for measurements (never saved): extension modules of later builds, FK options, the joint-correction default of later characters, item, rendering, FK / IK and route settings, the Maker switches, a rebuild.

- flags `mutates`, `slow`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 300 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `ext` | `object` |  |  | {\<member>: bool} of the character extension modules (camelCase CharaExtOptions members: enabled, moreAccessories, abmx, invisibleBody, ...); later builds |
| `fk` | `object` |  |  | {resetToIdentity, missingBonesZero, hipsOffset}: FkOptions (later FK applications) |
| `correctionAllOn` | `bool` |  |  | joint correction of characters added later all on (HS2US "Auto Joint Correction") or all off |
| `items` | `object` |  |  | {gimmickIcons: bool}: item gimmick icons shown (Studio's system-menu switch) |
| `render` | `object` |  |  | {aa, taaQuality, grading, ambient, lightFalloff, shadowDistance, backgroundColor, ssr (off \| on; default off)}: the rendering settings (HS2Next.RenderOptions); mosaicWide (bool): the wide screen-space mosaic over the genital-area parts (default on; the option render.mosaicWide overwrites it when changed) |
| `kinematics` | `object` |  |  | {exclusive, rotationLimits, limitMinInterior (0–179 degrees), missingTargetsFirstFrame}: the FK / IK options (IkOptions): exclusive = switching FK on switches IK off and the reverse; missingTargetsFirstFrame applies to later builds |
| `routes` | `object` |  |  | {timing (hs2\|length), orientSmoothing, pointLimit, lineWidth (0.1–4)}: the route settings (RouteSettings; the UI options routes.*, which overwrite them when changed) |
| `maker` | `object` |  |  | {keepColours: bool, copyAccessoryPluginData: bool, bodyThrottle: "auto" \| "always" \| "off"}: the Maker switches without the UI (keep colours on an item change = MakerServices.KeepColoursOnItemChange, default off; copy MaterialEditor / DynamicBoneEditor / Additional Accessory Controls data with an accessory slot copy = MakerServices.CopyAccessoryPluginData, default off; the body composite drag throttle = CharaEditFlush.BodyThrottle, default auto); the UI options maker.keepColours / maker.copyAccessoryPluginData / maker.bodyThrottle overwrite them when changed |
| `rebuild` | `bool` |  | `false` | rebuild every node (and the map) with the new options (slow) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ext` | `object` | yes | current extension switches |
| `fk` | `object` | yes | current FK options |
| `correctionAllOn` | `bool` | yes | current correction default |
| `items` | `object` | yes | current item switches {gimmickIcons} |
| `render` | `object` | yes | current rendering settings (render.*; taaExperimental true until U9 passes) |
| `kinematics` | `object` | yes | current FK / IK options {exclusive, rotationLimits, limitMinInterior, missingTargetsFirstFrame} |
| `routes` | `object` | yes | current route settings {timing, orientSmoothing, pointLimit, lineWidth} |
| `maker` | `object` | yes | current Maker switches {keepColours, copyAccessoryPluginData, bodyThrottle} |

Example: `{"cmd": "app.options", "correctionAllOn": true, "ext": {"abmx": false}}`

<a id="cmd-app-paths"></a>

### `app.paths`

The folders of this process (read-only): data root and mode, exe folder, settings file, UserData, output root, cache, plugin folder, HS2 folder, where each came from, fallbacks, the copy offer, the longest projected cache path.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mode` | `string` | yes | editor \| portable \| override \| perUser |
| `dataRoot` | `path` | yes |  |
| `exeDir` | `path` | yes |  |
| `settingsFile` | `path` | yes | the file this process reads and the guide / settings page write (it may not exist) |
| `userData` | `path` | yes |  |
| `outputDir` | `path` | yes |  |
| `cacheDir` | `path` | yes |  |
| `pluginsDir` | `path` | yes |  |
| `hs2Root` | `path` | yes | null when none |
| `sources` | `object` | yes | {settings, userData, outputDir, cacheDir}: launch \| arg \| file \| default \| fallback |
| `explicit` | `array<string>` | yes | the folders given on the command line (or by the Editor launch override) |
| `fallback` | `object` |  | {kind, from, to, reason} of the first folder that fell back to the per-user folder |
| `migration` | `object` |  | {offered, source, files, bytes}: the copy of an earlier UserData folder |
| `longestPath` | `int` | yes | the longest projected path of the cache (characters) |
| `tokenAcl` | `string` | yes | none \| inherited \| ownerOnly: the token file's access list |

<a id="cmd-app-perf"></a>

### `app.perf` · *experimental*

Performance numbers of the running app (read-only): frame times since the last reset, the motion stages' times per phase (from the first request with motion on), the dynamic-bone stages, process memory, the UnloadUnusedAssets calls and, on request, the engine object counts.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `reset` | `bool` |  | `false` | after this reply, start the frame and motion samples anew |
| `motion` | `bool` |  | `false` | time the motion stages from now on (they are not timed until a request asks once) and add the motion section |
| `objects` | `bool` |  | `false` | add the engine object counts (gameObjects, materials, meshes, textures, renderTextures) |
| `census` | `bool` |  | `false` | with objects: add the GameObjects and Materials by name (leak diagnosis) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `frames` | `int` | yes | frames sampled since the last reset (play mode and the player; 0 in edit-mode hosts) |
| `frameMs` | `object` | yes | {median, p95, max} of the frame times since the last reset |
| `motion` | `object` |  | with motion: {timed (bool), frames, stages {\<phase>: {medianMs, p95Ms}} (per frame, summed over the characters), dynamicBone {chains, particles, medianMs, p95Ms} (the bust and dynamic-bone stages)} |
| `memory` | `object` | yes | {managedBytes, unityAllocatedBytes, graphicsDriverBytes (-1 when the player is not a development build), monoHeapBytes} |
| `unloadUnused` | `object` | yes | {calls, started, medianMs, maxMs}: Resources.UnloadUnusedAssets calls the catalog made or counted (calls: ended; started: counted at their start) |
| `objects` | `object` |  | with objects: {gameObjects, materials, meshes, textures, renderTextures, census? {gameObjects {name: count}, materials {name: count}} (with census; a GameObject outside every scene is named with " [asset]")} |

Example: `{"cmd": "app.perf", "motion": true, "reset": true}`

<a id="cmd-app-quit"></a>

### `app.quit`

Ends the app after the reply (only with bridge.allowQuit).

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `code` | `int` |  | `0` | exit code |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `quitting` | `bool` | yes | true |
| `exitCode` | `int` | yes | exit code (`code` is the envelope's error code member) |

<a id="cmd-app-settings"></a>

### `app.settings`

The settings file's keys: value, source (file, arg, default) and whether a restart applies them; unknown keys with the nearest known key; set writes keys keeping the file's other members.

- flags `mutates`, `writesFiles`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `op` | `enum(get\|set)` |  | `"get"` | get (default) or set |
| `values` | `object` |  |  | set: {key: value} (null removes a key); every value is checked first |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `file` | `path` | yes |  |
| `values` | `object` | yes | {key: {value, source file \| arg \| default, restart}} |
| `unknownKeys` | `array<object>` | yes | {key, suggestion?} |
| `written` | `array<string>` |  | set: the keys whose value changed |

<a id="cmd-app-setup"></a>

### `app.setup`

The first-run guide: its state, the HS2 folder's check and suggestions, and its steps (set, migrate, finish, skip) for automation.

- flags `mutates`, `writesFiles`, `defers`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `op` | `enum(state\|set\|migrate\|finish\|skip)` |  | `"state"` | state (default): read; set: check and keep hs2Root / modsFolders / cacheDir (nothing written); migrate: copy an earlier UserData folder; finish: write the settings (while the guide runs, the index starts); skip: skip the guide |
| `hs2Root` | `path` |  |  | set: the HS2 folder (an abdata folder or the exe stands for its folder) |
| `modsFolders` | `array<path>` |  |  | set: the extra mods folders (each must be readable) |
| `cacheDir` | `path` |  |  | set: the cache folder ("" = the default) |
| `source` | `path` |  |  | migrate: the earlier UserData folder (default: the one offered) |
| `suggestions` | `bool` |  | `true` | state: gather the suggestions (up to 3 s) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `needed` | `bool` | yes | the setup stage needed the guide in this start |
| `page` | `string` | yes | the guide's page while it runs, else null |
| `hs2` | `object` | yes | {path, level ok \| warning \| error, code, message, zipmods, didYouMean} |
| `suggestions` | `array<object>` | yes | {path, from settings \| registry \| scan} |
| `mods` | `array<object>` | yes | {path, readable, zipmods} |
| `cache` | `object` | yes | {path, default, freeGB} |
| `migration` | `object` |  | {offered, source, files, bytes} or the copy's result {copied, same, conflicts, refused, bytes} |
| `written` | `array<string>` |  | finish: the keys written |

## Group: bake

<a id="cmd-bake-delete"></a>

### `bake.delete` · *experimental*

Hides a bake from the store's list, or with restore shows it again; no file is touched. No history.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `no` | `int` |  |  | a store row's number |
| `bake` | `string` |  |  | a bake's identity (32 hex digits), in place of no |
| `restore` | `bool` |  | `false` | show a deleted bake again |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `deleted` | `bool` | yes | the row is deleted now |

Example: `{"cmd": "bake.delete", "no": 73100074}`

<a id="cmd-bake-import"></a>

### `bake.import` · *experimental*

Imports the old bakes of a folder, read in place and never written, as new bakes in the store under their old numbers; a bake whose number the store holds is skipped, so a second run adds nothing. Needs a graphics device. No history.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | `path` |  |  | the folder of the old bakes, absolute or under the HS2 install's UserData; absent: UserData/CharaToItem there |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `imported` | `array<object>` | yes | {no, name, renderers, textures, findings}: findings as {severity, code, path, message} |
| `skipped` | `array<object>` | yes | {no, file, code}: the old bakes not imported and why |

Example: `{"cmd": "bake.import"}`

<a id="cmd-bake-keep"></a>

### `bake.keep` · *experimental*

Keeps a bake item's bake in the store, as a new row named as the item; a bake the store has already: nothing new. No history.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | an item drawn from a bake |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `kept` | `bool` | yes | a new row was made |
| `no` | `int` | yes | the row |

Example: `{"cmd": "bake.keep", "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-bake-list"></a>

### `bake.list` · *experimental*

The store's bakes.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `q` | `string` |  | `""` | text the name or the number holds (ignoring case); empty: every row |
| `deleted` | `bool` |  | `false` | also the deleted rows |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `rows` | `array<object>` | yes | {no, bake, name, created, kind, parts, fk, bytes, deleted}: created in UTC, parts the parts' keys or empty, fk how many FK bones |
| `lastNumber` | `int` | yes | the last number given; numbers are never given again |

Example: `{"cmd": "bake.list", "q": "shoes"}`

<a id="cmd-bake-make"></a>

### `bake.make` · *experimental*

Bakes characters as drawn into items with FK, in one history entry: the whole character, the chosen parts as one item, or one item per part. Each bake goes into the scene and, with store, into the store of bakes; the characters are hidden, not deleted. Needs a graphics device.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters, in place of key |
| `parts` | `array<string>` |  | `[]` | part keys of bake.parts; empty: the whole character. A part a character lacks is skipped |
| `split` | `bool` |  | `false` | one item per part |
| `name` | `string` |  | `""` | the name; empty: the character, the parts and the time. One item per part adds the part, several characters add the character |
| `fk` | `array<string>` |  | `["Hair", "Neck", "Breast", "Body", "RightHand", "LeftHand", "Skirt"]` | the FK groups whose bones the item takes: Hair, Neck, Breast, Body, RightHand, LeftHand, Skirt (ignoring case) |
| `insides` | `bool` |  | `true` | a part without the body or the head gets inside faces |
| `centre` | `bool` |  | `true` | a part bake's origin at the centre of its surface |
| `hide` | `bool` |  | `true` | hide the characters |
| `store` | `bool` |  | `true` | also keep the bakes in the store; false: in the scene only |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `items` | `array<string>` | yes | the new items' keys |
| `bakes` | `array<object>` | yes | {bake, no, main, bytes}: no the store row or null, main the main blob's resource, bytes of every blob |
| `ms` | `float` | yes | milliseconds the bake took |

Example: `{"cmd": "bake.make", "key": "<id>", "parts": ["clothes0", "clothes7"], "split": true}`

<a id="cmd-bake-parts"></a>

### `bake.parts` · *experimental*

The parts a character has that a bake can take: body, head, hair0-hair3, clothes0-clothes7, acc\<slot>, other.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `parts` | `array<object>` | yes | {part, label, item, renderers}: item the name of the part's object, null for other; renderers how many would be baked |

Example: `{"cmd": "bake.parts", "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-bake-purge"></a>

### `bake.purge` · *experimental*

Removes the deleted bakes from the store and every stored blob no remaining bake lists; what a scene holds is never touched. Refused while a bake is being written or imported. No history.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `rows` | `int` | yes | rows removed |
| `blobs` | `int` | yes | blob files removed |
| `bytes` | `int` | yes | their bytes |

Example: `{"cmd": "bake.purge"}`

<a id="cmd-bake-rename"></a>

### `bake.rename` · *experimental*

Renames a bake in the store; the scene's items of that bake that still carry the old name take the new one in one history entry. Undo renames the items back; the store keeps the new name.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `no` | `int` |  |  | a store row's number |
| `bake` | `string` |  |  | a bake's identity (32 hex digits), in place of no |
| `name` | `string` | yes |  | the new name |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `string` | yes | the new name |
| `items` | `array<string>` | yes | the items renamed |

Example: `{"cmd": "bake.rename", "name": "Shoes", "no": 73100074}`

<a id="cmd-bake-status"></a>

### `bake.status` · *experimental*

What an item drawn from a bake holds.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | an item drawn from a bake |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bake` | `string` | yes | the bake's identity |
| `main` | `string` | yes | the main blob's resource |
| `data` | `array<string>` | yes | the mesh and texture blobs' resources |
| `bytes` | `int` | yes | bytes of those blobs in the scene |
| `nodes` | `int` |  | nodes; null when the main cannot be read |
| `renderers` | `int` |  | renderers |
| `meshes` | `int` |  | meshes |
| `textures` | `int` |  | textures |
| `materials` | `int` |  | materials |
| `fk` | `array<string>` | yes | the item's FK bones |
| `correction` | `array<bool>` | yes | the eight joint-correction categories, or null |
| `members` | `array<string>` | yes | the renderers drawn with the mosaic, by path under the item |
| `parts` | `array<string>` | yes | the parts baked, or null for everything drawn |
| `kind` | `string` | yes | chara, parts, item, piece or imported |
| `source` | `object` | yes | {kind, name, ...}: what was baked |
| `no` | `int` | yes | the store row it was added from, or null |
| `piece` | `object` | yes | {steps, source}: for a piece the steps of its cut and its source bake; null otherwise |

Example: `{"cmd": "bake.status", "key": "<id>"}`

## Group: bridge

<a id="cmd-batch"></a>

### `batch`

Runs commands in order as one queue entry (one model transaction in Core).

- flags none; owner `bridge`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `cmds` | `array<object>` | yes |  | the member requests {cmd, ..., as?} |
| `stopOnError` | `bool` |  | `true` | stop at the first failed member |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `results` | `array<object>` | yes | member replies in order |
| `failedAt` | `int` |  | index of the first failed member |

results[] holds each member's own reply ({ok, cmd, ...}). On failure ok is false and failedAt is the index of the first failed member (error / code repeat its error). Members run within one frame unless one defers (capture, waits, loads); the next member then runs at the next pump. A member's "as": "name" lets later members use "$name" or "$name.path.0.key" in any argument value (only names defined earlier in the same batch are replaced). Members inherit strict and undo from the batch; the batch's timeout covers all members (default: the sum of the members' defaults, at most 600 s).

Example: `{"cmd": "batch", "cmds": [{"as": "p", "cmd": "ping"}, {"cmd": "help", "name": "$p.cmd"}]}`

<a id="cmd-bridge-version"></a>

### `bridge.version`

Versions of the protocol, bridge, Api, app and Unity; command and plugin counts.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `bridge`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `protocol` | `int` | yes |  |
| `bridge` | `string` | yes |  |
| `api` | `string` | yes |  |
| `app` | `string` | yes | app version |
| `appName` | `string` | yes |  |
| `unity` | `string` | yes |  |
| `commands` | `int` | yes |  |
| `plugins` | `array<object>` | yes | {guid, version, state} |
| `allowQuit` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `tokenRequired` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `build` | `object` |  | {kind (editor \| dev \| distribution), version, commit, dirty, builtUtc}: the build this app runs |

<a id="cmd-help"></a>

### `help`

Self-description: every command, or one command with examples.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `bridge`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `string` |  |  | one command (name or alias): adds examples, reply notes and timeouts |
| `group` | `string` |  |  | only the commands of this group |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `protocol` | `int` | yes |  |
| `commands` | `array<object>` |  | {name, group, summary, args[{name, type, required, default, doc}], returns[], flags[], since, owner} |
| `command` | `object` |  | with name: the one command |

Example: `{"cmd": "help"}`

Example: `{"cmd": "help", "name": "batch"}`

<a id="cmd-ping"></a>

### `ping`

Liveness check; also the app's readiness stages, frame, clock and screen size (an empty body is a ping).

- flags `readOnly`; owner `bridge`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `protocol` | `int` | yes | envelope version |
| `app` | `string` | yes | "HS2Next" (HS2's Studio Bridge has no such field) |
| `bridge` | `string` | yes | bridge version |
| `api` | `string` | yes | HS2Next.Api version |
| `ready` | `object` | yes | readiness stages {stage: bool} |
| `appReady` | `bool` | yes | every required stage is ready |
| `readyDetail` | `object` |  | progress texts of stages that have one {stage: text} |
| `studio` | `bool` | yes | = appReady (HS2 Studio Bridge member) |
| `version` | `int` | yes | 1 (HS2 Studio Bridge member) |
| `frame` | `int` | yes | frame of the last pump |
| `realtime` | `float` | yes | seconds since startup |
| `screen` | `string` | yes | "\<width>x\<height>" |
| `queued` | `int` | yes | requests waiting or running |

### `camera.get` · *HS2 parity*

The view camera in HS2's shape (pivot, rotation, distance, fov) plus its world position and forward. While a runtime override places the view, target, rot, dist and fov are the shown view's and overridden names its owner.

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |

| `camera` | `object` | yes | {target, rot, dist, fov, worldPos, forward, current, activeCamera, overridden} |

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |

| `target` | `vec3` |  |  | pivot (world) |
| `rot` | `euler` |  |  | rotation (Euler degrees; positive pitch looks down) |
| `dist` | `float` |  |  | distance from the pivot (its absolute value) |
| `fov` | `float` |  |  | vertical field of view (degrees) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |

| `camera` | `object` | yes | {target, rot, dist, fov, worldPos, forward, current, activeCamera, overridden} |

HS2's full camera dump; with set, moves the view first (in the same frame).

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `set` | `object` |  |  | {pos: vec3 world, rot: [x,y,z,w] quaternion or [x,y,z] Euler, dist (default the current distance, 10 when \< 1e-3), fov} |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `note` | `string` |  | with set |
| `requested` | `object` |  | with set: {position, rotation, target, dist} |
| `camera` | `object` | yes | {name, position, rotation, euler, forward, up, fov, aspect, near, far, orthographic, pixelWidth, pixelHeight, screen, clearFlags, background, cullingMask, allowHDR, allowMSAA, renderingPath, frame, studio, current, activeCamera, overridden} |

<a id="cmd-capture"></a>

### `capture` · *HS2 parity*

Renders the view into \<outputDir>/shots/\<name>.png at the late point of frame F + waitFrames (default 0: this frame, after its motion steps).

- flags `writesFiles`, `defers`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s; handles `waitFrames` itself

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `string` |  |  | file name without extension (default the local time yyyyMMdd_HHmmss_fff; invalid characters become _) |
| `width` | `int` |  | `1280` | 16-8192 |
| `height` | `int` |  | `720` | 16-8192 |
| `aa` | `enum(smaa\|msaa4\|none)` |  | `"smaa"` | anti-aliasing (SMAA High; msaa4 = 4x MSAA without SMAA) |
| `supersample` | `int` |  | `1` | 1, 2 or 4: render at k x the size, reduced by a linear-light box filter |
| `frame` | `bool` |  | `false` | draw the scene's frame image (the background is part of the scene and always drawn) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | `string` | yes | absolute PNG path |
| `frame` | `int` | yes | frame rendered |
| `width` | `int` | yes |  |
| `height` | `int` | yes |  |
| `ms` | `float` | yes | render + encode + write time |
| `renderWidth` | `int` | yes | width x supersample |
| `renderHeight` | `int` | yes | height x supersample |

<a id="cmd-screenshot"></a>

### `screenshot`

Takes a screenshot as F11 does into \<UserData>/cap (vanilla name yyyyMMddHHmmssfff.png); the reply comes after the file is written.

- flags `writesFiles`, `defers`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 180 s; handles `waitFrames` itself

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `string` |  |  | file name without extension (default the local time yyyyMMddHHmmssfff; invalid characters become _; _1, _2 … on a collision) |
| `mode` | `enum(fit\|viewport\|custom)` |  |  | fit (the viewport's aspect inside the window), viewport, custom (default the setting capture.mode) |
| `scale` | `float` |  |  | 1-4 for fit / viewport (default the setting) |
| `width` | `int` |  |  | custom: 16-8192 |
| `height` | `int` |  |  | custom: 16-8192 |
| `supersample` | `int` |  |  | 1, 2 or 4 (default the setting) |
| `frame` | `bool` |  |  | draw the frame image (default the setting, true) |
| `background` | `string` |  |  | reserved: "transparent" answers unsupported (R15) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | `string` | yes | absolute PNG path under \<UserData>/cap |
| `width` | `int` | yes |  |
| `height` | `int` | yes |  |
| `renderWidth` | `int` | yes |  |
| `renderHeight` | `int` | yes |  |
| `megapixels` | `float` | yes | of the render |
| `ms` | `float` | yes | from the request to the written file |
| `imagesPending` | `bool` | yes | the scene images were still loading (after 600 frames) |
| `timing` | `object` | yes | {mainThreadMs, renderMs, readbackMs, reduceMs, encodeMs, writeMs} |
| `memory` | `object` | yes | {gfxBefore, gfxAfterRender} Unity's Gfx Used Memory in bytes (-1 = not available) |

<a id="cmd-chara-mosaic"></a>

### `chara.mosaic`

The mosaic of a built character (read only): its mosaic-set members with their mode, source and the last camera's block, the MaterialEditor items kept but not applied, and the wide mosaic option.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |

| `protected` | `array<object>` | yes | [{kind, object, target, property}]: MaterialEditor items kept in the documents but not applied to members (finding me.mosaicProtected) |
| `wide` | `bool` | yes | the wide screen-space mosaic is on (option render.mosaicWide) |
| `findings` | `array<object>` | yes | the mosaic stage's findings (mosaic.patchesRestored, mosaic.transferUnavailable) and me.mosaicProtected per protected item |

<a id="cmd-chara-save"></a>

### `chara.save`

Saves a character node as a .hs2nchara file under \<UserData>/chara.

- flags `writesFiles`, `slow`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 120 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `name` | `path` |  |  | file name under \<UserData>/chara (.hs2nchara appended); one of name / path |
| `path` | `path` |  |  | relative path under \<UserData>/chara (subfolders created); one of name / path |
| `overwrite` | `bool` |  | `false` | replace an existing file |
| `thumb` | `enum(render\|view\|placeholder)` |  | `"render"` | render: the front shot (the Studio default pose and HS2's coordinate camera relative to the node, in an isolation layer); view: the display camera's position, rotation and field of view with the character as shown, isolated (front with warning thumbnail.noView without a display camera); both need a built character and a graphics device, else the placeholder |
| `clock` | `string` |  |  | ISO-8601 UTC time for createdUtc / modifiedUtc (byte-identical test saves; default now) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | `path` | yes |  |
| `bytes` | `int` | yes |  |
| `thumbnail` | `enum(render\|placeholder)` | yes |  |
| `framing` | `string` | yes | the framing rendered: "front", "view" or null (placeholder) |

<a id="cmd-chara-speed"></a>

### `chara.speed` · *HS2 parity*

Animation speed and forced loop of a character (one history entry).

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `speed` | `float` |  |  | chara.anime.speed (≥ 0; absent: kept) |
| `loop` | `bool` |  |  | chara.anime.forceLoop (absent: kept) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `speed` | `float` | yes |  |
| `loop` | `bool` | yes |  |

<a id="cmd-chara-yure"></a>

### `chara.yure` · *HS2 parity*

The sway table of the H row a character plays, in the members of HS2's private bridge: lines kept for its motion id, the matched line, the breast and hip enables, the bust shape flags and masks, the chains.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `isInit` | `bool` | yes | a table is loaded |
| `lineCount` | `int` | yes | lines kept for the row's motion id (and owner) |
| `matchedLine` | `int` | yes | index of the line applied (-1 = none: everything enabled) |
| `active` | `array<bool>` | yes | [breast L, breast R, hip L, hip R] enables |
| `shapeL` | `object` | yes | {breast [7 bools: body shape ids 2..8], nip (id 32)}: true = enabled (left) |
| `shapeR` | `object` | yes | the same for the right side |
| `disableBustShapeMask` | `array<any>` | yes | [left, right] x 8 bools (ids 2..8, 32): true = masked |

<a id="cmd-coord-save"></a>

### `coord.save` · *HS2 parity*

Saves the coordinate a character wears as a .hs2ncoord file under \<UserData>/coordinate/\<female|male> (the character's sex): the coordinate with its plugin data and the resources it references; the coordinate name written is coordName (in the file only: the character keeps its documents); the thumbnail is the Maker's coordinate shot (fresh documents of the sex wearing it, the Maker pose list's row 0, hair hidden, isolated) or the placeholder. Not recorded.

- flags `writesFiles`, `slow`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 120 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `name` | `path` |  |  | file name under \<UserData>/coordinate/\<female\|male> (.hs2ncoord appended); one of name / path |
| `path` | `path` |  |  | relative path under \<UserData>/coordinate/\<female\|male> (subfolders created); one of name / path |
| `coordName` | `string` |  |  | the coordinate name written into the file (coord.name; default: the character's) |
| `overwrite` | `bool` |  | `false` | replace an existing file |
| `thumb` | `enum(render\|placeholder)` |  | `"render"` | render: the coordinate thumbnail (needs a graphics device, else the placeholder with a warning); placeholder |
| `clock` | `string` |  |  | ISO-8601 UTC time for createdUtc / modifiedUtc (byte-identical test saves; default now) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | `path` | yes | absolute path |
| `bytes` | `int` | yes |  |
| `thumbnail` | `enum(render\|placeholder)` | yes | the thumbnail written |
| `sex` | `int` | yes | the coordinate's sex (0 male, 1 female: the folder) |
| `coordName` | `string` | yes | the coordinate name written |

Example: `{"cmd": "coord.save", "coordName": "Summer", "key": "<id>", "name": "my coordinate"}`

The look-at target of a built character; pos moves it (stored local to its parent, one history entry).

| `position` | `vec3` | yes |  |
| `local` | `vec3` | yes |  |
| `parent` | `string` | yes |  |
| `active` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `eyesLookPtn` | `int` | yes |  |

## Group: chara edit

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-accessories"></a>

### `chara.edit.accessories`

The accessories of a built character (read-only, numbers only; AccessoryReport and the slot objects), per build slot (0-based; the Accessories page shows slot + 1) in the member names, meaning and order of the HS2 Studio Bridge's chara.accessoryInfo, then HS2Next's: the stored part (type, id = the item ref, parentKey in effect, addMove, colorInfo, …), the scene visibility (show), the slot object (loaded, activity), the row and its columns, the realized prefab's CmpAccessory facts and raw defaults, the anchor (the slot object's parent), the object's pose, the two transform sets, every material slot under the object as written, the dynamic-bone chains; then the accessory id, the stored parent, the preferred slot, the plugin rules, the defaults «初期カラー» writes, the served copy. The top level adds the hair parts' activity.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `slot` | `int` |  |  | one build slot (0-based; a slot without an accessory reports type 350 and loaded false) |
| `accessory` | `string` |  |  | one accessory by id (not with slot) |
| `served` | `bool` |  | `true` | also name the copy the catalog serves each prefab from (served) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `slots` | `array<object>` | yes | per slot holding an accessory (ascending; or the one asked for): {slot, extra (≥ 20), type (the category, 350 = none), id (the item ref "\<guid>#\<id>" or null), rawId (its local id), parentKey (the key in effect: the stored parent, else the row's Parent column when it is one of the 54 attach points, else ""), addMove [2 × {position, rotation, scale}] (stored), colorInfo [4 × {color, glossPower, metallicPower, smoothnessPower}] (stored), hideCategory, hideTiming, noShake, partsOfHead (the key in effect is a head attach point), show (the scene's visibility state.accessories), loaded, object, active, activeInHierarchy, row {category, id, ref, Name, MainManifest, MainAB, MainData, Parent, TexManifest, TexAB, TexD, TexC} or null, facts {hasCmpAccessory, parentColumn, headWeighted, fixed; with an object: cmpComponents, cmpOnRoot, cmpUsed, particleSystems, dynamicBones, colliders; with a CmpAccessory: typeHair, useColor [3], useGloss [4], useMetallic [4], alphaRenderers, alphaLength, normalRenderers, normalLength, move1, move2, moveNames [2]}, particleDummy, defaults [4 × {color, gloss, metallic}] (the prefab's raw values), anchor {name, path} (the slot object's parent), world {position, rotation, lossyScale, localPosition, localRotation}, moves [2 × null \| {name, position, rotation (local Euler), localRotation, scale, worldPosition}], shaderType (the character's hair.shaderType), materials [{renderer, kind (normal / alpha / normal+alpha / other), slot, slots, enabled, active, activeInHierarchy, bounds {center, size}, material, shader, queue, mainTex, props {_Color, _Color2, _Color3, _TopColor, _UnderColor, _Specular, _Glossiness…4, _Metallic…4, _Smoothness: those the shader declares}}] (every renderer under the object), chains [{object, root, colliders, enabled, active, noShake, particles}]; then accessory (the id; null for a card build), category, item, parent (stored; "" = the row default), importSlot (the preferred slot), notLoaded (≥ 20 with MoreAccessories off), showFlag (the read MoreAccessories show), anchorUsed (the attach point the loader used, "head bones" or "none"), hairShaderType (the hair shader type applied to a hair-type slot, -1 otherwise), colourDefaults [4 × {color, gloss, metallic, smoothness}] (the document values «初期カラー» writes) or null, rules (Additional Accessory Controls' data as saved) or null, rowRef, served {MainData {kind, guid}} (served)} |
| `slotCount` | `int` | yes | the realized slots (20 + the loaded MoreAccessories slots) |
| `shaderType` | `int` | yes | hair.shaderType (0 hair_dithering, 1 hair_cutout) |
| `hairActive` | `array<any>` | yes | the four hair part objects' own activity (null: none) |
| `hairChildren` | `array<any>` | yes | per hair part [active children, all children] (Additional Accessory Controls' hair rules switch the children) |
| `male` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `extraSlotsOn` | `bool` | yes | the build loads MoreAccessories slots |
| `freeSlots` | `array<int>` | yes | the free build slots the slot pickers offer (0..19, then up to the highest used + 10); null for a card build |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.accessories", "key": "<id>", "slot": 4}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-accessory"></a>

### `chara.edit.accessory`

Adds, removes, reorders, moves to a build slot or copies accessories of a character (one undo entry). Slots are the 0-based build slots of the slot table (the Accessories page shows slot + 1): add / copy may name the target slot, which becomes the new entry's preferred slot (importSlot); moveSlot changes it; a slot ≥ 20 while MoreAccessories is off is kept as data (warning acc.slotNotLoaded).

- flags `mutates`, `slow`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 120 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `op` | `enum(add\|remove\|move\|moveSlot\|copy)` | yes |  | the operation (move = reorder the list; moveSlot = move to a free build slot) |
| `category` | `int` |  |  | add: the accessory category (351–363) |
| `ref` | `string` |  |  | add: the item row "\<guid>#\<id>" |
| `parent` | `string` |  |  | add: the anchor key ("" or one of the 54 attach points; default: the row's Parent column) |
| `slot` | `int` |  |  | add: the free build slot to add to (default: the lowest free slot; an occupied slot is bad_args); moveSlot: the free build slot to move to |
| `index` | `int` |  |  | add (deprecated; not with slot): list index without a preferred slot; move: the new list index |
| `accessory` | `string` |  |  | remove / move / moveSlot: the accessory id |
| `from` | `string` |  |  | copy: the source accessory id |
| `to` | `string` |  |  | copy: the target accessory id (overwrite its members; not with toSlot) |
| `toSlot` | `int` |  |  | copy: the target build slot (free: a new accessory there; occupied: that accessory's members overwritten, its id kept); neither to nor toSlot = «複製», the lowest free slot |
| `parts` | `array<string>` |  |  | copy: "slot", "move1", "move2" (default ["slot"]; the sets need an occupied target) |
| `mirrorParent` | `bool` |  | `false` | copy: mirror the parent («左右の親を変更»: HS2's 18 left / right attach point pairs, other values kept) |
| `mirror` | `enum(none\|leftRight\|upDown)` |  | `"none"` | copy: mirror the copied move rotation (y or x + 180°, wrapped into [0, 360)) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `accessory` | `string` |  | add / copy to a new slot: the new accessory id |
| `slot` | `int` |  | add / copy / move / moveSlot: the build slot of the new, copied-to or moved accessory (-1: none) |
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `consequences` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes |  |

Example: `{"category": 353, "cmd": "chara.edit.accessory", "key": "<id>", "op": "add", "ref": "illusion.hs2#0", "slot": 4}`

### `chara.edit.accessoryDefaults`

«初期カラー» of the Maker's accessory page: the used colour areas and the alpha colour of a non-hair accessory take the worn prefab's defaults (colour, gloss, metallic; unused areas kept). One undo entry "Edit accessory"; unsupported for a hair-type prefab or without the prefab's facts.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `accessory` | `string` |  |  | the accessory id (or slot) |
| `slot` | `int` |  |  | the accessory's build slot (0-based; instead of accessory) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `consequences` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes |  |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.accessoryDefaults", "key": "<id>", "slot": 4}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-accessoryhaircolour"></a>

### `chara.edit.accessoryHairColour`

«髪の毛の色を取得» of the Maker's accessory page: a hair-type accessory's colours 1–4 take a hair part's base / top / under / specular colours and its colour 1's smoothness / metallic the part's (the stored hair values). One undo entry "Edit accessory"; unsupported for a prefab that is not hair-type.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `accessory` | `string` |  |  | the accessory id (or slot) |
| `slot` | `int` |  |  | the accessory's build slot (0-based; instead of accessory) |
| `part` | `any` | yes |  | the hair part: "back", "front", "side", "option" or 0..3 («後ろ髪», «前髪», «横髪», «エクステ») |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `consequences` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes |  |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.accessoryHairColour", "key": "<id>", "part": "front", "slot": 5}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-bust"></a>

### `chara.edit.bust`

The bust of a built character (read-only, numbers only): the stored size / softness / weight, the breast chains' values as the bust stage holds them (the HS2 Studio Bridge's chara.bustPhysics: pattern-0 damping / elasticity / stiffness, inert per particle, gravity) and the breast normals (chara.meshNormals: source, entries, rate, SHA-256 of the drawn body mesh's normals).

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sex` | `int` | yes | 0 male, 1 female (HS2's numbers) |
| `size` | `float` | yes | body.shape[1] as stored |
| `softness` | `float` | yes | body.bust.softness as stored |
| `weight` | `float` | yes | body.bust.weight as stored |
| `physics` | `object` | yes | {enabled (the bust stage runs; males: and BustOptions.MaleBustPhysics), stageEnabled, maleBustPhysics (males), softness, weight, size (the values the stage applied), chains [{name (Mune_L / Mune_R), kind (0 / 1), pattern, active, particles, damping, elasticity, stiffness (particle 0; the stage writes pattern 0 alike), inert [per particle], gravity [3]}]} (the breast chains, left then right) |
| `normals` | `object` | yes | {source {kind (vanilla / replaced: a body mesh a build hook replaced), hook (its id or null), guid (the id it gives the body or null), asset, bundle} or null (males), missing, missingReason, entries [{object, vertices, min, max, applicable, applied}], rate (HS2's rate of the stored size: 1 for size ≥ 0.5, else InverseLerp(0, 0.5, size); null for males), blendRate (the rate last written; null: none), applied, meshCopied, sha256 (of the drawn o_body_cf / o_body_cm normals as little-endian float32 x, y, z per vertex, lower-case hex), mesh (name/vertices), blends, copies, lastBlendMs, lastCopyMs, copyBytes} |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.bust", "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-clothes"></a>

### `chara.edit.clothes`

The clothes of a built character (read-only, numbers only; ClothesReport), in the member names and order of the HS2 Studio Bridge's chara.clothesInfo, then HS2Next's: per kind the row and its columns, the realized prefab's facts (colour sets, main-colour alpha, break, option parts, def / half objects, colour groups), its default colour sets as document values, the compositing passes with their create-material properties, every material slot of the part as written, the objects' activity and the copy the catalog served the prefab from; the masks' flags, values and reference renderers.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `kind` | `any` |  |  | one clothes kind ("top", "bottom", "innerTop", "innerBottom", "gloves", "pantyhose", "socks", "shoes" or 0..7; default all eight) |
| `served` | `bool` |  | `true` | also name the copy the catalog serves each prefab from (served) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `kinds` | `array<object>` | yes | {kind (top … shoes), index (0..7), category, item, realized, object, active, activeInHierarchy, skipped (a one-piece top / inner top skipped the part), state (the stored clothes state), stateKinds (the states the kind offers), row {id, Name, MainAB, MainData, MainData02, Coordinate, NotBra, StateType, BreakDisableMask, KokanHide, MainTex*, ColorMask*Tex, Over*Mask, then the Over*MaskAB columns} or null, facts {hasCmpClothes, useColorN [3], useColorA [3], useBreak, objOpt01, objOpt02 (live option objects), objTopDef, objTopHalf, objBotDef, objBotHalf (present), uvScalePattern [4], defMainColor04 [4], defGloss04, defMetallic04, defLayout [3 × [4]], defRotation [3] (the prefab's raw values), groups [{renderers, length, slots, names, mainTex [w, h] or null, hasCreators}]; a kind without CmpClothes: {hasCmpClothes: false} only}, creators [{group (0..2), creator (colour / detail), material, mainTex, rt [w, h], props (the create material's properties as chara.edit.materials), name (the HS2Next name), rebuilds, draw}], defaults [3 × {base, pattern, patternColor, layout, rotation, gloss, metallic}] (the prefab's sets as document values: «初期カラー») or null, objects {topDef, topHalf, botDef, botHalf (activity, null = absent), opt1 [], opt2 []}, materials [{renderer, group (1..3; 0 = no colour group), slot, slots, enabled, active, material, shader, queue, keywords, props {_AlphaEx, _Cutoff, _alpha_a.._alpha_d, _Color, _Glossiness, _Metallic, _MainTex, _DetailMainTex: textures {name, w, h, format, creator ("colour j" / "detail j" when it is the group's creator RT)}}, mainTex, detailMainTex, alphaEx}] (every slot of every renderer under the part object), then rowRef ("category:key" the part unit resolved), prefab, onePiece, served {MainData {kind (zipmod / abdata / none), guid}} (served)} |
| `flags` | `object` | yes | {notBot, notInnerT, notInnerB (the one-piece couplings), underMaskReflectionType (where the lower masks come from: 0 top, 1 bottom, -1 none), underMaskBreakDisable (that row's BreakDisableMask), lowerOk0, lowerOk1} |
| `masks` | `object` | yes | {body, bra, innerTB, innerB, panst: {material, _alpha_a, _alpha_b, _alpha_c, _alpha_d, _AlphaMask, _AlphaMask2 (texture names)} or null (no reference renderer)} |
| `maskRenderers` | `object` | yes | {bra, innerTB, innerB, panst}: the reference renderers of the masks (null = none) |
| `subMenu` | `object` | yes | {bottom, innerBottom}: the kind can be edited (false while a one-piece top / inner top is worn) |
| `male` | `bool` | yes |  |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.clothes", "key": "<id>", "kind": "top"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-coordinate"></a>

### `chara.edit.coordinate`

Loads a coordinate onto characters as the Maker's «衣装カード» buttons: clothes (the eight clothes parts, the accessories kept), accessories (the accessory list and its rules, the clothes kept) or all (the whole coordinate with its plugin data); the coordinate name follows the file in every mode; per-part changes (no rebuild), the accessory visibility map filled and pruned. An HS2 coordinate card is imported first (in memory); characters of the other sex are refused (several keys: skipped with warning edit.coordinateSkipped); the clothes / accessories modes add warning coord.extKept; the reader's findings are warnings. One undo entry "Load coordinate".

- flags `mutates`, `slow`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 300 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters (one entry) |
| `file` | `path` | yes |  | an HS2 coordinate card (.png; a relative path under \<hs2Root>/UserData/coordinate/\<sex of the first key>, .png appended when missing) or a .hs2ncoord file (relative: under \<UserData>/coordinate); absolute paths of either |
| `mode` | `enum(clothes\|accessories\|all)` |  | `"all"` | what is loaded |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `consequences` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `source` | `object` | yes | {file (absolute), sex (0 / 1), name, mode}: the coordinate read |
| `imported` | `bool` | yes | the file was an HS2 coordinate card (imported in memory) |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.coordinate", "file": "coordinate.png", "key": "<id>", "mode": "clothes"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-digest"></a>

### `chara.edit.digest`

SHA-256 per section of the canonical text of a character's realized face / body / hair / clothes / accessories (read-only): drawn materials, creators (create materials and input rows), head-bone or body shape-bone local transforms (the destinations of the body shape map; never the eye-look bones) or the hair bundles' correction transforms, with a graphics device the creators' RT read-backs, for the body the mesh normals and the bust stage, for the hair its dynamic-bone chains, for the clothes their def / half / option objects, for the accessories their slot objects (activity, parent, the two transform sets). The same text in the Editor and the player.

- flags `readOnly`, `slow`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 120 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `parts` | `enum(face\|body\|hair\|clothes\|accessories\|all)` |  | `"face"` | which part (hair: the four hair parts; no creators, so creators / rts hash empty texts; clothes: the eight clothes kinds, no bones, so bones hashes an empty text; accessories: every slot object, no creators and no bones; all: every part) |
| `text` | `bool` |  | `false` | also return the canonical texts (texts {materials, creators, bones, normals?, bust?, chains?, objects?}) for diagnosis |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `parts` | `string` | yes |  |
| `materials` | `string` | yes |  |
| `creators` | `string` | yes |  |
| `bones` | `string` | yes |  |
| `rts` | `string` |  | with a graphics device only |
| `normals` | `string` |  | parts body / all: the body part's mesh normals (per renderer: mesh name / vertex count and the SHA-256 of its normals as float32 bits) |
| `bust` | `string` |  | parts body / all: the bust stage (card values; per chain pattern, activity, gravity and per particle damping / elasticity / stiffness / inert as float bits) |
| `objects` | `string` |  | parts clothes / accessories / all: per clothes kind the part object's activity and its def / half / option objects' activity; per accessory slot the slot object's activity, its parent path and the local transforms of trfMove01 / trfMove02 |
| `chains` | `string` |  | parts hair / all: the hair chains of the dynamic-bone stage (part, bundle, root, owner, active, noShake, particles and the captured rest local pose of their transforms as float bits) |
| `texts` | `object` |  | with text |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.digest", "key": "<id>", "parts": "face"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-get"></a>

### `chara.edit.get`

Values of a character's documents under `appearance.` in the save-format JSON forms (default: the whole chara.json and coord.json).

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character (imported) |
| `paths` | `array<string>` |  |  | paths relative to `appearance.` ("chara.face.head", "coord.clothes.top.colors[0]"; "chara" / "coord" = a whole document; "import" = the stored documents' import provenance, null for a character made in HS2Next); default both documents |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `values` | `object` | yes | {path: value} (JSON values; key = the path as given) |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.get", "key": "<id>", "paths": ["chara.face.shape[3]", "chara.face.head"]}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-hair"></a>

### `chara.edit.hair`

The hair of a built character (read-only, numbers only; HairReport, the reply shape of the HS2 Studio Bridge's chara.hairInfo): per part the row, the realized prefab's CmpHair facts, the stored and effective bundles with their limits, default rates, correction transforms (local / world) and dynamic-bone chains, the hair materials (shader, queue, main texture, the hair properties), the decoration colours per slot and the mesh pattern as written.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `part` | `any` |  |  | one part ("back", "front", "side", "option" or 0..3; default all four) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `parts` | `array<object>` | yes | {part (0..3), item, object (the part object), name, mainAB, mainData, mainData02, ringOff, texAB, texD, texC (the row's columns), facts {hasCmpHair, useTopColor, useUnderColor, useSameSkinColorButton, useMesh (HS2's instance flag: the prefab's or, with HairUnit.MeshOnEveryHair = the HairMeshforAll rule, any live rendHair), useAcsColor01..03, acsDefColor [[4]], rendHair (live renderers), rendHairLength, rendAccessory, bundles (boneInfo count), prefabUseMesh, meshWritten (the unit writes the mesh values; the Maker's mesh gate), rendHairEmpty, acsDefColorCount, shownBundles; a part without CmpHair: {hasCmpHair: false} only}, stored (stored bundle count), bundles [{index, shown, title, trfCorrect, parent, limits {posMin, posMax, rotMin, rotMax}, base {pos, rot}, default {move, rotate}, stored {move, rotate, noShake} or null, effective {move, rotate, noShake}, local {position, euler, rotation} or null, world {position, rotation} or null, chains [{name (owner), root, enabled (the chain's own state: its Studio switch on and not noShake = HS2's DynamicBone.enabled after the FK hair re-application), active (it simulates now; needs physics on), relation, particles, noShake, userEnabled, bundle, rest {position, rotation} (the root particle's captured local pose)}]}], otherChains (hair chains in no bundle), materials [{renderer, slot, material, shader, renderQueue, mainTex, props, keywords}] (every rendHair slot), decoration [{renderer, slot, material, colors [_Color, _Color2, _Color3: [4] or null], shader}] (every rendAccessory slot), otherRenderers (names), partName, row, prefab (the realized prefab), realized, storedValid (the stored list applies), mesh {item, texture, scale [2], offset [2], color (_MeshColor_01 as written), written, format, w, h, onOff (_Mesh_onoff), uniform, storedColor, layout}} |
| `shaderType` | `int` | yes | hair.shaderType (0 hair_dithering, 1 hair_cutout) |
| `meshOnEveryHair` | `bool` | yes | HairUnit.MeshOnEveryHair (the HairMeshforAll rule) |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.hair", "key": "<id>", "part": "front"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-hairbundle"></a>

### `chara.edit.hairBundle`

A hair bundle edit of the Maker («調整»): sets the given members of one bundle (rates stored unclamped) or resets them to the prefab default; the first edit of a part whose stored bundles are not the effective list (none stored, a count other than the prefab's) stores the whole list first (the prefab defaults); with «髪の調整をまとめて行う» (chara.hair.editor.ctrlTogether) each changed move / rotate component is set on every other bundle of the part (resets and noShake never propagate). all + reset = «全て初期化» (every bundle's rates to the defaults, noShake kept). One undo entry "Edit hair".

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters (one entry; a character whose prefab lacks the bundle is skipped with warning edit.bundleSkipped) |
| `part` | `any` | yes |  | the hair part: "back", "front", "side", "option" or 0..3 |
| `index` | `int` |  |  | the bundle (CmpHair.boneInfo index, = the position in the materialized list); not with all |
| `all` | `bool` |  | `false` | with reset: every bundle of the part («全て初期化») |
| `move` | `vec3` |  |  | the move rates [x, y, z] (0..1 between the prefab limits; stored as given) |
| `rotate` | `vec3` |  |  | the rotate rates [x, y, z] |
| `noShake` | `bool` |  |  | «揺らさない» (the bundle's chains inactive; a full chain rebuild) |
| `reset` | `bool` |  | `false` | reset to the prefab default instead of setting: the given members only select what is reset (their values are ignored; none = move and rotate; noShake given → false) |
| `axis` | `int` |  |  | with reset: only this component (0..2) of move / rotate (a slider row's ↺) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | paths that changed (the materialized list as chara.hair.\<part>.bundles) |
| `consequences` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes |  |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.hairBundle", "index": 0, "key": "<id>", "move": [0.5, 0.7, 0.3], "part": "back"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-hairskin"></a>

### `chara.edit.hairSkin`

«肌の色に合わせる» of the Maker: the part's underColor = HSVToRGB(H, max(0, S − 0.06), max(0, V − 0.06)) of body.skin.color (stored alpha kept) and smoothness = max(0, body.skin.gloss − 0.3); that part only, also with «前髪と後ろ髪の色を統一» on (HS2). The prefab's skin-match flag is the page's gate, not checked here. One undo entry "Edit hair".

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters (one entry) |
| `part` | `any` | yes |  | the hair part: "back", "front", "side", "option" or 0..3 |
