| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `consequences` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes |  |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.hairSkin", "key": "<id>", "part": "back"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-info"></a>

### `chara.edit.info`

The editor of a built character: dirty and deferred units, last flush, creators (sizes, bytes, rebuilds), owned materials, decoded layers, part rows, whether a reset reference exists; session opens / closes a bridge edit session (the first one also captures the reset reference).

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `session` | `bool` |  |  | true opens a bridge edit session (kept decoded layers; ends 60 s after the last edit; captures the reference of chara.edit.reset; only for a built character: unavailable, and nothing opened, otherwise), false closes it |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `dirty` | `array<string>` | yes | dirty unit ids |
| `rebuildRequested` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `deferred` | `array<string>` | yes | unit ids a preview flush deferred to the commit flush (anim.refresh, the light dynamic-bone pass, bust) |
| `reference` | `bool` | yes | the character has reset reference documents (its edit session opened in this scene session) |
| `inSession` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `flushes` | `int` | yes | flushes of this editor |
| `lastFlush` | `object` | yes | {units, creators, creatorsRebuilt, ms, errors, build, mode (normal / preview / build), deferred, faceDrawChanged, throttleWeight (the body rebuild's cost weight when the flush started: Σ over the body creators it rebuilt of 1 + their skin layers; 0 for the build), dynBone (the dynamic-bone chain step it ran: none / light / full)} or null |
| `hair` | `object` | yes | the hair: {parts [{part, name, item, prefab, facts (as chara.edit.hair)}] (back, front, side, option), lastFlush {dynBone (none / light / full), deferred (the flush deferred the light chain re-derivation to the commit)} or null, deferred (a light re-derivation is pending now)} |
| `clothes` | `object` | yes | the clothes: {kinds [{kind (top … shoes), index (0..7), item, prefab, realized, skipped, facts (as chara.edit.clothes)}], lastFlush {clothesSwapped [8], objectsReset [8] (kinds whose part unit ran without a swap, e.g. an option toggle, and so re-applied the stored clothes state), creatorsRebuilt [{kind, group (0..2), colour, detail}] (the clothes compositing passes the flush rebuilt)} or null} |
| `throttle` | `object` | yes | the body throttle of drags: {mode (auto / always / off = maker.bodyThrottle), enabled (the master switch), weight (the pending body rebuild's weight now), judged (the weight the throttle last judged on a drag frame), throttled (drag frames this character waited), total (all characters)} |
| `bustNormals` | `object` | yes | the breast normals: {rate (HS2's rate of the stored bust size; null for males), blendRate (the rate last written; null: none), applied, meshCopied} |
| `creators` | `array<object>` | yes | {name (create_skin_face, create_skin detail_face, create_skin_body, create_skin detail_body), width, height, size [w, h], mips, bytes, rebuilds, lastRebuildMs, layers} |
| `creatorBytes` | `int` | yes |  |
| `ownedMaterials` | `int` | yes |  |
| `decodedLayers` | `int` | yes |  |
| `parts` | `object` | yes | {head, skin, hair[4], clothes[8], accessories[{accessory, slot (the build slot), ref, loaded}]} |
| `accessories` | `object` | yes | the accessories: {slots [{slot (0-based build slot), accessory (the id; null for a card build), category, item, loaded, notLoaded (≥ 20 with MoreAccessories off), facts (as chara.edit.accessories)}] (build-slot order), lastFlush {accessorySlotsSwapped [slots whose object the flush replaced, loaded or removed], accessoryHairShaded [hair-type slots whose materials got the hair shader pass]} or null} |

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-item"></a>

### `chara.edit.item`

Changes an item of characters (a `ref` member: head, skin, hair / clothes / accessory item, pattern, mesh …; or an accessory's category, 350 = remove) with HS2's rule consequences (one undo entry "Change item").

- flags `mutates`, `slow`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 120 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters (one entry) |
| `path` | `string` | yes |  | the ref member, relative to `appearance.` (chara.face.head, chara.hair.front.item, coord.clothes.top.item, coord.accessories["\<id>"].item or .category) |
| `ref` | `any` | yes |  | the row "\<guid>#\<id>" (a category number for an accessory's category) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `consequences` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes |  |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.item", "key": "<id>", "path": "chara.face.head", "ref": "illusion.hs2#1"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-layout"></a>

### `chara.edit.layout`

A layout push of the Maker (mole / face paint «配置»): the vector (Scale, Scale, PosX, PosY) of a row of list 6 (mole) or 7 (face paint), looked up by id, into the layout member; rotation and layoutItem untouched (one undo entry "Edit face").

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters (one entry) |
| `path` | `string` | yes |  | chara.face.mole.layout (list 6) or chara.face.makeup.paint[0\|1].layout (list 7), relative to `appearance.` |
| `ref` | `contentId` | yes |  | the layout row "\<guid>#\<id>" (vanilla: illusion.hs2#0..7 of 6, #0..5 of 7) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `consequences` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes |  |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.layout", "key": "<id>", "path": "chara.face.mole.layout", "ref": "illusion.hs2#4"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-list"></a>

### `chara.edit.list`

The rows a Maker list offers: by a character's ref member (its sex; face skins filtered by its head) or by category and sex.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character: its sex (and head for face skins) |
| `path` | `string` |  |  | a ref member relative to `appearance.` (with key): decides the category |
| `category` | `int` |  |  | a list category (ChaListDefine numbers: 210, 211, 240, 351, …) |
| `sex` | `any` |  | `"female"` | 0 / "male" or 1 / "female" (without key) |
| `head` | `string` |  |  | face skins: only the rows of this head ref (default: the character's head with key, else every row) |
| `search` | `string` |  |  | text the row's names or ref must contain (normalised as the UI's search) |
| `language` | `enum(ja\|en\|zh-Hans)` |  | `"ja"` | the names' language (Name, EN_US, ZH_CN) |
| `offset` | `int` |  | `0` | first row |
| `limit` | `int` |  | `200` | rows at most (1..10000) |
| `columns` | `array<string>` |  |  | list columns to include per row as values {column: text} (e.g. ["Scale","PosX","PosY"] of the layout lists 6 / 7; a missing column gives null) |
| `current` | `bool` |  | `false` | with key + path: mark the row the member holds (rows[].current; currentRef / currentIndex) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `total` | `int` | yes | rows of the list (after search) |
| `category` | `int` | yes |  |
| `sex` | `int` | yes |  |
| `rows` | `array<object>` | yes | {ref, name, possess, gated, headRef? (face skins), preset? (head lists: the row names a face preset), values? (columns), current? (current)} in HS2's Maker order; face skins without a head filter: one row per definition, as HS2's list control |
| `currentRef` | `string` |  | current: the member's value |
| `currentIndex` | `int` |  | current: the index of its row among the rows after search (-1: not listed) |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.list", "key": "<id>", "path": "chara.face.skin"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-load"></a>

### `chara.edit.load`

Loads parts of a character file onto characters with HS2's five load flags «顔» face, «体» body, «髪» hair, «コーデ» coordinate, «キャラ設定» profile (the Edit tab's File node). All five replace the whole appearance as Change character does (both documents, the plugin data of both scopes, the resources and the import provenance; the node keeps its id, transform, children, animation, FK / IK, face, look, state and name). Fewer replace the chosen sections of the stored documents (face: the face block, the face-group skin overlays, the bone modifiers of head bones, the MaterialEditor items of the head; body: the rest of the body block and the other character items; hair: the hair block; coordinate: the worn coordinate with its plugin data as the coordinate load «全て読込み»; profile: name, futanari and the imported game parameters) and keep the character's own chara-scope plugin data and import provenance. A partial load needs a character of the same sex (bad_args `path`, reason load.otherSex); a card-backed target is imported first in the same entry. An HS2 card is imported in memory. One undo entry "Load character".

- flags `mutates`, `slow`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 300 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters (one entry) |
| `path` | `path` | yes |  | an HS2 card (.png) or a .hs2nchara file: absolute; a relative .hs2nchara under \<UserData>/chara; any other relative path under \<hs2Root>/UserData/chara/female or /male (sex) |
| `flags` | `any` |  | `"all"` | "all" or a list of the load flags "face", "body", "hair", "coordinate", "profile" (at least one; a list of all five = "all") |
| `sex` | `any` |  |  | 0 / "male" or 1 / "female": the HS2 card folder of a relative card path (default: the first key's sex) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `flags` | `array<string>` | yes | the load flags applied, in the order face, body, hair, coordinate, profile |
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | the member paths the load reported (relative to `appearance.`; "appearance" for all five) |
| `consequences` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `imported` | `bool` | yes | the file was an HS2 card (imported in memory) |
| `importedTargets` | `array<string>` | yes | the card-backed targets that were imported first (node ids) |
| `source` | `object` | yes | {file (absolute), sex (0 / 1), name}: the character read |
| `import` | `object` | yes | the import provenance of the (first) key's stored documents after the load ({from, fileName, sha256, fileSize, importedUtc, importer, hs2Version, coordSex, sideloaderGuids}; null for a character made in HS2Next; all five take the file's, a partial load keeps the character's own) |
| `findings` | `array<string>` | yes | the reader's / importer's findings of warning and error severity |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.load", "flags": ["face", "hair"], "key": "<id>", "path": "BR-Chan HS2 Rev2.png"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-materials"></a>

### `chara.edit.materials`

The drawn materials of a character's renderers by name (read-only): per slot the material, shader, queue, sorted keywords and every property the shader declares (the reply shape of the HS2 Studio Bridge's chara.materials).

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `renderers` | `array<string>` |  |  | renderer object names (default: the set of part) |
| `part` | `enum(face\|body\|hair\|clothes\|accessories)` |  | `"face"` | the default renderer set: face = o_head, o_eyebase_L, o_eyebase_R, o_eyeshadow, o_eyelashes; body = o_body_cf / o_body_cm; hair = every renderer under the four hair part objects (part order back, front, side, option; rendHair, rendAccessory and others), each with hairPart; clothes = every renderer under the eight clothes kind objects (kind order top … shoes, hierarchy order), each with kind; accessories = every renderer under the accessory slot objects (build-slot order, hierarchy order), each with slot; with hair, clothes and accessories texture properties are followed by "\<name>_ST" [scale x, y, offset x, y] (renderers overrides the set) |
| `hairPart` | `any` |  |  | part hair only: one hair part ("back", "front", "side", "option" or 0..3) |
| `kind` | `any` |  |  | part clothes only: one clothes kind ("top", "bottom", "innerTop", "innerBottom", "gloves", "pantyhose", "socks", "shoes" or 0..7) |
| `slot` | `int` |  |  | part accessories only: one build slot (0-based) |
| `props` | `array<string>` |  |  | only these property names (default all; with parts hair, clothes and accessories "\<name>_ST" selects a texture's scale / offset) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `renderers` | `array<object>` | yes | {name, enabled, active, parent, slots [{material, shader, queue, keywords, props {name: value}}], hairPart (part hair), kind (part clothes), slot (part accessories: the build slot)} in hierarchy order (every renderer under the character with one of the names): floats / ints as numbers, colours and vectors as [4] numbers, textures as {name, w, h, format} (parts hair, clothes and accessories: + wrapU, wrapV, mips) or null |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.materials", "key": "<id>", "props": ["_Texture3UV", "_Gloss"], "renderers": ["o_head"]}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-preset"></a>

### `chara.edit.preset`

Pushes the face preset of a head row onto characters (the whole face block, head included; one undo entry "Face preset"). A head whose preset asset is missing gets the head change only, as HS2's push (warning edit.presetMissing); a preset naming an unlisted head ends on the first listed head (edit.presetUnresolved).

- flags `mutates`, `slow`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 120 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters (one entry) |
| `head` | `string` |  |  | the head ref "\<guid>#\<id>" (default: each character's current head) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `consequences` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes |  |

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-reset"></a>

### `chara.edit.reset`

Resets members to each character's reference value (its documents when its edit session first opened in this scene session, HS2's edit-mode reset), else to the default (the Edit tab row's default or `defaults`); the clothes members gloss, metallic, layout (or one component) and rotation reset to the worn prefab's default (the Maker's ↺; without the prefab's defaults the rule above with warning edit.defaultsUnavailable), and so do an accessory's gloss / metallic of colours 1–4; an accessory member's default is its Accessories row's (transform components 0 / 0 / 1 as "…moves[c].position#i", the hair sliders 0.5, noShake false, parent ""); values unclamped, one undo entry "Reset value".

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters (one entry) |
| `paths` | `array<string>` | yes |  | members relative to `appearance.` (chara.face.eyebrow.tilt); one component of a vector or colour as "path#c" (chara.face.eyebrow.layout#2) |
| `defaults` | `array<any>` |  |  | one default per path (number, [numbers], bool, string or null) for characters without a reference; default: the Edit tab row's default of the path, none for paths without a row |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `consequences` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `reference` | `object` | yes | {\<node id>: bool} the character had a reference (else the defaults applied); a path with neither is skipped (warning edit.resetUnavailable) |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.reset", "key": "<id>", "paths": ["chara.face.eyebrow.tilt", "chara.face.eyebrow.layout#2"]}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-rtstats"></a>

### `chara.edit.rtstats`

Statistics of a character's face, body or clothes creators' render textures from a GPU read-back of mip 0 (read-only, numbers only; unavailable without a graphics device).

- flags `readOnly`, `slow`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 120 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `part` | `enum(face\|body\|clothes)` |  | `"face"` | which creators (clothes: per kind and colour group the colour creator, then the detail creator) |
| `kind` | `any` |  |  | part clothes only: one clothes kind (name or 0..7; default every kind) |
| `grid` | `int` |  | `8` | cells per side (1..64) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `part` | `string` | yes |  |
| `grid` | `int` | yes |  |
| `creators` | `array<object>` | yes | {name (colour / detail), creator (the HS2Next name), kind and group (0..2; part clothes), material, mainTex, w, h, srgb, format, mips, mean [r,g,b,a], std [4], cells [grid × grid × 4] (rows from the texture's bottom row v = 0 upwards, u ascending), sha256 (of the RGBA32 mip-0 bytes), props (the create material's properties as chara.edit.materials)}; values are the stored 8-bit values / 255 |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.rtstats", "grid": 8, "key": "<id>", "part": "face"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-set"></a>

### `chara.edit.set`

Sets document members of characters (Maker edit: values stored as given, never clamped; the consequences of HS2's edit rules are added; one undo entry).

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters (one entry) |
| `set` | `object` | yes |  | {path: JSON value}: a path relative to `appearance.` (chara.face.shape[3], coord.clothes.top.item, coord.accessories["\<id>"].parent) |
| `rebuild` | `bool` |  | `false` | allow the members whose change rebuilds the character (chara.body.uncensor, chara.studioOverlays) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | paths that changed (relative to `appearance.`; consequences included) |
| `consequences` | `array<string>` | yes | the rule consequences among changed / noop |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes | paths that already had the value |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.set", "key": "<id>", "set": {"chara.body.skin.color": [1, 0.9, 0.8, 1], "chara.face.shape[3]": 1.5}}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-swatch"></a>

### `chara.edit.swatch`

A colour swatch push of the Maker («カラーサンプル»), a row looked up by id (HS2's seven buttons: the vanilla ids 0..6). Skin (list 336): body.skin.color = HSVToRGB(BaseH, BaseS, BaseV), alpha 1; the face and body colour composites follow (one undo entry "Edit body"). Hair (list 305, on chara.hair.\<part>.baseColor): base / top / under / specular = HSVToRGB of the row's Base / Top / Under / Specular columns (each stored alpha kept), metallic / smoothness = its columns, on that part and with «前髪と後ろ髪の色を統一» on all four; no auto derivation (one undo entry "Edit hair").

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters (one entry; 336 and 305 are shared by both sexes) |
| `path` | `string` | yes |  | chara.body.skin.color (336) or chara.hair.\<back\|front\|side\|option>.baseColor (305), relative to `appearance.` |
| `ref` | `contentId` | yes |  | the swatch row "\<guid>#\<id>" of 336 / 305 (illusion.hs2#0..6) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `consequences` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes |  |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.swatch", "key": "<id>", "path": "chara.hair.back.baseColor", "ref": "illusion.hs2#5"}`

<a id="cmd-shape-info"></a>

### `shape.info` · *HS2 parity*

HS2's shape.info: the body (33) and face (59) slider values of a character from its documents, its head and versions.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character (imported) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bodyValues` | `array<float>` | yes |  |
| `faceValues` | `array<float>` | yes |  |
| `headId` | `int` | yes | the head's local id |
| `headRef` | `string` | yes | the head "\<guid>#\<id>" (HS2Next) |

<a id="cmd-shape-set"></a>

### `shape.set` · *HS2 parity*

HS2's shape.set: sets body / face slider values (whole arrays from index 0, or one index), stored as given; the flush runs before the reply (one undo entry).

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `body` | `array<float>` |  |  | body values from index 0 (at most 33) |
| `face` | `array<float>` |  |  | face values from index 0 (at most 59) |
| `bodyIndex` | `int` |  |  | one body index (0..32) |
| `bodyValue` | `float` |  | `0.5` | its value |
| `faceIndex` | `int` |  |  | one face index (0..58) |
| `faceValue` | `float` |  | `0.5` | its value |
| `minFrames` | `int` |  | `3` | HS2's settle wait (accepted; HS2Next applies before replying) |
| `maxFrames` | `int` |  | `120` | HS2's settle wait (accepted; HS2Next applies before replying) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bodySet` | `int` | yes |  |
| `faceSet` | `int` | yes |  |
| `framesWaited` | `int` | yes | 0 |
| `settled` | `bool` | yes | the edit is realised (built, nothing dirty) |
| `changedTransforms` | `int` | yes | transforms under the body rig whose local TRS changed |
| `bodyValues` | `array<float>` | yes |  |
| `faceValues` | `array<float>` | yes |  |
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | paths that changed (HS2Next) |

Example: `{"cmd": "shape.set", "faceIndex": 3, "faceValue": 1.5, "key": "<id>"}`

## Group: constraint

<a id="cmd-constraint-add"></a>

### `constraint.add` · *experimental*

Adds a constraint (one history entry): the target follows the source in the components named, laid over the model, which stays as it is. A loop of constraints and a set of components the target cannot take are refused and nothing is stored; mosaic parts are not editable, so such a bone is refused as a target and allowed as a source. A bone of a character that is not built is stored without being checked.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `source` | `object` | yes |  | {key, bone?}: a node, or a bone of a character or an item by its address |
| `target` | `object` | yes |  | {key, bone?, ik?}: a node, a bone of a character by its address, or an IK target of a character by name; not bone and ik together |
| `components` | `array<string>` | yes |  | a set of position, rotation, scale, lookAt, parent; parent stands without position, rotation and lookAt, lookAt without rotation |
| `keepOffset` | `bool` |  | `true` | true: the offset that keeps the target where it shows now; false: the identity |
| `weight` | `float` |  | `1.0` | 0..1: how far the value is blended over what shows without the constraint |
| `on` | `bool` |  | `true` | false: it is stored and does not act |
| `label` | `string` |  |  | the row's text, at most 256 characters |
| `index` | `int` |  |  | its place in the list (default: the end) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `constraint` | `string` | yes | the new entry's id |
| `state` | `string` | yes | active, off, sourceMissing, targetMissing, cycle, protected, bad, pending or limit |
| `lag` | `int` | yes | 1: it follows its source one frame behind, else 0 |
| `offset` | `object` | yes | {pos, rot, scale}: rot in Euler degrees; a member no component uses is at its identity |

Example: `{"cmd": "constraint.add", "components": ["parent"], "source": {"bone": "cf_J_Hand_R", "key": "<id>"}, "target": {"key": "<id>"}}`

<a id="cmd-constraint-clear"></a>

### `constraint.clear` · *experimental*

Removes every constraint of the scene (one history entry).

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `removed` | `int` | yes | entries removed |

Example: `{"cmd": "constraint.clear"}`

<a id="cmd-constraint-list"></a>

### `constraint.list` · *experimental*

The scene's constraints in list order, each with how it stands now.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | only the entries with this node at either end |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `constraints` | `array<object>` | yes | {id, label, on, weight, source {key, bone}, target {key, bone, ik}, components, offset {pos, rot, scale}, state (active, off, sourceMissing, targetMissing, cycle, protected, bad, pending or limit), lag (1: it follows its source one frame behind, else 0), effective (the target takes the value now), reason (what the state names, or on an active entry the text key of the note its row shows; else null)} |

Example: `{"cmd": "constraint.list", "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-constraint-move"></a>

### `constraint.move` · *experimental*

Moves a constraint in the list (one history entry). The list's order decides where two constraints write one target and where nothing else orders them.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `constraint` | `string` | yes |  | the entry's id |
| `index` | `int` | yes |  | its new place |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `index` | `int` | yes | its place now |

Example: `{"cmd": "constraint.move", "constraint": "<id>", "index": 0}`

<a id="cmd-constraint-remove"></a>

### `constraint.remove` · *experimental*

Removes a constraint (one history entry); its target shows what it would without it.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `constraint` | `string` | yes |  | the entry's id |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `removed` | `int` | yes | entries removed |

Example: `{"cmd": "constraint.remove", "constraint": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-constraint-set"></a>

### `constraint.set` · *experimental*

Changes a constraint (one history entry): what is given changes, the rest stays. A change that changes nothing adds no entry. A new source or target that closes a loop is refused. takeOffset needs both ends built and reads the values as shown, so on an active constraint it changes nothing: switch it off, pose the target, take the offset, switch it on.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `constraint` | `string` | yes |  | the entry's id |
| `on` | `bool` |  |  | the switch |
| `weight` | `float` |  |  | 0..1 |
| `label` | `string` |  |  | the row's text; null clears it |
| `source` | `object` |  |  | {key, bone?}: a node, or a bone of a character or an item by its address |
| `target` | `object` |  |  | {key, bone?, ik?}: a node, a bone of a character by its address, or an IK target of a character by name; not bone and ik together |
| `components` | `array<string>` |  |  | a set of position, rotation, scale, lookAt, parent; parent stands without position, rotation and lookAt, lookAt without rotation |
| `offset` | `object` |  |  | {pos?, rot?, scale?}: members left out keep their value, a member given as null goes back to its identity; null clears all three |
| `takeOffset` | `bool` |  | `false` | true: the offset becomes what keeps the target where it shows now, after the other members of this request; not with offset |
| `merge` | `bool` |  | `false` | join the history entry of the previous edit of this constraint |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `constraint` | `string` | yes | the entry's id |
| `state` | `string` | yes | active, off, sourceMissing, targetMissing, cycle, protected, bad, pending or limit |
| `lag` | `int` | yes | 1: it follows its source one frame behind, else 0 |
| `offset` | `object` | yes | {pos, rot, scale}: rot in Euler degrees; a member no component uses is at its identity |

Example: `{"cmd": "constraint.set", "constraint": "<id>", "weight": 0.5}`

<a id="cmd-constraint-status"></a>

### `constraint.status` · *experimental*

How the constraints run now: the counts, the order of a frame and the entries one frame behind; with perf, the evaluation's time per frame.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `perf` | `bool` |  | `false` | add perf: the evaluation's time per frame over the frames since the last reset (at most the last 4,096) |
| `reset` | `bool` |  | `false` | with perf: start a new window after this reply |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `count` | `int` | yes | entries of the list |
| `active` | `int` | yes | entries that act now |
| `passActive` | `bool` | yes | the scene steps its characters itself, in the order below |
| `order` | `array<object>` | yes | {unit (a node id or an entry id), kind (chara, item or constraint)} in the order of a frame, empty while the scene does not step its characters itself; a constraint on a bone runs inside its character's step and has no row |
| `lagged` | `array<string>` | yes | the ids of the entries one frame behind |
| `perf` | `object` |  | with perf: {frames, medianUs, p95Us, maxUs, walkUs}: walkUs, the median of what a walk of the pass took beside the characters' steps |

Example: `{"cmd": "constraint.status", "perf": true}`

## Group: content

<a id="cmd-content-quarantine"></a>

### `content.quarantine`

The bundles refused because a process ended while it loaded them (the crash quarantine of the user folder); with clear, one of them is lifted so that the next load opens it again. An entry also lifts itself when its mod file changes.

- flags none; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `clear` | `string` |  |  | a quarantined bundle's key ("\<zipmod file>!\<bundle path>", the folders may be left out) to lift |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `enabled` | `bool` | yes | the quarantine is on (the app turns it on at start; test hosts do not) |
| `cleared` | `bool` |  | with clear: an entry was lifted |
| `entries` | `array<object>` | yes | {key (zipmod file name!path), stamp (size:time of the mod file), since} |
| `lastRun` | `object` | yes | {key, stamp, since}: the entry the previous run's end added at this start, or null |

Example: `{"cmd": "content.quarantine"}`

Example: `{"clear": "[Mod] Example.zipmod!chara/example.unity3d", "cmd": "content.quarantine"}`

<a id="cmd-content-redirect"></a>

### `content.redirect`

Which source serves a named asset load of a bundle path (read-only): the path's copies (the abdata file first, then the zipmods shipping the path in load order) with whether each holds the asset, the copy the per-asset redirect serves it from, the source the catalog read before the redirect, and the switch states. Without asset: every name the zipmod copies hold with its serving copy; with row: the bundle path and asset of a list row's column; with neither: the redirectable paths.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | `string` |  |  | an abdata-relative bundle path ("chara/30/fo_top_30.unity3d"; the exact case is asked first, then any case) |
| `asset` | `string` |  |  | an asset name (case ignored, as a load by name); default every name of the path's zipmod copies |
| `row` | `object` |  |  | instead of path / asset: {category, ref "\<guid>#\<id>", column (the asset column, e.g. MainData, ThumbTex, OverBodyMask), ab (its bundle column; default \<column>AB when the row has it, ThumbAB for ThumbTex, TexAB for Tex* columns, else MainAB)} |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | `string` |  | the path (the indexed case) |
| `redirectable` | `any` | yes | the path has two or more sources (with neither path nor row: the number of such paths) |
| `copies` | `array<object>` |  | {guid (null = the abdata file), file, has (holds the asset; null: names unknown or no asset), names (how many), compression, cab (the serialized files' CAB names), resourceFiles, externals} |
| `served` | `object` |  | {kind (zipmod / abdata / none), guid, file}: the copy a load is served from now (the redirect's when it is on) |
| `today` | `object` |  | {kind, guid, file}: the source without the redirect (the row's own zipmod, else the first shipping one, else abdata) |
| `switch` | `object` | yes | {redirect (ContentCatalog.SideloaderAssetRedirect), zipmodSet (ContentCatalog.SideloaderZipmodSet)} |
| `assets` | `array<object>` |  | without asset: {name, served {kind, guid}} per name of the zipmod copies (abdata-only names are served by the abdata file) |
| `loads` | `int` |  | loads of the path this session that the redirect served from another source than before |
| `cabClashes` | `array<object>` |  | the catalog's content.cabClash findings naming the path: {key, other, resolution} |
| `paths` | `array<string>` |  | with neither path nor row: the redirectable paths (ordinal order) |

Example: `{"asset": "p_cf_top_dougi", "cmd": "content.redirect", "path": "chara/30/fo_top_30.unity3d"}`

<a id="cmd-content-stats"></a>

### `content.stats` · *experimental*

The catalog's open bundles and their owners (read-only; answers during boot): counts by state (owned, waiting in the LRU of unowned bundles, pinned, thumbnail-only, unscoped), bytes against the budget, evictions, assets loaded again after an eviction, the UnloadUnusedAssets calls, the live owners, loads outside every owner's scope, the pre-upload texture check's numbers and the quarantined bundles; with bundles the largest bundles with their owners.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `bundles` | `bool` |  | `false` | add the largest open bundles with their owners, bytes and state |
| `top` | `int` |  | `20` | with bundles: how many (1..500) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `open` | `int` | yes | open bundles |
| `owned` | `int` | yes | open bundles with a live owner (not pins) |
| `lru` | `int` | yes | unowned bundles waiting in the LRU |
| `pinned` | `int` | yes | bundles every character or list needs (never evicted) |
| `thumbnailOnly` | `int` | yes | bundles only thumbnails used |
| `unscoped` | `int` | yes | bundles loaded outside every owner's scope only (never evicted) |
| `pendingOpens` | `int` | yes | asynchronous opens in flight |
| `ownedBytes` | `int` | yes | resident + object bytes of the owned, pinned and unscoped bundles |
| `lruBytes` | `int` | yes | resident bytes of the LRU (what the budget limits; object bytes are freed by UnloadUnusedAssets, not by an eviction) |
| `budgetBytes` | `int` | yes | the LRU's byte budget (content.bundleCacheMB) |
| `evictions` | `int` | yes | bundles evicted (unloaded without destroying their loaded objects) |
| `reloadedAssets` | `int` | yes | assets loaded again from a bundle reopened after its eviction (a second object until the first is unused) |
| `unloadUnused` | `object` | yes | {calls, lastMs, medianMs, maxMs} |
| `owners` | `int` | yes | live owners (realizations, edit-unit generations, motions) |
| `unscopedLoads` | `int` | yes | loads outside every owner's scope (pins not counted) |
| `textureCheck` | `object` | yes | {verified, patched, refused, repairedOpens (opens that read a stored repaired copy: repaired in an earlier session or by the repair tool), cacheHits, pending, prescan {done, total}} |
| `quarantined` | `array<object>` | yes | {key, stamp}: bundles refused because a process died loading them |
| `prefetch` | `object` | yes | the Maker's hover / focus prefetch: {started, assetRequests (asynchronous asset loads started), used (the edit's flush then took the bundle), expired (ended unused), refused (the asset was refused before a request), limited (not started: 4 in flight), inFlight} |
| `bundles` | `array<object>` |  | with bundles: {key (zipmod file name!path or source:path), owners [labels], residentBytes, objectBytes, state} largest first |

Example: `{"bundles": true, "cmd": "content.stats", "top": 10}`

<a id="cmd-content-zipmods"></a>

### `content.zipmods`

The zipmod set (read-only): per GUID in load order the file the loader keeps, its version and \<game> values, whether it loads and why not (the game filter: game; another file of the GUID kept: version), the files of the GUID and the file the earlier set (the last file of the scan per GUID, no game filter) takes.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `guid` | `string` |  |  | one GUID (case ignored; not_found when no scanned zipmod has it) |
| `filter` | `enum(all\|loaded\|skipped\|several)` |  | `"all"` | all GUIDs, the loaded ones, those with a skipped file, those with several files the game filter keeps (a version choice) |
| `offset` | `int` |  | `0` | first entry |
| `limit` | `int` |  | `10000` | entries at most (1..10000) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `switch` | `object` | yes | {zipmodSet, redirect} |
| `scanned` | `int` | yes | zipmod files of the scan (with a GUID) |
| `guids` | `int` | yes |  |
| `loaded` | `int` | yes | GUIDs that load |
| `skipped` | `object` | yes | {game, version}: files the loader skips |
| `notForHs2` | `int` | yes | files whose \<game> values name no HS2 game (skipped by the game filter when the zipmod set is on) |
| `several` | `int` | yes | GUIDs with several files the game filter keeps (the version choice decides) |
| `differs` | `int` | yes | GUIDs whose kept file is not the earlier set's |
| `noGuid` | `int` | yes | zipmod files without a GUID (ignored) |
| `total` | `int` | yes | entries after guid / filter |
| `zipmods` | `array<object>` | yes | {guid, file (the kept file, else the first), version, games (null = not read), forHs2, loaded, skipped (null, game, version), today (the earlier set's file), files [{file, version, skipped}] (every file of the GUID, scan order)} |

Example: `{"cmd": "content.zipmods", "filter": "skipped"}`

## Group: cut

<a id="cmd-cut-make"></a>

### `cut.make` · *experimental*

Cuts a character or an item by a marker or a shape into pieces, in one history entry; the target is hidden, not deleted. A character or a plain item is baked as drawn first (needs a graphics device); a bake item is cut at its FK pose; a piece is cut again from its own source.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | the target: a character or an item |
| `marker` | `node` |  |  | a marker |
| `shape` | `object` |  |  | {kind, pos, rot, size} in world space, in place of marker: kind plane, box, tear or hole; rot [x, y, z] in degrees or [x, y, z, w]; size the full extent per axis in units, absent: the shape's own |
| `mode` | `string` |  |  | plane both, front or back; box both, inside or outside; tear and hole open. Absent: both, or open |
| `ragged` | `object` |  |  | {amp, tooth, random, seed}: a ragged edge, in units (1 = 10 cm): amp 0-0.5, tooth 0.05-2 (absent 0.2), random 0-1 (absent 0.5), seed 1-9999 (absent 1); amp 0 or absent: a clean cut |
| `clothesOnly` | `bool` |  | `false` | cut garments only; every other part goes whole to the side its centre is on |
| `section` | `object` |  |  | {color, ownInside}: the cut faces' colour, [r, g, b] or darkRed, flesh, bone, black, grey (absent dark red), and whether garments and hair show their own fabric inside (absent true) |
| `hide` | `bool` |  | `true` | hide the target |
| `name` | `string` |  | `""` | the pieces' name, with the side when there are two; empty: the target, the side and the time |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pieces` | `array<string>` | yes | the pieces' keys |
| `ms` | `float` | yes | milliseconds the cut took |

Example: `{"cmd": "cut.make", "key": "<id>", "marker": "<marker>", "mode": "both", "ragged": {"amp": 0.1}}`

<a id="cmd-cut-marker"></a>

### `cut.marker` · *experimental*

Adds a marker of a cut, at the centre of a target's drawn objects or at the camera's pivot, in one history entry, and selects it. Markers are drawn in the view and left out of captures.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `kind` | `enum(plane\|box\|tear\|hole)` | yes |  | the shape |
| `target` | `node` |  |  | a node the marker is put on |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `string` | yes | the marker's key |

Example: `{"cmd": "cut.marker", "kind": "plane", "target": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-cut-set"></a>

### `cut.set` · *experimental*

Changes a piece's cut and computes the piece again from its source, in one history entry: the last step's shape (by a marker or values), mode, ragged edge and clothes only, and the section. recut computes it again with no change.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a piece |
| `marker` | `node` |  |  | a marker the last step takes |
| `shape` | `object` |  |  | {kind, pos, rot, size} in world space, in place of marker: kind plane, box, tear or hole; rot [x, y, z] in degrees or [x, y, z, w]; size the full extent per axis in units, absent: the shape's own |
| `mode` | `string` |  |  | plane both, front or back; box both, inside or outside; tear and hole open. Absent: both, or open; a piece of both keeps its own side |
| `ragged` | `any` |  |  | {amp, tooth, random, seed}: a ragged edge, in units (1 = 10 cm): amp 0-0.5, tooth 0.05-2 (absent 0.2), random 0-1 (absent 0.5), seed 1-9999 (absent 1); amp 0 or absent: a clean cut; null: a clean cut |
| `clothesOnly` | `bool` |  |  | cut garments only |
| `section` | `object` |  |  | {color, ownInside}: the cut faces' colour, [r, g, b] or darkRed, flesh, bone, black, grey (absent dark red), and whether garments and hair show their own fabric inside (absent true) |
| `recut` | `bool` |  | `false` | compute the piece again from its source with no change |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `bool` | yes | the piece's cut or its result changed |
| `ms` | `float` | yes | milliseconds it took |

Example: `{"cmd": "cut.set", "key": "<piece>", "section": {"color": "bone"}}`

<a id="cmd-cut-status"></a>

### `cut.status` · *experimental*

A piece's cut as stored.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a piece |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `source` | `object` | yes | {bake, main, data}: the bake that was cut |
| `pose` | `object` | yes | {bone: [x, y, z]}: the source's FK values at the cut, or null for its baked pose |
| `steps` | `array<object>` | yes | {shape, pos, rot, size, keep, ragged, clothesOnly, pose} in the source's root space, from the first source; pose: the source's FK values that step was decided at, null when it takes the previous step's (the first step the block's pose) |
| `section` | `object` | yes | {color, ownInside} |
| `recomputed` | `bool` | yes | the stored result was missing and was computed again when the piece was built |

Example: `{"cmd": "cut.status", "key": "<piece>"}`

## Group: dumps

<a id="cmd-bones-dump"></a>

### `bones.dump` · *HS2 parity*

Every bone transform of a built character (or every transform of a built item) in HS2's form: local position / rotation / Euler / scale as exact doubles, '/'-paths from the root.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 120 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character or item |
| `root` | `enum(body\|head\|all)` |  | `"body"` | body: cf_J_Root without the head bone root; head: p_cf_head_bone; all: body, then head (paths start at the head root's name); ignored for an item (its instance root) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bones` | `array<object>` | yes | {name, path, lp, lq, le, ls} |

<a id="cmd-chara-bones"></a>

### `chara.bones` · *HS2 parity*

World position and forward of named bones of a built character (HS2: rounded to 4 decimals); unknown names are left out.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `names` | `array<string>` | yes |  | bone transform names: the body skeleton, the head skeleton, then a search under the character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bones` | `object` | yes | {\<name>: {pos, forward}} |
| `time` | `float` | yes | Time.time |

<a id="cmd-face-dump"></a>

### `face.dump` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

The head's skinned renderers (blend shapes, weights, face materials), the realized expression and look state of a built character (HS2's form).

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `reflect` | `bool` |  | `true` | also the look angles (eyeLook, neckLook under HS2's names) and the stage switches |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `renderers` | `array<object>` | yes |  |
| `expression` | `object` | yes |  |

## Group: effects

<a id="cmd-effects-reset"></a>

### `effects.reset` · *HS2 parity*

Resets blocks to HS2's defaults (ScreenEffectDefine; the panel's default buttons); one undo entry.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `blocks` | `array<string>` |  |  | block names; default all |
| `label` | `string` |  |  | history label (default "Reset effects") |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | blocks whose values changed |

<a id="cmd-effects-set"></a>

### `effects.set` · *HS2 parity*

Sets effect values by path under effects. (validated before any is applied; one undo entry).

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `set` | `object` | yes |  | {\<path under effects.>: \<value>}: "bloom.intensity": 3, "colorGrading.lut": "illusion.hs2#3", "vignette.enabled": false, "depthOfField.focus": "\<node id>", ... |
| `label` | `string` |  |  | history label (default "Effects") |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | paths written |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes | paths that already had the value |

Example: `{"cmd": "effects.set", "set": {"bloom.intensity": 3, "vignette.intensity": 0.5}}`

<a id="cmd-effects-state"></a>

### `effects.state` · *HS2 parity*

The screen-effect blocks: state values (as scene.json) and what the runtime realized (grading, SSAO, bloom, DOF focus, vignette, probe, fog RenderSettings, sun-shaft caster, self shadow).

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `blocks` | `array<string>` |  |  | block names (colorGrading, ambientOcclusion, bloom, depthOfField, vignette, ssr, reflectionProbe, fog, sunShafts, shadows, ambient, sky); default all |
| `timing` | `bool` |  | `false` | bake / read / frame milliseconds |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `effects` | `object` | yes | {\<block>: state values} |
| `realized` | `object` | yes | per block |
| `findings` | `array<object>` | yes | effects.* and fog.* findings of the scene |
| `timing` | `object` |  | with timing |

## Group: fk/pose

<a id="cmd-fk-capture"></a>

### `fk.capture` · *HS2 parity*

Copies the current pose of the Body, Neck and hand bones into FK, switches FK and its Body and Neck groups on (one history entry); for an item its FK bones' current rotations.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character or item |
| `stopAnime` | `bool` |  | `true` | also set the animation speed to 0 (items: item.anime.speed) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `fkEnabled` | `bool` | yes |  |

<a id="cmd-fk-copy"></a>

### `fk.copy` · *HS2 parity*

Copies the animation pose or the current pose into FK values (one history entry); modes stay unless enable.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character (items: wrong_kind; they keep fk.capture) |
| `from` | `enum(anime\|current)` |  | `"anime"` | anime: the clip and hand patterns (no look, IK, FK); current: the final pose (HS2 "IK → FK") |
| `groups` | `array<string>` |  |  | FK groups (Hair, Neck, Breast, Body, RightHand, LeftHand, Skirt, ignoring case; anime: Body, Neck, RightHand, LeftHand only); default Body, Neck, RightHand, LeftHand |
| `enable` | `bool` |  | `false` | also switch FK and the groups on (with kinematics.exclusive: IK off) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `copied` | `int` | yes | FK values written |
| `from` | `string` | yes |  |
| `fkEnabled` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `ikEnabled` | `bool` | yes |  |

<a id="cmd-fk-enable"></a>

### `fk.enable` · *HS2 parity*

Switches FK, or FK groups, on or off; bones that become applied without a value keep the pose you see (one history entry). IK is not touched.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character, or a built item (no groups; its dynamic bones are off while FK is on) |
| `on` | `bool` |  | `true` | the new state |
| `groups` | `array<string>` |  |  | groups to switch instead of FK itself: Hair, Neck, Breast, Body, RightHand, LeftHand, Skirt (ignoring case); RightArm, LeftArm, RightLeg and LeftLeg switch the Body group, with a warning; characters only |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `fkEnabled` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `ikEnabled` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `activeFK` | `array<bool>` | yes |  |

<a id="cmd-fk-list"></a>

### `fk.list` · *HS2 parity*

The FK bones of a built character as Studio lists them (ascending key) with their model values, and the FK / IK / correction flags; or the FK bones of a built item.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character or item |
| `group` | `string` |  | `""` | only entries whose group text contains this, ignoring case ("Body" = column-0 bones; the limb bones are "3", "5", "9", "17") |
| `exact` | `bool` |  | `false` | rot / pos as exact doubles and scale added (HS2 exact:true); else rounded to 4 decimals |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bones` | `array<object>` | yes | [{bone, key, group, groupFlags, level, boneWeight, rot, pos, scale?, name?}] |
| `fkEnabled` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `activeFK` | `array<bool>` | yes | Hair, Neck, Breast, Body, RightHand, LeftHand, Skirt |
| `hipsPosition` | `vec3` |  | characters: chara.fk.hipsPosition or null (cf_J_Hips' pos stays (0, hipsOffset, 0), HS2's shape) |

<a id="cmd-fk-set"></a>

### `fk.set` · *HS2 parity*

FK values (local Euler degrees) of bones by name, stored under the bone's primary key, and the hips position / offset (one history entry); for an item under the bone name.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character or item |
| `bones` | `object` |  |  | {\<bone name>: [x, y, z]}: model key, partner key or transform name (items: a found bone of the item's bone list); required unless hipsPosition or hipsOffset is given |
| `hipsPosition` | `vec3` |  |  | characters: chara.fk.hipsPosition, the cf_J_Hips local position FK writes with the Body group ([x, y, z], or null = the clip's) |
| `hipsOffset` | `float` |  |  | characters: chara.fk.hipsOffset (HS2_FKHeightAdjustUI's height offset) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `set` | `int` | yes |  |
| `missing` | `array<string>` |  | names that are no FK bone of the character |
| `hipsPosition` | `vec3` |  | characters: the stored hips position or null |

Example: `{"bones": {"cf_J_Spine01": [10, 0, 0]}, "cmd": "fk.set", "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-ik-bake"></a>

### `ik.bake`

Bakes the solved pose into FK: Body-group FK values and fk.hipsPosition, FK and its Body group on, IK off (targets kept; one history entry "Bake IK").

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bones` | `int` | yes | Body-group FK values written |
| `hipsPosition` | `vec3` | yes | the stored cf_J_Hips local position |
| `fkEnabled` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `ikEnabled` | `bool` | yes |  |

<a id="cmd-ik-copy"></a>

### `ik.copy` · *HS2 parity*

Copies the animation pose, the current pose or the imported HS2 IK block into the IK targets (one history entry); modes stay unless enable.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `from` | `enum(anime\|current\|import)` |  | `"anime"` | anime: the clip and hand patterns (no look, IK, FK); current: the final pose; import: the node's hs2.pose IK block (2.3 imports), converted |
| `groups` | `array<string>` |  |  | IK groups (Body, RightLeg, LeftLeg, RightArm, LeftArm, ignoring case); default all (not with import) |
| `enable` | `bool` |  | `false` | also switch IK and the groups on (with kinematics.exclusive: FK off); not with import |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `copied` | `array<string>` | yes | target names written |
| `from` | `string` | yes |  |
| `ikEnabled` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `fkEnabled` | `bool` | yes |  |

<a id="cmd-ik-enable"></a>

### `ik.enable` · *HS2 parity*

Switches IK, or IK groups, on or off; targets of groups that become applied without a value keep the pose you see (one history entry). With app.options kinematics.exclusive, IK on also switches FK off.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `on` | `bool` |  | `true` | the new state |
| `groups` | `array<string>` |  |  | groups to switch instead of IK itself: body, rightLeg, leftLeg, rightArm, leftArm (ignoring case) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ikEnabled` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `fkEnabled` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `activeIK` | `array<bool>` | yes | body, rightLeg, leftLeg, rightArm, leftArm |
| `captured` | `array<string>` | yes | target names written by the capture rule |

<a id="cmd-ik-list"></a>

### `ik.list` · *HS2 parity*

The 13 IK targets of a character in HS2's order and shape (the effective value: the stored one, else the runtime snapshot), the IK / FK flags, and HS2Next's solve info and owned bones.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character (solve, owned and world need a built one) |
| `exact` | `bool` |  | `false` | pos / rot as exact doubles; else rounded to 4 decimals |
| `world` | `bool` |  | `false` | add each target's world pose {pos, rot} (built characters) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ikEnabled` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `enableIK` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `enableFK` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `activeIK` | `array<bool>` | yes | body, rightLeg, leftLeg, rightArm, leftArm |
| `activeFK` | `array<bool>` | yes |  |
| `targets` | `array<object>` | yes | [{name, key 0–12, group, kind (effector / bendGoal), usesRot, pos, rot (null when the target has no value yet), world?: {pos, rot}, stored}] |
| `bodySemantics` | `string` | yes | "hips": the Body target is cf_J_Hips (HS2: "spine01") |
| `effective` | `array<string>` | yes | targets whose value is a runtime snapshot (not stored) |
| `solve` | `object` | yes | {ran, residual {name: distance}, clamped [names], poleFallback {bend goal name: clip / rest}, torsoResidualDeg, ms, ...} of the last solve, or null |
| `owned` | `array<string>` | yes | bones the last solve wrote (FK's value is their starting pose) |

<a id="cmd-ik-set"></a>

### `ik.set` · *HS2 parity*

IK target values by name (one history entry "IK targets"); unspecified fields keep the effective value, the other missing targets keep the pose you see.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character (world space: a built one) |
| `targets` | `object` | yes |  | {\<Name>: {pos?: [x, y, z], rot?: [x, y, z]}}: Body, LeftShoulder, LeftArmChain, LeftHand, RightShoulder, RightArmChain, RightHand, LeftThigh, LeftLegChain, LeftFoot, RightThigh, RightLegChain, RightFoot (ignoring case); rot is used by the hands and feet only |
| `space` | `enum(local\|world)` |  | `"local"` | local: the character root's space (as stored); world: world space |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `set` | `int` | yes |  |
| `missing` | `array<string>` |  | names that are no IK target |
| `rotIgnored` | `array<string>` |  | targets whose rot was ignored (they use none) |
| `captured` | `array<string>` | yes | other missing targets written from the pose you see |

Example: `{"cmd": "ik.set", "key": "<id>", "targets": {"RightHand": {"pos": [0.3, 1.2, 0.1]}}}`

<a id="cmd-pose-list"></a>

### `pose.list`

The pose files under \<hs2Root>/UserData/studio/pose and / or \<UserData>/pose (or a subfolder), filtered by a text; paths are what pose.load takes.

- flags `readOnly`, `slow`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 120 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `q` | `string` |  | `""` | case-insensitive substring of the relative path or the pose name |
| `dir` | `path` |  | `""` | a subfolder of the pose root(s) to scan |
| `limit` | `int` |  | `200` | maximum entries (1–100000) |
| `source` | `enum(hs2\|hs2next\|all)` |  | `"hs2"` | hs2: HS2's pose library; hs2next: the HS2Next pose files of \<UserData>/pose; all: both (HS2's first) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `root` | `path` | yes |  |
| `poses` | `array<object>` | yes | [{path, name, sex, kind (dat / png / hs2npose), source}] |
| `roots` | `object` | yes | {hs2, hs2next}: the scanned roots (null when not scanned) |
| `total` | `int` | yes | matching poses |
| `truncated` | `bool` |  | more poses match than listed |

<a id="cmd-pose-load"></a>

### `pose.load` · *HS2 parity*

Loads an HS2 pose file (.dat or PoseTools .png; IK targets converted) or an HS2Next pose file (.hs2npose) into a built character (one history entry).

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `path` | `path` | yes |  | relative to \<hs2Root>/UserData/studio/pose (a .hs2npose path: relative to \<UserData>/pose), or absolute |
| `parts` | `array<string>` |  |  | HS2Next pose files only: the parts to load (anime, hands, fk, ik, correction, face, look); default every part in the file |
| `ik` | `bool` |  | `true` | HS2 files: convert and write the IK targets of a file with IK (false: 2.3's raw-only import, ikPending) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sexMatches` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `loaded` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `name` | `string` | yes |  |
| `object` | `object` | yes |  |
| `ikApproximated` | `bool` |  | IK targets were converted and written |
| `ikScale` | `int` |  | 1, 10, or 0 (rejected / none) |
| `ikBodyShift` | `vec3` |  | the Body conversion's shift (root space) or null |
| `ikUnreachable` | `array<string>` |  | end targets beyond the reach clamp |
| `parts` | `array<string>` |  | HS2Next pose files: the parts set |
| `source` | `string` | yes | hs2 or hs2next |
| `findings` | `array<object>` | yes |  |

Example: `{"cmd": "pose.load", "key": "<id>", "path": "STANDING/Floor/Pose 1.dat"}`

<a id="cmd-pose-save"></a>

### `pose.save`

Saves a character's pose (anime, hands, fk, ik, correction, face, look) as an HS2Next pose file under \<UserData>/pose; missing FK values and IK targets are filled from the pose you see in the file only (no history entry).

- flags `writesFiles`, `slow`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 120 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a character |
| `path` | `path` | yes |  | relative path under \<UserData>/pose (subfolders created; .hs2npose appended unless present) |
| `name` | `string` |  |  | the pose name (default: the file name) |
| `parts` | `array<string>` |  |  | parts to write: anime, hands, fk, ik, correction, face, look (default all) |
| `overwrite` | `bool` |  | `false` | replace an existing file |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | `string` | yes | absolute path |
| `bytes` | `int` | yes |  |
| `parts` | `array<string>` | yes | parts written |
| `name` | `string` | yes |  |
| `relative` | `string` | yes | relative to \<UserData>/pose ('/'; what pose.load takes) |
| `filledFk` | `array<string>` | yes | FK keys filled in the file |
| `filledIk` | `array<string>` | yes | IK targets filled in the file |

## Group: item motion

<a id="cmd-item-anime"></a>

### `item.anime`

An item's animation: pattern, speed (0–3) and normalized time as one undo entry, restart (not recorded); without arguments the current state.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | the item node |
| `pattern` | `int` |  |  | item.anime.pattern: index into the item's anime patterns (a pattern without a valid entry only stores the value) |
| `speed` | `float` |  |  | item.anime.speed (0–3) |
| `time` | `float` |  |  | item.anime.time: layer-0 normalized time of the current state (of the new one when pattern is given too) |
| `restart` | `bool` |  | `false` | replay the current state from 0 (after the other arguments; not recorded) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `string` | yes | node id |
| `pattern` | `int` | yes | stored item.anime.pattern |
| `speed` | `float` | yes | stored item.anime.speed |
| `time` | `float` | yes | the driver's layer-0 normalized time; the stored item.anime.time without a driven Animator |
| `storedTime` | `float` | yes | item.anime.time in the model (written by captures) |
| `driven` | `bool` | yes | the item has a driven Animator (isAnime with a controller) |
| `state` | `string` | yes | the anime-info state name of the playing layer-0 state, or null |
| `stateHash` | `int` |  | short name hash of the playing layer-0 state |
| `patterns` | `array<object>` | yes | [{name, state, valid}] of the ItemComponent's animeInfos |

Example: `{"cmd": "item.anime", "key": "<id>", "pattern": 1, "speed": 0.5}`

<a id="cmd-item-motion"></a>

### `item.motion`

Runtime switches of an item's motion (pause, time scale) and a physics reset; not saved, not recorded.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | the item node |
| `paused` | `bool` |  |  | CharaMotion.Paused of the item (animation, FK and dynamic bones step with dt 0) |
| `timeScale` | `float` |  |  | CharaMotion.TimeScale of the item (0–10) |
| `resetPhysics` | `bool` |  | `false` | drop the dynamic-bone chains' history (particles at the current bones) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `string` | yes | node id |
| `motion` | `bool` | yes | the item has motion (a driven Animator, FK bones or dynamic bones) |
| `paused` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `timeScale` | `float` | yes |  |
| `time` | `float` | yes | scaled seconds stepped |
| `steps` | `int` | yes |  |
| `errors` | `int` | yes |  |
| `stages` | `array<object>` | yes | [{name, phase, enabled}] in step order |
| `animation` | `object` |  | {state, time, speed} when driven |
| `fk` | `object` |  | {on, bones} |
| `dynamicBones` | `object` |  | {active, chains, activeChains, particles} |

## Group: items

<a id="cmd-item-materials"></a>

### `item.materials` · *HS2 parity*

The materials of a built item per renderer slot: MaterialInfo flags, original and current shader, queue, recorded keywords, Studio property values, texture names.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | the item node |
| `props` | `array<string>` |  |  | extra property names to report |
| `slotsOnly` | `bool` |  | `false` | only slots with a MaterialInfo flag |
| `allProps` | `bool` |  | `false` | props holds every property the shader declares, in chara.edit.materials' forms |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `renderers` | `array<object>` | yes | {path, type, enabled, hidden, active, slots: [{index, flags (letters 123abcEAG, "" unflagged MaterialInfo, null none), material, shaderOriginal, shader, fallback, queue, keywords (recorded originals, sorted), keywordsCurrent, props, textures, textureSize}]} |

<a id="cmd-item-set"></a>

### `item.set` · *HS2 parity*

Sets item-section fields of one or several items (save-format JSON forms), validated all-or-nothing, as one undo entry.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | the item node |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several item nodes (one entry for all) |
| `set` | `object` | yes |  | {\<item-section path>: \<value>}: "colors[0].main": [r,g,b,a], "alpha": 0.5, "emission.power": 2, "panel": {"hs2": "DefaultData/bg/x.png"}, "fk.bones[\"x\"]": [x,y,z], "options[0]": false, ... |
| `label` | `string` |  |  | history label (default by the first path: Item colour / pattern / alpha / emission / panel / FK / dynamic bones / options / animation) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | canonical paths that changed (on any key) |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes | paths that already had the value (on every key) |
| `keys` | `object` |  | with keys: {\<id>: {changed, noop}} |

Example: `{"cmd": "item.set", "key": "<id>", "set": {"alpha": 0.5, "colors[0].main": [1, 0, 0, 1]}}`

<a id="cmd-item-state"></a>

### `item.state` · *HS2 parity*

An item's stored section, what it offers (HS2's inspector gates) with its default-button values, and what the realization holds.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | the item node |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `string` | yes | node id |
| `ref` | `contentId` | yes | the stored item.ref |
| `row` | `object` | yes | {group, category, no, name, guid, fallback} of the loaded list row (null when not built) |
| `state` | `object` | yes | the item section as in scene.doc |
| `features` | `object` | yes | color[3], metallic[3], pattern[3], glass, alpha, emission, emissionColor, emissionPower, lightCancel, panel, particle, fk, dynamicBones, options, animePatterns, animated, icon, sound, lights, check, checkGlass, defaults |
| `realized` | `object` | yes | {state, renderers, slots, ownedMaterials, fallbackShaders, particles, fkBones, fkMissing, dynamicBones, animator, lights, icon, iconActive, sound, audioSuppressed, componentsDisabled, patterns, panel, buildMs} |

Sets a node's own visibility (one undo entry "Visibility"); descendants follow as effective visibility.

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | the node |
| `visible` | `bool` | yes |  | the node's own flag |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `string` | yes | node id |
| `visible` | `bool` | yes | own flag |
| `effective` | `bool` | yes | effective visibility |

## Group: log

<a id="cmd-log-tail"></a>

### `log.tail`

The newest log entries of the app (Unity's log, all threads).

- flags `readOnly`; owner `bridge`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `n` | `int` |  | `50` | entries to return (1-1000) |
| `level` | `enum(log\|warning\|error)` |  | `"log"` | lowest severity (error includes exceptions and asserts) |
| `since` | `int` |  | `0` | only entries with seq greater than this (a previous reply's last) |
| `stack` | `bool` |  | `false` | include stack traces |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `entries` | `array<object>` | yes | {seq, time (UTC), type (log\|warning\|error\|exception\|assert), message, stack?}, oldest first |
| `last` | `int` | yes | seq of the newest entry in the buffer (pass as since next time) |
| `gap` | `bool` | yes | entries after since were already dropped from the buffer |
| `capacity` | `int` | yes |  |

## Group: map/item

<a id="cmd-item-add"></a>

### `item.add` · *HS2 parity*

Adds a Studio item with its prefab defaults (one undo entry "Add item").

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `group` | `int` |  |  | item group (checked against the row for vanilla ints); 647158 = the accessories offered as items, with category |
| `category` | `int` |  |  | item category (checked against the row for vanilla ints); with group 647158 the accessory category 351-363 |
| `no` | `int` |  |  | item number: a vanilla item alone, a mod's own id with guid |
| `guid` | `string` |  |  | the item's source GUID (with no) |
| `ref` | `contentId` |  |  | "guid#no" (item.search's id) |
| `parent` | `node` |  |  | parent node (default the scene root) |
| `point` | `string` |  |  | attach point when parent is a character (N_*) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `added` | `array<object>` | yes | the new node as in scene.objects |

Example: `{"category": 0, "cmd": "item.add", "group": 0, "no": 399}`

<a id="cmd-item-parent"></a>

### `item.parent` · *HS2 parity*

Moves a node onto a character's attach point, under another node or back to the root, keeping its world pose (one undo entry "Parent").

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | the node to move |
| `chara` | `node` |  |  | the character to attach to |
| `point` | `string` |  |  | attach point name N_* or its number "1"-"54" ("48" = N_Hand_R); empty or absent with chara = unparent |
| `parent` | `node` |  |  | any node that takes children (point then null) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `parent` | `string` | yes | point name, parent label or "" |
| `local` | `vec3` | yes | new parent-local position (R4) |
| `localRot` | `euler` | yes | new parent-local rotation (R4) |
| `bone` | `string` |  | the attach transform's name |

<a id="cmd-item-search"></a>

### `item.search` · *HS2 parity*

Visible Studio items whose "group / category / name" contains q.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `q` | `string` |  | `""` | case-insensitive substring of "\<group name> / \<category name> / \<item name>" |
| `group` | `int` |  |  | only this item group |
| `category` | `int` |  |  | only this item category |
| `limit` | `int` |  | `60` | at most this many |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `items` | `array<object>` | yes | {group, category, no, groupName, categoryName, name, id, guid} |
| `truncated` | `bool` |  | more than limit |

<a id="cmd-map-load"></a>

### `map.load` · *HS2 parity*

Sets the scene's map (one undo entry "Map"); replies when it has loaded or failed unless wait is false.

- flags `mutates`, `slow`, `defers`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 300 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `no` | `int` |  |  | map number: a vanilla map alone, a mod's own id with guid |
| `guid` | `string` |  |  | the map's source GUID (with no) |
| `ref` | `contentId` |  |  | "guid#no" (the reply's id) |
| `name` | `string` |  |  | the first visible map whose name equals it (ignoring case), else the first that contains it |
| `clear` | `bool` |  | `false` | no map |
| `wait` | `bool` |  | `true` | reply when the map is Loaded or failed |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `no` | `int` | yes | local id (−1 none) |
| `name` | `string` | yes | map name |
| `ref` | `contentId` | yes | "guid#no" (top-level id is the envelope's) |
| `guid` | `string` | yes | source GUID |
| `state` | `string` | yes | MapState |
| `loadMs` | `int` | yes | load time |
| `findings` | `array<object>` | yes | map.* findings of this load |
| `note` | `string` |  | with wait false |
| `features` | `object` | yes | {option, light}: the MapComponent's blocks |
| `mainLight` | `object` | yes | {name, reason} |
| `reselected` | `bool` |  | the map was already the scene's: nothing loaded, the pose kept, no history entry (M3) |

Example: `{"cmd": "map.load", "no": 10}`

<a id="cmd-maps"></a>

### `maps` · *HS2 parity*

The visible Studio maps in load order.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `q` | `string` |  | `""` | case-insensitive substring of the name |
| `limit` | `int` |  | `50` | at most this many |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `maps` | `array<object>` | yes | {no, name, id, guid} |
| `truncated` | `bool` |  | more than limit |

## Group: maps

<a id="cmd-map-renderers"></a>

### `map.renderers` · *HS2 parity*

The loaded map's renderers in pre-order with lightmap data, rendering layers and per-slot materials (original and current shader, keywords as loaded, queue).

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `filter` | `string` |  | `""` | path substring (ordinal) |
| `offset` | `int` |  | `0` | skip this many matches |
| `limit` | `int` |  | `2000` | at most this many |
| `materials` | `bool` |  | `true` | per-slot materials |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `count` | `int` | yes | returned |
| `total` | `int` | yes | matching the filter |
| `renderers` | `array<object>` | yes | {path, type, layer, enabled, active, lightmapIndex, lightmapScaleOffset, staticBatched, renderingLayerMask, materials: [{name, shaderOriginal, shader, keywords, queue} \| null]} |

<a id="cmd-map-set"></a>

### `map.set` · *HS2 parity*

Sets the map pose and the option / light flags (validated before any is applied; one undo entry).

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `pos` | `vec3` |  |  | scene.map.pos |
| `rot` | `euler` |  |  | scene.map.rot (stored as given) |
| `option` | `bool` |  |  | scene.map.options (the MapComponent's option blocks) |
| `light` | `bool` |  |  | scene.map.lights (the light blocks) |
| `label` | `string` |  |  | history label (default: Map pose / Map options / Map lights) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | of pos, rot, option, light |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes | the names that already had the value |
