<a id="cmd-map-state"></a>

### `map.state` · *HS2 parity*

The loaded map: roots, pose, flags, MapComponent features and blocks, sea, RenderSettings, lightmaps, probes, counts, the main light; optionally every map light and the post-load step timing.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 120 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `lights` | `bool` |  | `false` | every Light under the roots (pre-order, inactive included) |
| `blocks` | `bool` |  | `true` | the MapComponent's option / light block objects |
| `timing` | `bool` |  | `false` | bundle / load / per-step milliseconds |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `no` | `int` | yes | local id (−1 none) |
| `ref` | `contentId` | yes | "guid#id" or null |
| `guid` | `string` | yes |  |
| `name` | `string` | yes |  |
| `state` | `string` | yes | MapState |
| `loading` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `scenePath` | `string` | yes |  |
| `roots` | `array<object>` | yes | {name, path, pos, rot (localEulerAngles), active (activeSelf), children} |
| `pose` | `object` | yes | {pos, rot} of scene.map |
| `flags` | `object` | yes | {option, light} of scene.map |
| `features` | `object` | yes | {option, light, componentPath, componentRoot} |
| `blocks` | `object` |  | {options: [{on: [{path, active}], off}], lights} |
| `sea` | `object` | yes | {renderers, keywordOff} |
| `renderSettings` | `object` | yes | the active scene's RenderSettings |
| `lightmaps` | `object` | yes | {count, mode, textures: [{index, color, dir, mask}]} |
| `probes` | `object` | yes | {count, tetrahedralized} |
| `counts` | `object` | yes | {renderers, active, lightmapped, staticBatched, lights, lightsActive, shadowed, reflectionProbes, terrains, cameras, audioSources, audioPlaying, mirrors, scripts} |
| `mainLight` | `object` | yes | {name, path, reason} |
| `mapScripts` | `object` | yes | {lightCurves, uvScrolls, fountainUVs, time, running} |
| `audio` | `object` | yes | {sources, wasPlaying, disabled, unavailable, link} |
| `lights` | `array<object>` |  | with lights: {path, type, enabled, active, color, intensity, original {color, intensity}, range, spot, shadows, shadowStrength, cull, renderingLayers, cookie, bakeType, isBaked, renderMode} |
| `timing` | `object` |  | with timing: {bundleMs, loadMs, steps: {name: ms}} |
| `findings` | `array<object>` | yes | map.* findings of the current map's load |

## Group: materials

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-bonemods"></a>

### `chara.edit.boneMods` · *experimental*

The bone modifiers of a built character, stored with the character, and the bones of its hair.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `hair` | `bool` |  | `true` | only the modifiers of the hair bones and those whose bone is found nowhere |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `mods` | `array<object>` | yes | {bone, location, scale, length, position, rotation, found (the bone resolves on this character)} |
| `bones` | `array<string>` | yes | the names of the hair bones: the hair root and what lies under it |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.boneMods", "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-bonemods-clear"></a>

### `chara.edit.boneMods.clear` · *experimental*

Removes bone modifiers (one history entry): the entries of one bone, or of every hair bone.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `bone` | `string` |  |  | one bone's name, a stored entry that is found nowhere included |
| `all` | `bool` |  |  | true: every hair bone |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `removed` | `int` | yes | the bones whose entries left |

Example: `{"all": true, "cmd": "chara.edit.boneMods.clear", "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-bonemods-set"></a>

### `chara.edit.boneMods.set` · *experimental*

Changes the modifier of a hair bone (one history entry): the members given, the others keep their value. A member given as null goes back to no effect; a modifier with no effect removes the entry. Every other modifier stays as it is. A bone that keeps the mosaic in place answers unsupported.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `bone` | `string` | yes |  | a hair bone's name |
| `scale` | `vec3` |  |  | [x, y, z] |
| `length` | `float` |  |  | the bone's length factor |
| `position` | `vec3` |  |  | [x, y, z] |
| `rotation` | `euler` |  |  | Euler degrees |
| `merge` | `bool` |  | `false` | join the history entry of the previous edit of this bone |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bone` | `string` | yes | the bone |
| `removed` | `bool` | yes | the entry left |

Example: `{"bone": "N_hair_Root", "cmd": "chara.edit.boneMods.set", "key": "<id>", "length": 1.1}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-materials-clear"></a>

### `chara.edit.materials.clear` · *experimental*

Removes items of a material array of a built character (one history entry): one item, the items of a material, or all of them. Read-only items stay; what they replaced shows again.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `target` | `string` | yes |  | the array: character, hair:\<part or 0..3>, clothes:\<kind or 0..7> or acc:\<id> |
| `item` | `object` |  |  | one item's key: {type, material, property}, {type: shader, material} or {type: renderer, renderer, property} |
| `material` | `string` |  |  | every editable item of this material |
| `all` | `bool` |  |  | true: every editable item of the array |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `removed` | `int` | yes | items removed |

Example: `{"all": true, "cmd": "chara.edit.materials.clear", "key": "<id>", "target": "character"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-materials-items"></a>

### `chara.edit.materials.items` · *experimental*

The items of a built character's material arrays and what each does now.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `target` | `string` |  |  | one array: character, hair:\<part or 0..3>, clothes:\<kind or 0..7> or acc:\<id> (default: all of them) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `targets` | `array<object>` | yes | {target, items: [{index, type, key (null for an unknown type), editable, status, why (null when it applies), then the item's stored members}]} |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.materials.items", "key": "<id>", "target": "hair:back"}`

<a id="cmd-chara-edit-materials-set"></a>

### `chara.edit.materials.set` · *experimental*

Edits a material array of a built character (one history entry; all the edits or none). A value equal to what the material holds without the item removes the item; a member given as null clears it. A card-backed character is imported first. Mosaic parts answer unsupported.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `target` | `string` | yes |  | the array: character, hair:\<part or 0..3>, clothes:\<kind or 0..7> or acc:\<id> |
| `type` | `enum(float\|color\|keyword\|renderer\|texture\|shader)` |  |  | one edit: the item's type |
| `material` | `string` |  |  | the material's name, as stored; every material of that name on the target |
| `property` | `string` |  |  | the property as stored, without the first underscore of the shader's name; a renderer's property is Enabled |
| `renderer` | `string` |  |  | a `renderer` item: the renderer's name on a character, its path on an item or the map |
| `value` | `any` |  |  | float: a number; color: [r, g, b, a]; keyword: true or false; renderer: 0 or 1 |
| `file` | `path` |  |  | texture: a PNG or JPEG to take into the scene, at most 8192 pixels on a side and 64 MiB: relative to UserData, or an absolute path |
| `image` | `string` |  |  | texture: the res name of an image the scene holds; null clears it |
| `offset` | `any` |  |  | texture: [x, y]; null clears it |
| `scale` | `any` |  |  | texture: [x, y]; null clears it |
| `shader` | `string` |  |  | shader: a name of materials.shaders; null clears it |
| `renderQueue` | `int` |  |  | shader: 0..5000; null clears it |
| `items` | `array<object>` |  |  | several edits instead of one, each as those arguments |
| `merge` | `bool` |  | `false` | with one edit: join the history entry of the previous edit of this item |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `target` | `string` | yes | the array |
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | the keys of the items set, as \<type>/\<material or renderer>/\<property> |
| `removed` | `array<string>` | yes | the keys of the items that left |
| `items` | `array<object>` | yes | the items the edits name, as chara.edit.materials.items lists them |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.edit.materials.set", "key": "<id>", "material": "<material>", "property": "Metallic", "target": "clothes:top", "type": "float", "value": 0.4}`

<a id="cmd-item-materials-clear"></a>

### `item.materials.clear` · *experimental*

Removes items of the material array of a built item (one history entry).

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built item |
| `item` | `object` |  |  | one item's key: {type, material, property}, {type: shader, material} or {type: renderer, renderer, property} |
| `material` | `string` |  |  | every editable item of this material |
| `all` | `bool` |  |  | true: every editable item of the array |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `removed` | `int` | yes | items removed |

Example: `{"all": true, "cmd": "item.materials.clear", "key": "<id>"}`

Edits the material array of a built item (one history entry; all the edits or none). A material edit sits on top of the item's colours and patterns. A renderer is named by its path from the item. Mosaic parts answer unsupported.

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|

| `renderer` | `string` |  |  | a `renderer` item: the renderer's name on a character, its path on an item or the map |
| `value` | `any` |  |  | float: a number; color: [r, g, b, a]; keyword: true or false; renderer: 0 or 1 |
| `file` | `path` |  |  | texture: a PNG or JPEG to take into the scene, at most 8192 pixels on a side and 64 MiB: relative to UserData, or an absolute path |
| `image` | `string` |  |  | texture: the res name of an image the scene holds; null clears it |
| `offset` | `any` |  |  | texture: [x, y]; null clears it |

| `shader` | `string` |  |  | shader: a name of materials.shaders; null clears it |

| `items` | `array<object>` |  |  | several edits instead of one, each as those arguments |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |

| `target` | `string` | yes | item |
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | the keys of the items set |
| `removed` | `array<string>` | yes | the keys of the items that left |
| `items` | `array<object>` | yes | the items the edits name |

Example: `{"cmd": "item.materials.set", "key": "<id>", "material": "<material>", "property": "Color", "type": "color", "value": [1, 0, 0, 1]}`

<a id="cmd-map-materials"></a>

### `map.materials` · *experimental*

The loaded map's materials by name: each is one copy shared by its renderers.

## Group: motion

<a id="cmd-chara-motion"></a>

### `chara.motion`

Runtime motion switches of a built character (pause, time scale, auto step, physics, look, stages), joint correction, reset and hand stepping; not saved.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `paused` | `bool` |  |  | CharaMotion.Paused (steps run with dt 0) |
| `timeScale` | `float` |  |  | CharaMotion.TimeScale (≥ 0) |
| `autoStep` | `bool` |  |  | step from LateUpdate every frame (false: only step moves the character) |
| `physics` | `bool` |  |  | false: dynamic bones and bust physics off, the bust chains in their kinematic pose (HS2 shape.physics on:false); true: back on |
| `look` | `bool` |  |  | false: the eye and neck look stages off; true: back on |
| `stages` | `object` |  |  | {\<stage name>: bool} for the stages of the character (anim, hands, neck, eyes, face, correct, bust, dynbone, ...); the FK writer's stages are switched with fk.enable |
| `correction` | `object` |  |  | {master: bool} = the correct stage; {flags: [8 bools]} = the model's chara.correction (recorded) |
| `step` | `object` |  |  | {dt: float (0 \< dt ≤ 1, default 1/60), n: int (0–10000, default 1)}: step the character by hand now |
| `reset` | `bool` |  | `false` | drop the stage histories (time and step count back to 0) and the node's earlier overrides |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `paused` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `timeScale` | `float` | yes |  |
| `autoStep` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `time` | `float` | yes | CharaMotion.Time (scaled seconds stepped) |
| `stepCount` | `int` | yes |  |
| `errors` | `int` | yes |  |
| `maxStep` | `float` | yes |  |
| `physics` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `look` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `stages` | `array<object>` | yes | [{name, phase, enabled, owner: fk \| motion \| bridge \| a plugin's guid}] in step order |
| `correction` | `object` | yes | {master, flags} |
| `frame` | `int` | yes | the bridge frame of this reply |

Example: `{"autoStep": false, "cmd": "chara.motion", "key": "<id>", "physics": false, "step": {"dt": 0.016666668, "n": 60}}`

## Group: plugin

<a id="cmd-plugin-disable"></a>

### `plugin.disable`

Disables a loaded or faulted plugin now: its Unload, then everything it registered is removed (commands, ext handlers, events, frame actions, undo entries, hooks, supplies, UI); characters its build hooks touched are rebuilt. Its assembly, statics and objects stay in the process.

- flags `writesFiles`; owner `core`; since 1.1.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `guid` | `string` | yes |  | the plugin's GUID |
| `persist` | `bool` |  | `false` | also add the GUID to plugins.disabled in the settings file (it then stays disabled at the next start) |
| `withDependents` | `bool` |  | `false` | also disable the loaded plugins that depend on it (first, in reverse load order); without it they refuse the disable |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `guid` | `string` | yes | the plugin's GUID as its manifest writes it |
| `state` | `string` | yes | its state now (disabled) |
| `disabled` | `array<string>` | yes | the GUIDs disabled, in that order (dependents first) |
| `persisted` | `bool` | yes | the settings file's plugins.disabled holds the GUID |
| `rebuilt` | `int` | yes | characters queued for a rebuild (their last build ran one of its build hooks) |
| `overrides` | `int` | yes | runtime override slots released (the scene shows the model values again) |
| `ms` | `float` | yes | the time of the disable |

Example: `{"cmd": "plugin.disable", "guid": "stp3.sample.sequencer"}`

<a id="cmd-plugin-enable"></a>

### `plugin.enable`

Enables a disabled, faulted or failed plugin now: the load steps on the assemblies already loaded, a new instance and Load, the open scene's ext blocks of its namespaces read again, then SceneLoaded (Initial) and AppReady. A DLL that changed on disk loads after a restart (plugin.staleAssembly).

- flags `writesFiles`; owner `core`; since 1.1.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `guid` | `string` | yes |  | the plugin's GUID |
| `persist` | `bool` |  | `false` | also remove the GUID from plugins.disabled in the settings file |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `guid` | `string` | yes | the plugin's GUID as its manifest writes it |
| `state` | `string` | yes | its state now (loaded) |
| `persisted` | `bool` | yes | the settings file's plugins.disabled no longer holds the GUID |
| `ms` | `float` | yes | the time of the enable |
| `extBlocks` | `int` | yes | the open scene's ext blocks its handlers read again |
| `rebuilt` | `int` | yes | characters queued for a rebuild (it registered build hooks) |

Example: `{"cmd": "plugin.enable", "guid": "stp3.sample.sequencer"}`

<a id="cmd-plugin-list"></a>

### `plugin.list`

The plugins of this run: the plugin folder and switch, and every candidate with its state, reason, assemblies, commands, ext namespaces, event handlers, engine registrations, faults and Update times.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `diagnostics` | `bool` |  | `false` | add the managed bytes after a full collection and the engine object counts (leak checks) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `root` | `path` | yes | the plugin folder of this run (absolute), null before the plugins stage |
| `enabled` | `bool` | yes | plugins load in this run |
| `source` | `string` | yes | what decided the folder and the switch: settings, -pluginsDir, -noPlugins or env |
| `plugins` | `array<object>` | yes | every candidate: guid, name, version, apiVersion, dir, state, reason, assemblies, entryType, commands, extNamespaces, events, history, overrides, hooks, supplies, ui, faults, lastError, enableError, updateMs, loadMs, unknown |
| `diagnostics` | `object` |  | with diagnostics: managedBytes, gameObjects, materials, meshes, textures, renderTextures |

## Group: ragdoll

<a id="cmd-fx-shoot"></a>

### `fx.shoot` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

One bullet: it pushes the parts it passes through, ends at a wall or the ground, and draws a tracer and a flash, blood when that option is on. With no body in the scene it draws and hits nothing. No history.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `from` | `any` | yes |  | where it starts: [x, y, z] or {key, bone, offset}, {key, accessory} (the muzzle chara.accessoryAxis reads for that accessory slot of a character), or an item's key (its far end towards the target) |
| `target` | `any` | yes |  | what it aims at: [x, y, z] or {key, bone, offset}; on a character the bone defaults to cf_J_Spine02 |
| `spread` | `float` |  | `0.0` | a random offset in a disc of that angle, in degrees |
| `kill` | `bool` |  | `false` | the first character hit goes limp |
| `momentum` | `float` |  | `181.0` | how hard |
| `stain` | `bool` |  | `false` | the first character hit takes a stain |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `hits` | `int` | yes | the bodies hit |
| `muzzle` | `vec3` | yes | where the tracer starts |
| `target` | `vec3` | yes | where the bullet ended |

Example: `{"cmd": "fx.shoot", "from": [0, 10, 10], "momentum": 40, "target": {"bone": "cf_J_Spine02", "key": "<id>"}}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-bake"></a>

### `ragdoll.bake` · *experimental*

Writes the body's pose to FK and releases the body at once, in one history entry: the groups Body and Neck and the hips position take what the body wrote, the hips offset goes to 0, FK and those groups go on, IK goes off, the animation stops. What corrections lay on top stays on top, so the view does not move.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last ragdoll.build, else the selection's first character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `bones` | `int` | yes | the FK bones written |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.bake"}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-build"></a>

### `ragdoll.build` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

Makes a character a physics body that follows its pose until it is hit; one history entry. A character that has a body keeps it. The character becomes the one a command without a key means. The first body of a scene makes the physics world, the last one's end takes it away.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last ragdoll.build, else the selection's first character |
| `mapFloor` | `bool` |  | `true` | the floor comes from the map; false: from the feet, for the whole scene |
| `room` | `array<float>` |  |  | [x0, x1, z0, z1]: the room bodies stay in, written to the scene |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `built` | `bool` | yes | true when a body was made; false when it had one |
| `floor` | `float` |  | the height of the floor, with mapFloor true and a map that answers |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.build", "key": "<id>", "room": [-20, 20, -10, 10]}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-follower"></a>

### `ragdoll.follower` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

Makes a folder that follows a part's bone, by a constraint with no offset, in one history entry. The folder goes on following the bone after a release.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last ragdoll.build, else the selection's first character |
| `part` | `string` |  | `"hand_R"` | a part: pelvis, abdomen, chest, head, upperarm_L, _R, forearm_L, _R, hand_L, _R, thigh_L, _R, shin_L, _R, foot_L, _R |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `folder` | `string` | yes | the folder's key |
| `constraint` | `string` | yes | the constraint's id |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.follower", "part": "hand_R"}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-knock"></a>

### `ragdoll.knock` · *experimental*

The body goes down, and gets up by itself when its values say so. A limp body stays limp. No history.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last ragdoll.build, else the selection's first character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `state` | `string` | yes | up, down, gettingUp or limp, after it |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.knock"}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-lock"></a>

### `ragdoll.lock` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

Each part whose key starts with one of the names holds its rotation against its parent as it is now, with a strong muscle, also on a limp body; one history entry.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last ragdoll.build, else the selection's first character |
| `parts` | `string` |  | `"upperarm,forearm,hand"` | names, comma separated, that part keys start with |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `locked` | `int` | yes | the number of joints |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.lock", "parts": "forearm,hand"}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-obstacle"></a>

### `ragdoll.obstacle` · *experimental*

An item takes part in the physics as a static collider that follows it, or stops; one history entry.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | an item |
| `on` | `bool` |  | `true` | false: it stops |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `obstacles` | `int` | yes | how many items take part now |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.obstacle", "key": "<id>"}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-parts"></a>

### `ragdoll.parts` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

Where the parts of a body are.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last ragdoll.build, else the selection's first character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `parts` | `object` | yes | {\<part>: {pos, forward, up}}: the centre of mass and the bone's forward and up as the body sets them, to three decimals |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.parts"}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-pin"></a>

### `ragdoll.pin` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

Pins a part at its centre of mass where it is, or to something it then follows; one history entry.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last ragdoll.build, else the selection's first character |
| `part` | `string` |  | `"hand_R"` | a part: pelvis, abdomen, chest, head, upperarm_L, _R, forearm_L, _R, hand_L, _R, thigh_L, _R, shin_L, _R, foot_L, _R |
| `follow` | `any` |  |  | what the pin follows: a node's key, or {key, bone} |
| `snap` | `bool` |  | `false` | the pin goes onto what it follows; false: it keeps the distance it has |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pinned` | `bool` | yes | true |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.pin", "follow": {"bone": "cf_J_Hand_L", "key": "<id>"}, "part": "hand_R", "snap": true}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-push"></a>

### `ragdoll.push` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

An impulse on a part, given in world axes, in the character's frame (z is its front), or away from a node. Every part wakes. No history.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last ragdoll.build, else the selection's first character |
| `part` | `string` |  | `"chest"` | a part: pelvis, abdomen, chest, head, upperarm_L, _R, forearm_L, _R, hand_L, _R, thigh_L, _R, shin_L, _R, foot_L, _R |
| `impulse` | `vec3` |  |  | the impulse in world axes |
| `local` | `vec3` |  |  | the impulse in the character's frame |
| `away` | `node` |  |  | a node: the impulse runs from it to the bone, level, by strength, plus lift upwards |
| `bone` | `string` |  | `"cf_J_Spine02"` | the bone of the character for away |
| `strength` | `float` |  | `100.0` | the impulse for away |
| `lift` | `float` |  | `0.0` | the part of it upwards for away |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `pushed` | `bool` | yes | true |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.push", "local": [0, 0, -250], "part": "chest"}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-pusher"></a>

### `ragdoll.pusher` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

A capsule along two bones of a character, with or without a body, that moves bodies and that bodies do not move; one history entry. A release with no key takes it away.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last ragdoll.build, else the selection's first character |
| `a` | `string` |  | `"cf_J_LegLow01_R"` | a bone |
| `b` | `string` |  | `"cf_J_Toes01_R"` | the other bone |
| `radius` | `float` |  | `0.45` | its radius |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `count` | `int` | yes | how many pushers there are now |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.pusher", "key": "<id>", "radius": 0.5}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-release"></a>

### `ragdoll.release` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

With no key every body, prop, pusher and effect goes and the physics world with them, so the next ragdoll.build starts in a fresh world: a loop's first event repeats a run this way. With a key that body goes and the rest stay. A body's pins, ties, holds and pose go with it, its values stay. One history entry; nothing to release makes none.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: every character |
| `blend` | `bool` |  | `false` | with a key and a running clock the bones go to the pose over the scene's blend time |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.release"}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-set"></a>

### `ragdoll.set` · *experimental*

Sets values in one history entry: a character's muscle, muscleDamping, pin, pinFreq, pinFalloff, pinHipsOnly, gravityComp, knockDistance, knockHit, downMuscle, minDown, getUp, relocate, faceCamera, legCollision, mass; the scene's gravity, timeScale, hz, friction, angularDrag, pushSpeed, grabStiffness, grabMax, followStiffness, followMax, blendOut, transfer, maxPartDv, hitWeaken, weakenRegain, weakMuscle, staggerAt, momentum, fireRate, propKind, propMass, propSpeed, propSize; room as [x0, x1, z0, z1] or {center, size}, room.center, room.size; floor as {mode, offset, height}, floor.mode, floor.offset, floor.height. Numbers are stored as given; the ranges act when a value is applied. A preset comes first, so a value given with it wins. snap puts every part on its target, at rest, standing.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last ragdoll.build, else the selection's first character; needed for a character's values, preset and snap |
| `values` | `object` |  |  | name: value |
| `snap` | `bool` |  | `false` | every part goes to its target |
| `preset` | `enum(limp\|stiff\|puppet\|hipsPin)` |  |  | the values of a preset |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | the names given that changed something, with preset and snap |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.set", "preset": "limp", "values": {"floor.mode": "feet", "timeScale": 0.5}}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-settle"></a>

### `ragdoll.settle` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

For a time the trunk turns its front towards a direction, as a spring with a damper. No history, not saved.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last ragdoll.build, else the selection's first character |
| `dir` | `vec3` |  | `[0.0, 1.0, 0.0]` | the direction the front of the trunk goes to, [x, y, z]; absent: up |
| `seconds` | `float` |  | `2.0` | how long, in scene seconds |
| `gain` | `float` |  | `6.0` | the spring |
| `damp` | `float` |  | `1.5` | the damper |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ok2` | `bool` | yes | true when the character has a body |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.settle", "damp": 3, "gain": 16, "seconds": 5.5}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-status"></a>

### `ragdoll.status` · *experimental*

How the physics world stands, and with a key that character, with none every character that has a body.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `world` | `object` | yes | {bodies, props, pushers, hz, steps, floor, floorSource, room, lastHit}: steps the substeps run; floor the room's floor and floorSource map, feet or fixed, both null with no room; room {center [x, z], size [w, h, d]} or null; lastHit the speed the last prop gave a body or null |
| `characters` | `array<object>` | yes | {key, state, awake, parts, pins [{pin, part, follow, lag, state}], ties, holds, overridden, protected, settled, weakest}: state up, down, gettingUp or limp, null with no body; awake true when a part is awake; parts how many; a pin's follow the node it follows or null, its lag 1 when a constraint of the same frame moves that node, its state ok, missing or off; ties and holds how many; overridden and protected the parts whose bone a driven bone writes and the parts that drive nothing; settled true when a pose at rest is kept; weakest what hits took from the weakest part, 0 to 1 |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.status"}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-throw"></a>

### `ragdoll.throw` · *experimental*

Throws a prop along a ray, from where it enters the room. Props last 20 seconds, 40 at most, and are not saved. No history.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `from` | `any` |  |  | where the ray starts: [x, y, z] or {key, bone, offset}, or an item's key |
| `target` | `any` |  |  | what it aims at: [x, y, z] or {key, bone, offset} |
| `ray` | `object` |  |  | {origin, dir}, in place of from and target |
| `kind` | `enum(ball\|box\|heavy)` |  |  | absent: the scene's propKind |
| `mass` | `float` |  |  | absent: the scene's propMass |
| `speed` | `float` |  |  | absent: the scene's propSpeed |
| `size` | `float` |  |  | absent: the scene's propSize |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `props` | `int` | yes | how many props there are now |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.throw", "from": [0, 10, 30], "kind": "box", "target": [0, 8, 0]}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-tie"></a>

### `ragdoll.tie` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

Ties two parts where they are now, until a release; one history entry.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last ragdoll.build, else the selection's first character |
| `a` | `string` |  | `"hand_L"` | a part: pelvis, abdomen, chest, head, upperarm_L, _R, forearm_L, _R, hand_L, _R, thigh_L, _R, shin_L, _R, foot_L, _R |
| `b` | `string` |  | `"hand_R"` | the other part |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tied` | `bool` | yes | true |

Example: `{"a": "hand_L", "b": "hand_R", "cmd": "ragdoll.tie"}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-turn"></a>

### `ragdoll.turn` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

Once, a torque on the trunk turns its front towards a direction, in proportion to how far it is. No history.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last ragdoll.build, else the selection's first character |
| `dir` | `vec3` |  | `[0.0, 1.0, 0.0]` | the direction the front of the trunk goes to, [x, y, z]; absent: up |
| `strength` | `float` |  | `2.0` | how hard |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ok2` | `bool` | yes | true when the character has a body |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.turn", "dir": [0, 1, 0], "strength": 2}`

<a id="cmd-ragdoll-unpin"></a>

### `ragdoll.unpin` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

Drops the pins of a character, or with no key of every character; one history entry.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: every character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `unpinned` | `int` | yes | how many pins went |

Example: `{"cmd": "ragdoll.unpin"}`

## Group: record

<a id="cmd-record-start"></a>

### `record.start` · *HS2 parity*

Starts recording the view as numbered PNG files at a fixed time step: from the next frame on every frame of the scene advances by exactly 1 / fps whatever the real time and is captured as the capture command captures; frames of the global pause are not written. A video is made from the files with an external tool.

- flags `writesFiles`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | `path` |  |  | the folder: relative to the output root, or absolute under the output root or the UserData folder (default \<output root>/record/\<yyyyMMdd_HHmmss>); created when missing |
| `width` | `int` |  | `1280` | the image width (16-8192) |
| `height` | `int` |  | `720` | the image height (16-8192) |
| `fps` | `int` |  | `30` | frames per second of scene time (1-120): the time step is 1 / fps |
| `fixed` | `bool` |  | `true` | fixed-step recording (false is not supported) |
| `supersample` | `int` |  | `1` | 1, 2 or 4: render at k times the size and reduce |
| `frame` | `bool` |  | `false` | draw the scene's frame image |
| `frames` | `int` |  | `0` | frames to record (0 = until record.stop; at most 999999) |
| `timeline` | `bool` |  | `false` | play the scene's Timeline from 0 at the first frame and stop after one pass (or at its end when it does not loop) |
| `name` | `string` |  | `"frame_"` | the file name prefix |
| `overwrite` | `bool` |  | `false` | accept a folder that holds files (files of the same names are replaced, others kept) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | `path` | yes | the absolute folder |
| `pattern` | `string` | yes | the file names: the prefix + %06d.png, counting from 1 |
| `width` | `int` | yes |  |
| `height` | `int` | yes |  |
| `renderWidth` | `int` | yes | the render size (with supersampling) |
| `renderHeight` | `int` | yes |  |
| `fps` | `int` | yes |  |
| `dt` | `float` | yes | the time step in seconds (as single precision) |

Example: `{"cmd": "record.start", "fps": 30, "height": 720, "timeline": true, "width": 1280}`

<a id="cmd-record-status"></a>

### `record.status`

The recording now, or the result of the last one until the next start.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `recording` | `bool` | yes | frames are being recorded |
| `frames` | `int` | yes | frames recorded |
| `written` | `int` | yes | files written |
| `pending` | `int` | yes | frames whose file is not written yet |
| `path` | `path` | yes | the folder (null before the first recording) |
| `pattern` | `string` | yes | the file names |
| `fps` | `int` | yes |  |
| `dt` | `float` | yes |  |
| `sceneTime` | `float` | yes | the scene time of the last recorded frame (null without frames) |
| `stoppedBy` | `string` | yes | why the last recording stopped (null while recording) |
| `failure` | `string` | yes | the first failure, or null (record.json writes it as error) |
| `maxPending` | `int` | yes |  |
| `waits` | `int` | yes |  |
| `syncFrames` | `int` | yes |  |
| `held` | `int` | yes |  |

Example: `{"cmd": "record.status"}`

<a id="cmd-record-stop"></a>

### `record.stop` · *HS2 parity*

Stops the recording; the reply comes when every pending frame is written (at most 60 s), after record.json (the time step and the scene time of every frame) is written into the folder.

- flags `writesFiles`, `defers`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 90 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `frames` | `int` | yes | frames recorded |
| `width` | `int` | yes |  |
| `height` | `int` | yes |  |
| `path` | `path` | yes | the folder |
| `pattern` | `string` | yes | the file names |
| `written` | `int` | yes | files written |
| `stoppedBy` | `string` | yes | stop, frames, pass, ended, scene, quit, error, disk or limit |
| `sceneTimeStart` | `float` | yes | the scene time of the first frame (null without frames) |
| `sceneTimeEnd` | `float` | yes | the scene time of the last frame (null without frames) |
| `ms` | `float` | yes | milliseconds from the start to the last file written |
| `failure` | `string` | yes | the first failure, or null (record.json writes it as error) |
| `maxPending` | `int` | yes | the most frames between their render and their file at one time (at most 4) |
| `waits` | `int` | yes | frames before which the recording waited for the pending frames |
| `syncFrames` | `int` | yes | frames rendered and written at once because the pending frames did not complete in time |
| `held` | `int` | yes | frames of the global pause (not written) |

Example: `{"cmd": "record.stop"}`

## Group: routes

<a id="cmd-route-add"></a>

### `route.add` · *HS2 parity*

Adds a route with its start point at route-local zero (one undo entry "Add route"); placed like the other adds.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `parent` | `node` |  |  | parent node (default the scene root; never inside a route) |
| `point` | `string` |  |  | attach point under a character parent |
| `index` | `int` |  | `-1` | sibling index (default the end) |
| `pos` | `vec3` |  |  | local position |
| `rot` | `euler` |  |  | local rotation (Euler degrees) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `string` | yes | the new route |
| `points` | `array<string>` | yes | [the start point's id] |

Example: `{"cmd": "route.add", "pos": [0, 0, 0]}`

<a id="cmd-route-addpoint"></a>

### `route.addPoint` · *HS2 parity*

Adds a point (one undo entry "Add route point"); default appended, continuing the path (last + (last - previous)); the option routes.pointLimit allows 11 points.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | the route |
| `index` | `int` |  |  | 1 … count (default the end) |
| `pos` | `vec3` |  |  | route-local position (default the continued path) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `string` | yes |  |
| `point` | `string` | yes | the new point's id ("id" is an envelope field) |
| `index` | `int` | yes |  |
| `count` | `int` | yes | points afterwards |

<a id="cmd-route-deletepoint"></a>

### `route.deletePoint` · *HS2 parity*

Deletes points (one undo entry "Delete route point"); the start point cannot be deleted.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | the route |
| `point` | `any` |  |  | a point id or index |
| `points` | `array<any>` |  |  | several |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `deleted` | `array<string>` | yes | ids |
| `count` | `int` | yes | points afterwards |

<a id="cmd-route-list"></a>

### `route.list` · *HS2 parity*

Every route of the scene in pre-order with its state.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `routes` | `array<object>` | yes | {key, name, state, points, parent} |

<a id="cmd-route-play"></a>

### `route.play` · *HS2 parity*

Plays routes from the start (an ended one restarts; restart also restarts playing ones); not recorded.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a route |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several |
| `all` | `bool` |  |  | every route of the scene |
| `restart` | `bool` |  | `false` | also restart playing routes |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `routes` | `array<object>` | yes | {key, state} |

<a id="cmd-route-set"></a>

### `route.set` · *HS2 parity*

Sets route members (loop, showLine, orient none / xy / y, color) on one or several routes (one undo entry "Route").

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a route |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several routes |
| `loop` | `bool` |  |  | loop the route (two points: HS2's ping-pong) |
| `showLine` | `bool` |  |  | draw the route line and markers |
| `orient` | `enum(none\|xy\|y)` |  |  | none, xy (orient to the path) or y (yaw only) |
| `color` | `color` |  |  | [r, g, b(, a)] (alpha 1 when omitted) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | canonical paths |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes |  |

<a id="cmd-route-setpoint"></a>

### `route.setPoint` · *HS2 parity*

Sets point members (one undo entry "Route point"): pos (not the start point), rot (the start point only), speed, ease (one of the 32 names), connection line / curve (curve writes the midpoint aid), link, aid {pos, init}, resetAid.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | the route |
| `point` | `any` |  |  | a point id or index |
| `points` | `array<any>` |  |  | several (pos / rot / aid: the first) |
| `pos` | `vec3` |  |  | route-local position |
| `rot` | `euler` |  |  | rotation of the start point |
| `speed` | `float` |  |  | stored as given (the UI's range 0.1-30) |
| `ease` | `string` |  |  | a StudioTween ease name |
| `connection` | `enum(line\|curve)` |  |  | line or curve (curve writes the midpoint aid) |
| `link` | `bool` |  |  | merge this Curve point into the previous curve chain |
| `aid` | `object` |  |  | {pos (point-local), init} |
| `resetAid` | `bool` |  |  | the midpoint rule again |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `aid` | `object` | yes | the first point's aid {pos, init} or null |

<a id="cmd-route-state"></a>

### `route.state` · *HS2 parity*

A route's document values, its runtime state (stopped / playing / ended, route time, segments of the HS2 timing rule), the child root's pose and the drawn line.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | the route |
| `line` | `bool` |  | `true` | include the line's world vertices |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `state` | `string` | yes | stopped, playing or ended |
| `playing` | `bool` | yes | route.playing in the model |
| `time` | `float` | yes | route time τ (s) |
| `total` | `float` | yes | total duration D (null = endless) |
| `segment` | `int` | yes | queued segment index |
| `percentage` | `float` | yes | tween percentage p |
| `eased` | `float` | yes | spline parameter s |
| `segments` | `array<object>` | yes | {from, to, duration, speed, ease, connection, linked [ids], lead} |
| `childRoot` | `object` | yes | {pos, rot (world, Euler), localPos, localRot} |
| `points` | `array<object>` | yes | {id, index, pos, rot, speed, ease, connection, link, aid {pos, init} \| null, world} |
| `line` | `object` | yes | {count, expected, points [world]} (null with one point) |

<a id="cmd-route-stop"></a>

### `route.stop` · *HS2 parity*

Stops routes (the child root back at the start point); not recorded.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a route |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several |
| `all` | `bool` |  |  | every route of the scene |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `routes` | `array<object>` | yes | {key, state} |

| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several nodes; one of key, keys |

The undo and redo history (labels, newest first) and the model version.

No arguments.

### `obj.transform` · *HS2 parity*

Sets a node's parent-local transform (one undo entry "Transform").

| `key` | `node` | yes |  | the node |
| `pos` | `vec3` |  |  | transform.pos (parent-local) |
| `rot` | `euler` |  |  | transform.rot (Euler degrees) |
| `scale` | `vec3` |  |  | transform.scale; kinds without a scale channel accept only [1, 1, 1] |

| `object` | `object` | yes | the node as in scene.objects |

Example: `{"cmd": "obj.transform", "key": "<id>", "pos": [1, 0, 2], "rot": [0, 30, 0]}`

The scene in HS2's shape: map, view camera and every node (pre-order) with HS2Next additions.

No arguments.

| `camera` | `object` | yes | camera.get's camera |

<a id="cmd-scene-doc"></a>

### `scene.doc`

The canonical documents a save would write now (scene.json, every character's chara.json / coord.json) with their SHA-256.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | one node only (its node object and documents) |
| `capture` | `bool` |  | `true` | write the runtime-owned values (animation time, look, ...) into the model first, as a save does |
| `embed` | `bool` |  | `false` | documents as embedded JSON values instead of strings |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |

| `scene` | `any` |  | scene.json text (or object with embed) |
| `charas` | `object` |  | {\<node id>: {chara, coord} \| null (card-backed)} |
| `res` | `array<string>` |  | res/ names the characters' documents reference |
| `node` | `any` |  | with key: the node object |
| `sha256` | `string` | yes | hex SHA-256 of the UTF-8 texts in node order |
| `nodes` | `int` |  | node count |
| `version` | `int` |  | model version |
| `dirty` | `bool` |  | unsaved changes |

<a id="cmd-scene-list"></a>

### `scene.list`

The .hs2nscene files under \<UserData>/scene/ (recursive, by name).

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `q` | `string` |  | `""` | case-insensitive substring of the relative path |
| `limit` | `int` |  | `200` | at most this many |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scenes` | `array<object>` | yes | {name (relative, '/'), bytes, modifiedUtc} |
| `truncated` | `bool` |  | more than limit |

<a id="cmd-scene-load"></a>

### `scene.load` · *HS2 parity*

Opens a .hs2nscene (history cleared); replies after the map has loaded unless wait is false.

- flags `mutates`, `slow`, `defers`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 300 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `path` |  |  | file under \<UserData>/scene/ (.hs2nscene appended when missing); one of name, path |
| `path` | `path` |  |  | relative path under \<UserData>/scene/, or an absolute .hs2nscene path; one of name, path |
| `wait` | `bool` |  | `true` | reply after the map has loaded or failed (play mode / player) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | `string` | yes | absolute path |
| `nodes` | `int` | yes | node count |
| `map` | `object` | yes | {no, name, id, state, loadMs} |
| `findings` | `array<object>` | yes | load findings {severity, code, path, message} |
| `newerFormat` | `bool` | yes | written by a newer HS2Next |
| `missingRequiredExt` | `bool` | yes | a required extension block is not known |
| `built` | `int` | yes | characters built |
| `failed` | `int` | yes | characters not built |

<a id="cmd-scene-new"></a>

### `scene.new` · *HS2 parity*

An empty scene (history cleared, map unloaded, default view; runtime overrides cleared). Not undoable.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `nodes` | `int` | yes | 0 |

<a id="cmd-scene-save"></a>

### `scene.save` · *HS2 parity*

Saves the scene as .hs2nscene under \<UserData>/scene/.

- flags `writesFiles`, `slow`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 120 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `path` |  |  | file name in \<UserData>/scene/ (.hs2nscene appended unless present); one of name, path |
| `path` | `path` |  |  | relative path under \<UserData>/scene/ (subfolders created); one of name, path |
| `overwrite` | `bool` |  | `false` | replace an existing file |
| `thumb` | `enum(render\|keep\|placeholder)` |  | `"render"` | thumbnail: render the view (needs a graphics device, else keep), keep the previous one, or the grey placeholder |
| `title` | `string` |  |  | sets the scene title first (recorded "Title") |
| `clock` | `string` |  |  | ISO-8601 UTC time for createdUtc / modifiedUtc (byte-identical test saves) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | `string` | yes | absolute path |
| `name` | `string` | yes | relative to the scene folder ('/') |
| `bytes` | `int` | yes | file size |
| `thumbnail` | `string` | yes | mode used |
| `findings` | `array<object>` | yes | {severity, code, path, message} |
| `version` | `int` | yes | model version |
| `dirty` | `bool` | yes | false |

Example: `{"cmd": "scene.save", "name": "my scene", "overwrite": true}`
