## Group: script

<a id="cmd-camera-follow"></a>

### `camera.follow` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

The view camera follows a node or one of its bones for some seconds of the scene clock, over the Timeline's camera: the followed point is smoothed, the angles and the distance run from rot and dist to rotTo and distTo; then the camera shows what it showed before. Never saved; no history. seconds 0 or less ends a follow.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `target` | `any` |  |  | {key, bone, offset} or a node's key: a character's bone defaults to cf_J_Spine02, the offset is in world axes; not needed to end a follow |
| `rot` | `euler` |  |  | the camera's angles at the start (absent: [60, 0, 0]) |
| `dist` | `float` |  | `12.0` | the distance at the start |
| `fov` | `float` |  | `0.0` | the field of view; 0: the one shown |
| `seconds` | `float` |  | `2.0` | how long it follows; 0 or less ends a follow |
| `rotTo` | `euler` |  |  | the angles at the end; absent: rot |
| `distTo` | `float` |  |  | the distance at the end; absent: dist |
| `above` | `bool` |  | `false` | the distance never goes below 1 |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `following` | `bool` | yes | a follow runs |
| `until` | `float` | yes | the scene clock's time at which it ends; null when none runs |

Example: `{"cmd": "camera.follow", "dist": 8, "distTo": 5, "rot": [10, 180, 0], "seconds": 3, "target": {"bone": "cf_J_Head", "key": "<id>"}}`

<a id="cmd-chara-accessoryaxis"></a>

### `chara.accessoryAxis` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

The axis of a character's accessory slot read from its meshes: the centre, the main direction pointing away from where the slot hangs, the muzzle at its far end and the anchor, in world coordinates.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |
| `slot` | `int` |  | `0` | the accessory slot, 0-based, as chara.accessories |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `axis` | `vec3` | yes | the unit direction |
| `center` | `vec3` | yes | the mean of the points |
| `muzzle` | `vec3` | yes | the mean of the points within 0.3 units of the far end |
| `anchor` | `vec3` | yes | where the slot hangs |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.accessoryAxis", "key": "<id>", "slot": 2}`

<a id="cmd-dof"></a>

### `dof` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

Sets the depth of field shown, over the Timeline's and the scene's, changing only what is given: on, the focus (a node, a bone, a point, a distance or the camera's pivot, with a blend), the focal size and aperture (or sharp and full), the blur. release and reset hand it back; the scene's own depth of field is never changed. Never saved; no history.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `on` | `bool` |  |  | the depth of field on or off |
| `focus` | `any` |  |  | "target" or null: the camera's pivot; a node's key (a character between the eyes, an item its point); {key, bone, offset}; {point: [x, y, z]}; {dist: d} along the view |
| `blend` | `float` |  | `0.0` | seconds of the scene clock over which the focus goes from where it is to the new one |
| `ease` | `enum(linear\|in\|out\|inout)` |  | `"inout"` | the blend's curve |
| `size` | `float` |  |  | the focal size, 0 to 2 |
| `aperture` | `float` |  |  | the aperture, 0 to 0.99 |
| `sharp` | `float` |  |  | with full instead of size and aperture: the sharp range (alone: full 3) |
| `full` | `float` |  |  | the distance factor of the full blur (alone: sharp 0.1); clearly larger than 1 + sharp |
| `blur` | `float` |  |  | the largest blur on a 1080-line frame; 0 or less: the renderer's |
| `near` | `bool` |  |  | false is not supported: the near side is always blurred |
| `release` | `bool` |  | `false` | hand the depth of field back to the Timeline or the scene |
| `reset` | `bool` |  | `false` | the same as release: the scene's own depth of field stays as it is |
| `overlap` | `any` |  |  | not a setting of HS2Next's depth of field |
| `highRes` | `any` |  |  | not a setting of HS2Next's depth of field |
| `blurType` | `any` |  |  | not a setting of HS2Next's depth of field |
| `samples` | `any` |  |  | not a setting of HS2Next's depth of field |
| `visualize` | `any` |  |  | not a setting of HS2Next's depth of field |
| `maxBlurSize` | `any` |  |  | not a setting of HS2Next's depth of field |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `dof` | `object` | yes | {on, size, aperture, sharp, full, blur, focus, driver, blending}: sharp and full when the size and aperture came from them, focus as given ("target" for the pivot), driver dof, timeline or scene |

Example: `{"blend": 1.5, "cmd": "dof", "focus": "<id>", "full": 3, "on": true, "sharp": 0.1}`

<a id="cmd-obj-moveto"></a>

### `obj.moveTo` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

Moves a node in a straight line over the ground towards a target on the scene clock, stopping some units before it: the node turns to face the target at once, then its position runs from where it is shown to the end over the given seconds (the pause holds it). The end goes into the scene; nothing enters the history. A new move of the node replaces the running one.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | the node that moves |
| `target` | `any` | yes |  | where it goes: [x, y, z], {key, bone, offset} or a node's key; a character's bone defaults to cf_J_Spine02; the height is not used |
| `stop` | `float` |  | `4.0` | the distance at which it stops before the target |
| `time` | `float` |  | `1.0` | the seconds the move takes (at least 0.05) |
| `stanceSpeed` | `float` |  | `0.0` | above 0: a character's animation speed becomes the speed of the move divided by this, between 0.2 and 2 |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `to` | `vec3` | yes | where the move ends |
| `distance` | `float` | yes | the distance it covers |

Example: `{"cmd": "obj.moveTo", "key": "<id>", "stop": 4, "target": {"key": "<id>"}, "time": 2}`

<a id="cmd-script-add"></a>

### `script.add` · *experimental*

Adds a copy of a store row to the scene as a new script. One history entry.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `no` | `int` | yes |  | a store row's number |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `string` | yes | the new scene script's id |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.add", "no": 3}`

<a id="cmd-script-borrow"></a>

### `script.borrow` · *experimental*

Borrows timed commands and track keys of a time window from another scene script or a store row into a scene script, moved by shift, roles renamed; camera and depth-of-field keys only with camera. A borrowed key at a time the track has replaces it. One history entry.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `any` | yes |  | a scene script: its id, its name when one script has it, or its index from 0: the script that borrows |
| `from` | `any` |  |  | the scene script borrowed from (as script) |
| `no` | `int` |  |  | the store row borrowed from, in place of from |
| `window` | `array<float>` |  |  | [t0, t1): the times borrowed; absent: all |
| `shift` | `float` |  | `0.0` | seconds added to every borrowed time |
| `roles` | `object` |  |  | {role of the source: role of the script}; a role left out keeps its name |
| `events` | `bool` |  | `true` | borrow timed commands |
| `tracks` | `bool` |  | `true` | borrow track keys |
| `camera` | `bool` |  | `false` | also the camera and depth-of-field keys |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `events` | `int` | yes | timed commands borrowed |
| `keys` | `int` | yes | keys borrowed |
| `tracks` | `int` | yes | tracks that took keys, new ones included |
| `replaced` | `int` | yes | keys of the script replaced by a borrowed key at their time |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.borrow", "from": 1, "roles": {"x": "a"}, "script": 0, "shift": -2, "window": [2, 5]}`

<a id="cmd-script-capture"></a>

### `script.capture` · *experimental*

Writes the edits of the loaded Timeline back into its script: track keys, focus nodes and timed commands' strings that name a cast node become the role again. One history entry.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `any` |  |  | a scene script: its id, its name when one script has it, or its index from 0; absent: the one the Timeline was loaded from |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `string` | yes | the script's id |
| `tracks` | `int` | yes | its Timeline's tracks |
| `keys` | `int` | yes | their keys |
| `events` | `int` | yes | its timed commands |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.capture"}`

<a id="cmd-script-cast"></a>

### `script.cast` · *experimental*

Casts a scene script's roles to nodes of the scene: a character role to a character, a prop role to an item; null leaves a role uncast. One history entry.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `any` | yes |  | a scene script: its id, its name when one script has it, or its index from 0 |
| `roles` | `object` | yes |  | {role: key or null} |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cast` | `object` | yes | {role: key} of the cast roles after the change |
| `uncast` | `array<string>` | yes | the roles not cast |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.cast", "roles": {"a": "<id>"}, "script": 0}`

<a id="cmd-script-delete"></a>

### `script.delete` · *experimental*

Hides a store row, or with restore shows it again; no file is touched. No history.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `no` | `int` | yes |  | a store row's number |
| `restore` | `bool` |  | `false` | show a deleted row again |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `deleted` | `bool` | yes | the row is deleted now |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.delete", "no": 3}`

<a id="cmd-script-export"></a>

### `script.export` · *experimental*

Writes a scene script (without its cast) or a store row to a script file. No history.

- flags `writesFiles`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `any` |  |  | a scene script: its id, its name when one script has it, or its index from 0 |
| `no` | `int` |  |  | a store row's number, in place of script |
| `path` | `path` | yes |  | the file: relative to the output root, or absolute under the output root or the user data folder; .hs2nscript is added when missing; a file there is replaced |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | `string` | yes | the file written |
| `bytes` | `int` | yes | its size |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.export", "path": "walk.hs2nscript", "script": 0}`

<a id="cmd-script-get"></a>

### `script.get` · *experimental*

A scene script's document with its cast, or the document of a store row.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `any` |  |  | a scene script: its id, its name when one script has it, or its index from 0 |
| `no` | `int` |  |  | a store row's number, in place of script |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `object` | yes | the script's document (format hs2next.script): name, map, roles, setup, prepare, timeline |
| `cast` | `object` | yes | {role: key}: the scene script's cast; empty for a store row |
| `prepared` | `string` | yes | the preparation's mark, null when it was not prepared |
| `loaded` | `bool` | yes | the Timeline was loaded from this script |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.get", "script": 0}`

<a id="cmd-script-import"></a>

### `script.import` · *experimental*

Imports a script file (.hs2nscript, at most 16 MiB) into the store or into the scene; a script of the same id is replaced only with replace, else the copy gets a new id. One history entry with to scene.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `path` | `path` | yes |  | the file: relative to the output root, or absolute |
| `to` | `enum(store\|scene)` |  | `"store"` | where the script goes |
| `replace` | `bool` |  | `false` | replace the store row or the scene script of the file's id |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `no` | `int` | yes | to store: the store row |
| `script` | `string` | yes | to scene: the scene script's id |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.import", "path": "walk.hs2nscript", "to": "scene"}`

<a id="cmd-script-keep"></a>

### `script.keep` · *experimental*

Keeps a scene script in the script store: the row of its id is replaced (same number), else a new row. No history.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `any` | yes |  | a scene script: its id, its name when one script has it, or its index from 0 |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `no` | `int` | yes | the store row |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.keep", "script": 0}`

<a id="cmd-script-list"></a>

### `script.list` · *experimental*

The scene's scripts with their roles and cast, or with store the rows of the script store.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `store` | `bool` |  | `false` | list the store's rows instead of the scene's scripts |
| `q` | `string` |  | `""` | text the name (or the id, or a row's number) holds, ignoring case; empty: every one |
| `deleted` | `bool` |  | `false` | with store: also the deleted rows |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `scripts` | `array<object>` | yes | the scene's scripts: {script, name, roles, cast, uncast, prepared, loaded, store}: roles the role names, cast {role: key} of the cast roles, uncast the others, prepared the preparation's mark or null, loaded true for the script the Timeline was loaded from, store the store row it came from or was kept as |
| `loaded` | `string` | yes | the script the Timeline was loaded from, or null |
| `rows` | `array<object>` | yes | with store: {no, id, name, created, modified, deleted}, times in UTC |
| `lastNumber` | `int` | yes | with store: the last number given; numbers are never given again |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.list"}`

<a id="cmd-script-play"></a>

### `script.play` · *experimental*

Plays a scene script: casts its roles (with load: loads the map and the cast that is missing), runs its preparation when it is due (one history entry), sends its set-up, loads its Timeline into the scene's (one history entry when it changes) and plays it, or records one pass with record. A failed step is reported and the play goes on; with the envelope's strict it stops before the Timeline is loaded.

- flags `mutates`, `slow`, `defers`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 600 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `any` | yes |  | a scene script: its id, its name when one script has it, or its index from 0 |
| `from` | `float` |  | `0.0` | the Timeline's time to play from |
| `prepare` | `enum(auto\|always\|never)` |  | `"auto"` | auto: the preparation runs when it was not done for this cast and these steps; always; never |
| `load` | `bool` |  | `true` | load the script's map and the roles that are not in the scene |
| `partial` | `bool` |  | `false` | play with roles uncast: the steps, timed commands and tracks that name them are left out |
| `reload` | `bool` |  | `false` | load the script's Timeline again even when the scene's holds edits of it |
| `discard` | `bool` |  | `false` | replace a Timeline that holds edits of another script |
| `record` | `object` |  |  | record one pass instead of playing: record.start's arguments (path, width, height, fps, ...) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `string` | yes | the script's id |
| `roles` | `object` | yes | {role: key} of the cast |
| `loaded` | `bool` | yes | the Timeline was loaded (it changed) |
| `prepared` | `bool` | yes | the preparation ran |
| `setup` | `object` | yes | {sent, failed} |
| `prepare` | `object` | yes | {sent, failed} |
| `skipped` | `int` | yes | steps and timed commands left out for an uncast role |
| `failures` | `array<object>` | yes | {phase, index, cmd, code, message} of each failed step |
| `state` | `string` | yes | the Timeline's state: playing, paused, stopped, ended |
| `time` | `float` | yes | the Timeline's time |
| `record` | `object` |  | with record: record.start's reply |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.play", "script": 0}`

<a id="cmd-script-purge"></a>

### `script.purge` · *experimental*

Removes the files of the store's deleted rows and those rows; nothing else. Nothing is removed while the store's index cannot be read. No history.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `rows` | `int` | yes | rows removed |
| `files` | `int` | yes | files removed |
| `bytes` | `int` | yes | their bytes |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.purge"}`

<a id="cmd-script-remove"></a>

### `script.remove` · *experimental*

Removes a script from the scene. When the Timeline was loaded from it, the Timeline stays as it is without its mark. One history entry.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `any` | yes |  | a scene script: its id, its name when one script has it, or its index from 0 |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `removed` | `string` | yes | the removed script's id |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.remove", "script": "Walk"}`

<a id="cmd-script-set"></a>

### `script.set` · *experimental*

Puts a script document into the scene: a new script, or with replace the scene script of its id (which keeps its cast). Tracks and timed commands may be given in the form timeline.build takes. One history entry.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `object` | yes |  | the document: {format: "hs2next.script", version: 1, id, name, description, map, roles, setup, prepare, timeline} |
| `replace` | `bool` |  | `false` | replace the scene script of the document's id instead of adding a new script |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `string` | yes | the script's id |
| `roles` | `int` | yes | its roles |
| `steps` | `int` | yes | its set-up and preparation steps |
| `tracks` | `int` | yes | its Timeline's tracks |
| `keys` | `int` | yes | their keys |
| `events` | `int` | yes | its timed commands |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.set", "script": {"format": "hs2next.script", "name": "Walk", "roles": [{"kind": "chara", "role": "a"}], "timeline": {"duration": 5, "events": [], "tracks": []}, "version": 1}}`

<a id="cmd-script-status"></a>

### `script.status` · *experimental*

The state of the scene script the Timeline was loaded from (else the one last played): the Timeline's state and time, the cast, the last play's failures, the timed commands fired, what the moves, the follow and the dof command hold, a recording.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `script` | `string` | yes | the script's id, or null |
| `name` | `string` | yes | its name |
| `state` | `string` | yes | the Timeline's state: none, stopped, playing, paused, ended |
| `time` | `float` | yes | the Timeline's time |
| `duration` | `float` | yes | the Timeline's length |
| `loop` | `bool` | yes | the Timeline loops |
| `roles` | `object` | yes | {role: key} of the cast roles |
| `uncast` | `array<string>` | yes | the roles not cast |
| `prepared` | `string` | yes | the preparation's mark, or null |
| `lastPlay` | `object` | yes | {setup, prepare, skipped, failures} of the last play, or null |
| `eventsFired` | `int` | yes | timed commands fired |
| `eventErrors` | `int` | yes | timed commands that failed |
| `lastEventError` | `object` | yes | {event, code, message}, or null |
| `slots` | `object` | yes | {moves, follow, dof}: the moves running, the camera follow and the dof command holding |
| `recording` | `bool` | yes | a recording of the script runs |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.status"}`

<a id="cmd-script-stop"></a>

### `script.stop` · *experimental*

Stops the scene script: the Timeline stops and what the moves, the camera follow and the dof command hold is released; with reset the cast's ragdolls are released and the cast's stains dried. No history.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `reset` | `bool` |  | `true` | release the cast's ragdolls and dry the cast's stains |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `state` | `string` | yes | the Timeline's state |
| `released` | `int` | yes | what the moves, the follow and the dof command held, released |
| `ragdolls` | `int` | yes | cast characters whose ragdoll was released |
| `dried` | `int` | yes | cast characters dried |

Example: `{"cmd": "script.stop"}`

## Group: time

<a id="cmd-scene-pause"></a>

### `scene.pause`

Reads or sets the global pause of the scene clock (every scene time consumer stops; the UI, camera, edits, bridge and captures keep working); not saved, reset by scene.new / scene.load.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `paused` | `bool` |  |  | true pauses, false resumes; absent = read |
| `shaderTime` | `string` |  |  | the shader-time scope: all (every scene camera takes the scene clock's time), captures (capture / thumbnail cameras only), off (URP's time) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `paused` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `time` | `float` | yes | scene seconds (SceneClock.Time) |
| `frame` | `int` | yes | SceneClock.Frame |
| `steps` | `int` | yes | steps run since the clock was reset |
| `shaderTime` | `string` | yes | the shader-time scope |

Example: `{"cmd": "scene.pause", "paused": true}`

<a id="cmd-scene-step"></a>

### `scene.step`

Runs n frames of dt while the scene is paused (characters, items, routes, map scripts, particles, map animators, shader time), synchronously.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 300 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `n` | `int` |  | `1` | frames (1–10,000) |
| `dt` | `float` |  | `0.0166666675` | seconds per frame (0 \< dt ≤ 1) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `time` | `float` | yes | scene seconds after the steps |
| `frame` | `int` | yes | SceneClock.Frame |
| `steps` | `int` | yes | steps run since the clock was reset |
| `ms` | `float` | yes | milliseconds the steps took |

Example: `{"cmd": "scene.step", "dt": 0.016666668, "n": 30}`

## Group: timeline

<a id="cmd-overrides-list"></a>

### `overrides.list` · *experimental*

The runtime override slots of every owner (the Timeline, plugins): values laid over the scene for display, never saved. Per slot its node, path, owner, priority, whether it has a value, wins, takes effect and its target exists.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | only the slots of this node (default: every slot, the scene's camera and depth of field first) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `overrides` | `array<object>` | yes | {key, path, owner, priority, hasValue, winning, effective, targetExists, value} |

Example: `{"cmd": "overrides.list"}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-build"></a>

### `timeline.build` · *HS2 parity*

Builds the scene's Timeline from tracks in HS2's input vocabulary (one history entry): the tracks replace the Timeline's tracks, or are added to them with append; the timed commands are kept unless events is given. Then the Timeline stops and plays from 0 unless play is false. Nothing is built when any track or key is refused. The reply carries no XML length: HS2Next writes no Timeline XML.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `duration` | `float` |  | `10.0` | the length in seconds (0 \< duration ≤ 3600); with append the Timeline's own length is kept when absent |
| `tracks` | `array<object>` |  |  | tracks {type, key?, bone?, slot?, raw?, ease?, label?, enabled?, keys: [{t, ease?, …}]}: type camera, dof, pos, rot (with bone: an FK bone of a character), scale, anime, animeSpeed, animeTime, eyes, mouth, eyesOpen, mouthOpen or color; camera keys take target, rot, dist, fov (later keys carry missing members over); dof keys on, focus, size / aperture or sharp / full, blur, near; anime keys group, category, no (+ guid or ref); eyes keys v = the position in the character's eye list (the pattern id with raw); other keys v |
| `play` | `bool` |  | `true` | stop, then play from 0 after building; false keeps the playback state |
| `append` | `bool` |  | `false` | add the tracks to the Timeline's tracks instead of replacing them |
| `returnXml` | `bool` |  | `false` | accepted for HS2 scripts: HS2Next has no Timeline XML (a warning) |
| `blockLength` | `float` |  |  | accepted for HS2 scripts; no effect |
| `loop` | `bool` |  |  | playback wraps at the end (absent: true; with append the Timeline's own) |
| `autoplay` | `bool` |  |  | play from 0 after the scene is loaded (absent: false; with append the Timeline's own) |
| `events` | `array<object>` |  |  | timed commands {t, do, note?, enabled?} replacing the Timeline's (absent: kept) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tracks` | `int` | yes | tracks of the Timeline |
| `keys` | `int` | yes | keys of every track |
| `events` | `int` | yes | timed commands |
| `duration` | `float` | yes | seconds |
| `state` | `enum(none\|stopped\|playing\|paused\|ended)` | yes | the playback state after the build |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.build", "duration": 10, "tracks": [{"keys": [{"dist": 3, "fov": 40, "rot": [10, 0, 0], "t": 0, "target": [0, 1, 0]}, {"rot": [10, 90, 0], "t": 5}], "type": "camera"}]}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-clear"></a>

### `timeline.clear` · *HS2 parity*

Stops the Timeline and removes it from the scene (one history entry); a scene without a Timeline is saved as it was before it had one.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `cleared` | `bool` | yes | the scene had a Timeline |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.clear"}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-events"></a>

### `timeline.events` · *HS2 parity*

Replaces the Timeline's timed commands (one history entry). A timed command is a request {cmd, …} that runs when the playing Timeline passes its time, never on a seek, a pause, a stop or a load; it runs one frame after the time is passed, without an undo entry unless its request says undo: true. A scene without a Timeline gets one of max(10, the latest time) seconds.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `events` | `array<object>` |  |  | timed commands {t, do, note?, enabled?}: do is a request object with cmd and its arguments (no id); commands that build or edit the Timeline are refused |
| `clear` | `bool` |  | `false` | remove every timed command (events, when given, then replace them) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `events` | `int` | yes | timed commands of the Timeline |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.events", "events": [{"do": {"cmd": "chara.face", "eyes": 3, "key": "<node id>"}, "t": 2.5}]}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-get"></a>

### `timeline.get`

The scene's Timeline in its stored form (as the scene file holds it), with the playback state.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `timeline` | `object` | yes | {duration, loop, autoplay, tracks, events}, or null when the scene has none |
| `state` | `string` | yes |  |
| `time` | `float` | yes |  |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.get"}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-key-delete"></a>

### `timeline.key.delete`

Deletes keys of a track by their times (one history entry).

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `track` | `any` | yes |  | the track: its id, or its index |
| `t` | `float` |  |  | the time of one key |
| `times` | `array<float>` |  |  | the times of several keys |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `deleted` | `int` | yes | keys deleted |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.key.delete", "t": 2.5, "track": 0}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-key-move"></a>

### `timeline.key.move`

Moves keys of a track in time (one history entry «Move keys»; with merge, consecutive moves of a drag join one entry). Two keys of a track never share a time, and every key stays within the Timeline.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `track` | `any` | yes |  | the track: its id, or its index |
| `t` | `float` |  |  | the time of one key |
| `times` | `array<float>` |  |  | the times of several keys |
| `to` | `float` |  |  | the new time of the one key |
| `by` | `float` |  |  | seconds added to every key's time |
| `merge` | `bool` |  | `false` | join the history entry of the previous move of this track |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `times` | `array<float>` | yes | the new times, in the order given |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.key.move", "t": 2.5, "to": 3, "track": 0}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-key-set"></a>

### `timeline.key.set`

Sets the key of a track at time t (one history entry «Key»; with merge, a second edit of the same key joins it). Without v the key takes the value the target shows now.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `track` | `any` | yes |  | the track: its id, or its index |
| `t` | `float` | yes |  | the key's time in seconds (within the Timeline) |
| `v` | `any` |  |  | the value in the stored form of the track's type (absent: the value shown now) |
| `ease` | `enum(step\|linear\|inout\|in\|out)` |  |  | the shape of the segment to the next key (absent: the key's own, else inout; anime, eyes and mouth always step) |
| `merge` | `bool` |  | `false` | join the history entry of the previous edit of this key |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `track` | `string` | yes |  |
| `index` | `int` | yes | the key's index in the track |
| `t` | `float` | yes |  |
| `v` | `any` | yes | the stored value |
| `ease` | `string` | yes |  |
| `keys` | `int` | yes | keys of the track |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.key.set", "t": 2.5, "track": 0, "v": [0, 1, 0]}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-pause"></a>

### `timeline.pause` · *HS2 parity*

Pauses a playing Timeline; its values stay shown. Otherwise nothing changes.

- flags none; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `state` | `string` | yes |  |
| `time` | `float` | yes |  |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.pause"}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-play"></a>

### `timeline.play` · *HS2 parity*

Plays the Timeline on the scene clock: from 0 when it is stopped or has ended, from the paused time when it is paused, or from from (also while it plays: a restart). Playing again without from changes nothing. The global pause holds the time.

- flags none; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `from` | `float` |  |  | the start time in seconds (clamped to the Timeline) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `state` | `string` | yes | none, stopped, playing, paused or ended |
| `time` | `float` | yes | the play head in seconds |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.play"}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-seek"></a>

### `timeline.seek` · *HS2 parity*

Moves the play head and shows the Timeline's values at that time at once; no timed command runs. A playing Timeline plays on from there; a stopped or ended one is paused there.

- flags none; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `t` | `float` |  | `0.0` | the time in seconds (clamped to the Timeline) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `state` | `string` | yes |  |
| `time` | `float` | yes |  |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.seek", "t": 2.5}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-set"></a>

### `timeline.set`

Replaces the Timeline with one in its stored form, or changes its length, loop or autoplay (one history entry); a scene without a Timeline gets one. timeline: null removes it.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `timeline` | `object` |  |  | the Timeline in its stored form (as timeline.get answers it), or null to remove it |
| `duration` | `float` |  |  | the length in seconds (0 \< duration ≤ 3600) |
| `loop` | `bool` |  |  | playback wraps at the end |
| `autoplay` | `bool` |  |  | play from 0 after the scene is loaded |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `tracks` | `int` | yes |  |
| `keys` | `int` | yes |  |
| `events` | `int` | yes |  |
| `duration` | `float` | yes |  |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.set", "timeline": {"duration": 10}}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-status"></a>

### `timeline.status` · *HS2 parity*

The Timeline and its playback: state, play head, length, tracks, keys, timed commands and their failures; with perf, the time the playback took per frame while playing.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `perf` | `bool` |  | `false` | add perf: the playback's time per frame over the frames played since the last reset (at most the last 4,096) |
| `reset` | `bool` |  | `false` | with perf: start a new window after this reply |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `playing` | `bool` | yes | the Timeline plays (also while the global pause holds it) |
| `time` | `float` | yes | the play head in seconds (0 without a Timeline) |
| `duration` | `float` | yes | seconds (0 without a Timeline) |
| `interpolables` | `int` | yes | tracks showing a value now |
| `state` | `enum(none\|stopped\|playing\|paused\|ended)` | yes |  |
| `loop` | `bool` | yes | playback wraps at the end (null without a Timeline) |
| `autoplay` | `bool` | yes | plays after the scene is loaded (null without a Timeline) |
| `tracks` | `int` | yes |  |
| `keys` | `int` | yes |  |
| `events` | `int` | yes | timed commands |
| `activeTracks` | `int` | yes | tracks that can show a value (enabled, the first of their channel, their target in the scene) |
| `eventsFired` | `int` | yes | timed commands started since the scene was opened |
| `eventErrors` | `int` | yes | timed commands that failed since the scene was opened |
| `lastEventError` | `object` | yes | {event, code, message} of the last failure, or null |
| `scenePaused` | `bool` | yes | the global pause holds the scene clock |
| `suspended` | `int` | yes | tracks showing your edit until the play head moves |
| `perf` | `object` |  | with perf: {frames, medianUs, p95Us, maxUs} |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.status"}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-stop"></a>

### `timeline.stop` · *HS2 parity*

Stops the Timeline: the play head goes to 0 and the scene shows its own values again. With bake, the values the Timeline shows are first written into the scene (one history entry «Bake Timeline pose»; the camera as the current view, without history).

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `bake` | `bool` |  | `false` | keep the shown pose: write the Timeline's values into the scene before stopping |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `state` | `string` | yes |  |
| `time` | `float` | yes |  |
| `baked` | `int` | yes | values written into the scene by the bake |

Example: `{"bake": true, "cmd": "timeline.stop"}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-track-add"></a>

### `timeline.track.add`

Adds a track (one history entry); a scene without a Timeline gets one. One track per channel: a second track of the same target and value is refused.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `type` | `enum(camera\|dof\|pos\|rot\|scale\|anime\|animeSpeed\|animeTime\|eyes\|mouth\|eyesOpen\|mouthOpen\|color)` | yes |  | the value the track drives |
| `key` | `node` |  |  | the node (none for camera and dof) |
| `bone` | `string` |  |  | rot of a character: the FK bone (its transform name) |
| `slot` | `int` |  |  | color of an item: its colour slot 0-3 (none for a light) |
| `label` | `string` |  |  | a display label (absent: from the target and type) |
| `enabled` | `bool` |  | `true` | a disabled track shows nothing |
| `index` | `int` |  |  | the place in the track list (absent: the end) |
| `keys` | `array<object>` |  |  | keys {t, ease, v} in the stored form (absent: none) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `track` | `string` | yes | the new track's id |
| `index` | `int` | yes | its place in the track list |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.track.add", "key": "<node id>", "type": "pos"}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-track-remove"></a>

### `timeline.track.remove`

Removes tracks with their keys (one history entry).

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `track` | `any` |  |  | a track: its id, or its index |
| `tracks` | `array<any>` |  |  | several tracks (ids or indexes) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `removed` | `int` | yes | tracks removed |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.track.remove", "track": 0}`

<a id="cmd-timeline-track-set"></a>

### `timeline.track.set`

Changes a track's target, bone, slot, label, enabled or place in the list (one history entry).

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.2.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `track` | `any` | yes |  | the track: its id, or its index |
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a new target node of a kind the track's type takes |
| `bone` | `string` |  |  | rot tracks: the FK bone (null: the node's own rotation) |
| `slot` | `int` |  |  | color tracks of an item: the colour slot 0-3 |
| `label` | `string` |  |  | the display label (null: from the target and type) |
| `enabled` | `bool` |  |  | a disabled track shows nothing |
| `index` | `int` |  |  | a new place in the track list |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `track` | `string` | yes | the track's id |
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | the members changed |

Example: `{"cmd": "timeline.track.set", "enabled": false, "track": 0}`

## Group: ui

<a id="cmd-ui-action"></a>

### `ui.action` · *experimental*

Runs a UI action by id (the same behaviour as its key, menu and toolbar button).

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `action` | `string` | yes |  | an action id (edit.undo, tool.move, file.saveAs, add.female, …); `id` is the envelope's request id |
| `args` | `object` |  |  | action arguments (file.saveAs {name\|path}, file.open {path}, add.female {path}, add.item {ref}, ui.language {language}, edit.show {node, page, section, part (the hair part), kind (the clothes kind), slot (the accessory build slot, 0-based)}, …) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `ran` | `bool` | yes |  |
| `result` | `any` | yes | the action's result |

Example: `{"action": "tool.rotate", "cmd": "ui.action"}`

<a id="cmd-ui-input"></a>

### `ui.input` · *experimental*

Synthesises key, text, pointer and wheel events through the UI panel (one event per frame), then waits `frames` frames. Key events Delete / Backspace / Ctrl+A edit a focused text field as typing does.

- flags `mutates`, `defers`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `events` | `array<object>` | yes |  | [{type: key\|text\|pointer\|wheel, key, mods [ctrl, shift, alt], text, x, y (panel px) or target (element name) + ox, oy, button (0 left, 1 right, 2 middle), phase down\|move\|up, delta}] |
| `frames` | `int` |  | `1` | frames to wait after the last event |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `sent` | `int` | yes |  |
| `consumed` | `int` | yes | events whose propagation was stopped (a handler took them) |

<a id="cmd-ui-panel"></a>

### `ui.panel` · *experimental*

Shows, hides, focuses or moves a panel of the fixed layout.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `panel` | `string` | yes |  | panel id (workspace, inspector, add.chara, …, scene, history) |
| `op` | `enum(show\|hide\|focus\|move)` | yes |  | show (and focus) / hide / focus / move to region |
| `region` | `enum(left\|right\|bottom)` |  |  | for move |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `panel` | `object` | yes | {id, region, visible, active} |

<a id="cmd-ui-query"></a>

### `ui.query` · *experimental*

Finds UI elements by name ("name" or "#name") or class (".class"): text, value, enabled, visible, bounds.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; gate `none`; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `string` | yes |  | element name, #name or .class |
| `all` | `bool` |  | `false` | every match (default: the first) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `elements` | `array<object>` | yes | {name, type, text, value, enabled, visible, tooltip, worldBound [x, y, w, h] (panel px), screenRect [x, y, w, h] (window px, y up)} |

### `ui.screenshot` · *experimental*

A screenshot of the window with the UI (shots/ui_\<name>.png in the output root; needs graphics).

|---|---|---|---|---|
| `name` | `string` |  | `"ui"` | file name stem |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |

The Studio UI's state: language, key preset, tool / space / pivot / snap, layout and panels, focus, modal dialog, selection, IME composition, FPS (unavailable without the UI).

## Group: ui scene

<a id="cmd-chara-accessories"></a>

### `chara.accessories` · *HS2 parity*

Accessory visibility of characters by accessory id or build slot (the slot the Studio State tab and the Accessories page name, 0-based), or all (one undo entry); without show / all only reads.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters (one entry) |
| `all` | `bool` |  |  | every worn accessory |
| `show` | `object` |  |  | {\<accessory id> or \<build slot number (0-based; the slot table of the first key)>: bool} |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `accessories` | `object` | yes | {\<id>: bool} (first key) |
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | accessory ids that changed |

<a id="cmd-chara-change"></a>

### `chara.change` · *HS2 parity*

Replaces the appearance of characters with a card (imported) or a .hs2nchara file; the nodes keep id, transform, children, animation, FK / IK, face, look, state and name (one undo entry).

- flags `mutates`, `slow`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 300 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters |
| `path` | `path` | yes |  | a card or .hs2nchara: absolute, a relative .hs2nchara under \<UserData>/chara, a relative card under \<hs2Root>/UserData/chara/female or /male (sex) |
| `sex` | `any` |  | `"female"` | 0 / "male" or 1 / "female": the HS2 card folder of a relative card path |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | the node ids |
| `findings` | `array<object>` | yes | the import's warnings and errors |

<a id="cmd-chara-clothes"></a>

### `chara.clothes` · *HS2 parity*

Clothes states of characters with HS2's cycling and couplings (the stored values are the coupled ones; one undo entry); without all / states only reads.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters (one entry) |
| `all` | `int` |  |  | 0 on, 1 half, 2 off for the eight kinds in order (HS2's All) |
| `states` | `object` |  |  | {kind: state}: kind = top, bottom, bra, shorts, gloves, pantyhose, socks, shoes or 0-7; state 0–2 |
| `next` | `bool` |  | `true` | cycling direction for an unsupported state (true = +1) |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `states` | `array<int>` | yes | the 8 stored states (first key) |
| `supported` | `array<any>` | yes | 8 x the supported states ([] = not a state kind; all when not built) |
| `realized` | `array<int>` | yes | the 8 states as shown (null when not built) |
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | kind names that changed (any key) |
| `keys` | `object` |  | with keys: {\<id>: {states, supported, realized}} |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.clothes", "key": "<id>", "states": {"top": 1}}`

<a id="cmd-chara-clothesdump"></a>

### `chara.clothesDump` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

Number-only dump of a built character's clothes realization: states, supported states, one-piece flags, part and def / half object activity, mask values (HS2 Studio Bridge shape).

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` | yes |  | a built character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `states` | `array<int>` | yes |  |
| `supported` | `array<any>` | yes |  |
| `flags` | `object` | yes | {notBot, notInnerT, notInnerB} |
| `kinds` | `array<object>` | yes | {kind, id, present, active, topDef, topHalf, botDef, botHalf, opt1, opt2} |
| `masks` | `object` | yes | {body {_alpha_a, _alpha_b, _alpha_d}, bra {_alpha_a, _alpha_b}, innerTB, innerB, panst {_alpha_d}} |

<a id="cmd-chara-state"></a>

### `chara.state`

Other state members of characters (liquid, nipple, wet, gloss, son, silhouette, nippleThroughClothes; tears, cheek of the face), stored as given (one undo entry).

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | several characters (one entry) |
| `set` | `object` | yes |  | {liquid: [5 levels 0-2] \| "liquid[i]": level, nipple, wet, gloss, son: {visible, length} \| "son.length", silhouette: {enabled, color} \| "silhouette.color": [r,g,b,a], nippleThroughClothes: bool (false = flat nipples while a top or a bra is on), tears, cheek} |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | members that changed (as given) |
| `noop` | `array<string>` | yes | members that already had the value |

Example: `{"cmd": "chara.state", "key": "<id>", "set": {"gloss": 0.3, "wet": 0.5}}`

Renames a node (one undo entry "Rename"); null or "" = the default name of its kind.

Adds the nodes of another scene file at the root with new ids (its scene settings are ignored; one undo entry); the imported top nodes are selected.

<a id="cmd-select"></a>

### `select` · *HS2 parity*

Sets the node selection (view state, not recorded): keys replace it (empty clears), add appends; sub selects one sub-object (FK bone, IK target, look target, bone, hair bundle, accessory transform set, route point) and its node alone.

- flags `mutates`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | one node |
| `keys` | `array<node>` |  |  | the nodes (empty = clear) |
| `add` | `bool` |  | `false` | append to the selection instead of replacing it |
| `sub` | `string` |  |  | "\<id>/fk/\<bone>", "\<id>/ik/\<Target>", "\<id>/look", "\<id>/bone/\<address>", "\<id>/hair/\<part>/\<index>" (part back, front, side or option), "\<id>/accmove/\<accessoryId>/\<set>" (set 0 or 1) or "\<routeId>/points/\<pointId>[/aid]"; null clears the sub-selection |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `selected` | `array<string>` | yes | ids in order (primary first) |
| `primary` | `string` | yes |  |
| `sub` | `string` | yes | the sub-selection or null |

Example: `{"cmd": "select", "keys": ["<id>"]}`

<a id="cmd-selection"></a>

### `selection`

The node selection and the sub-selection.

- flags `readOnly`; owner `core`; since 1.0.0; timeout 60 s

No arguments.

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `selected` | `array<string>` | yes |  |
| `primary` | `string` | yes |  |
| `sub` | `string` | yes |  |

## Group: wet

<a id="cmd-ground-probe"></a>

### `ground.probe` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

The highest upward surface of the map under each point, between its height plus above and its height minus below. Characters and items are not the map.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `points` | `array<any>` | yes |  | each [x, z] or [x, y, z] |
| `y` | `float` |  | `0.0` | the height of a point given as [x, z] |
| `above` | `float` |  | `6.0` | how far above a point the search starts |
| `below` | `float` |  | `30.0` | how far below a point it ends |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `hits` | `array<object>` | yes | {x, z, y, normal} per point: y and normal are null where nothing answers |
| `source` | `string` | yes | what the first answer came from: gpu, mesh, terrain or collider; null with no answer |

Example: `{"cmd": "ground.probe", "points": [[0, 0], [1, 0, 2]]}`

<a id="cmd-wet-attach"></a>

### `wet.attach` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

Clears the character's stains and puddles, then adds one stain, dry, from where the ray first meets the character, or from the default origin with neither from nor target; one history entry. A ray that meets nothing changes nothing. The character becomes the one a command without a key means.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last wet.attach, else the selection's first character |
| `from` | `any` |  |  | where the ray starts: [x, y, z], {key, bone, offset}, {key, accessory} (the muzzle chara.accessoryAxis reads for that accessory slot of a character), or an item's key (its far end towards the target); alone, the ray goes to the character's hips |
| `target` | `any` |  |  | what the ray aims at: [x, y, z] or {key, bone, offset}; alone, the ray comes from 1.5 units in front of it; the ray runs past it by half |
| `preset` | `string` |  | `"water"` | water or blood |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `status` | `string` | yes | ok |
| `floor` | `float` |  | the height drops land on, as last measured: here at the attach, under the character; absent when nothing gives one |
| `stain` | `string` | yes | the stain's name |

Example: `{"cmd": "wet.attach", "key": "<id>", "preset": "water"}`

<a id="cmd-wet-dry"></a>

### `wet.dry` · *experimental*

A stain goes back to 0 and stops; its drops go, and with puddles its puddles. What a kept stain froze stays.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last wet.attach, else the selection's first character |
| `name` | `string` |  |  | a stain of the character by its name; absent: every stain that is not kept |
| `puddles` | `bool` |  | `true` | their puddles go too |

Example: `{"cmd": "wet.dry", "puddles": false}`

<a id="cmd-wet-floor"></a>

### `wet.floor` · *experimental*

Reads the height drops land on, or sets it: map (the map's surface under the origin as it stands, else the feet), feet or fixed; then the offset. The puddles move by the difference; a character that moves moves none. A fixed floor with no height takes the floor of now. While the character has no stain the values wait, as those of wet.set.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last wet.attach, else the selection's first character |
| `mode` | `enum(map\|feet\|fixed)` |  |  | how the height is found |
| `offset` | `float` |  |  | added to the height in every mode |
| `height` | `float` |  |  | the world height of a fixed floor |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `y` | `float` | yes | the height now, offset included |
| `source` | `string` | yes | map, feet, fixed or none |

Example: `{"cmd": "wet.floor", "mode": "feet", "offset": 0.1}`

<a id="cmd-wet-keep"></a>

### `wet.keep` · *experimental*

Freezes the current stain as a layer: its clock stops where it is, it takes no further edit and its puddles keep their colour. A new current stain with the same origin and values starts dry on top.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last wet.attach, else the selection's first character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `stain` | `string` | yes | the new stain's name |

Example: `{"cmd": "wet.keep"}`

<a id="cmd-wet-remove"></a>

### `wet.remove` · *experimental*

Takes a stain and its puddles away, or with all the character's whole block.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last wet.attach, else the selection's first character |
| `name` | `string` |  |  | a stain of the character by its name; absent: the current stain |
| `all` | `bool` |  | `false` | every stain and puddle, and the block's switches |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `removed` | `int` | yes | stains removed |

Example: `{"all": true, "cmd": "wet.remove"}`

<a id="cmd-wet-set"></a>

### `wet.set` · *experimental*

Sets values of a stain or of the character's block in one history entry: by the names maxRadius, spreadTime, dripTail, dripRate, puddleGrowth, puddleScale and showMarker, or by the member's path, as look.amount, shape.up, drips.color, puddle.edge, origin.down, hold, loop, preset, film.gloss, mirror.scale, targets.hair, floor.offset. Numbers are stored as given; the ranges act when a value is applied. A change of shape or origin bakes the stain again. While the character has no stain the values wait for its next wet.attach or wet.stain; they are not saved. A kept stain takes no value. The clock, the maps and the lists have their own commands.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last wet.attach, else the selection's first character |
| `name` | `string` |  |  | a stain of the character by its name; absent: the current stain |
| `values` | `object` | yes |  | name or path: value |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `changed` | `array<string>` | yes | the names given that changed something |

Example: `{"cmd": "wet.set", "values": {"look.amount": 0.6, "maxRadius": 3}}`

<a id="cmd-wet-stain"></a>

### `wet.stain` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

Adds a named stain, dry, or replaces the one of that name, on top of what the character has; one history entry. With nothing but a name that a stain has, that stain is applied again on what the character wears now, for the parts without a live map. Values given by wet.set while the character had no stain count here unless an argument says otherwise.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last wet.attach, else the selection's first character |
| `name` | `string` |  | `"stain"` | the stain's name, at most 64 characters |
| `preset` | `string` |  | `"water"` | water or blood |
| `from` | `any` |  |  | where the ray starts: [x, y, z], {key, bone, offset}, {key, accessory} (the muzzle chara.accessoryAxis reads for that accessory slot of a character), or an item's key (its far end towards the target); alone, the ray goes to the character's hips |
| `target` | `any` |  |  | what the ray aims at: [x, y, z] or {key, bone, offset}; alone, the ray comes from 1.5 units in front of it; the ray runs past it by half |
| `radius` | `float` |  | `1.8` | how far it spreads, in units |
| `spread` | `float` |  | `3.0` | the seconds it takes |
| `tail` | `float` |  | `5.0` | the seconds the dripping takes to end after the spread |
| `skinDrips` | `bool` |  | `false` | skin and face shed drops too |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `status` | `string` | yes | ok |

Example: `{"cmd": "wet.stain", "from": [0, 10, 10], "key": "<id>", "name": "first", "preset": "blood", "target": {"bone": "cf_J_Spine03", "key": "<id>", "offset": [0, 0, 0.2]}}`

<a id="cmd-wet-start"></a>

### `wet.start` · *experimental*

A stain runs from 0. Its clock is the scene clock: the pause stops it, a step steps it, a Timeline seek moves nothing.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last wet.attach, else the selection's first character |
| `name` | `string` |  |  | a stain of the character by its name; absent: the current stain |
| `loop` | `bool` |  |  | spread, drip, hold, dry, again; absent: as it is |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `t` | `float` | yes | the stain's clock |

Example: `{"cmd": "wet.start", "loop": true}`

<a id="cmd-wet-status"></a>

### `wet.status` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

How the stains stand: with a key that character, with none every character that has a stain.

- flags `readOnly`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `stains` | `array<string>` | yes | the names of the stains of the characters below, each once |
| `characters` | `array<object>` | yes | {key, current, t, running, radius, targets, unreadable, tiled, noFilm, puddles, floor, floorSource, maps {live, dormant}}: current the name of the current stain or null, t its clock in seconds, radius its radius now, targets the parts that show a stain, unreadable the parts that cannot show one, tiled the parts a tiled image leaves out, noFilm the clothes with colour and no gloss, puddles, floor the height drops land on, as last measured (when the character's stains change or one starts, when the character is built or loaded, when the map changes, and while drops fall once the origin moved more than 0.05 units, at most every 0.25 s of scene time): while no stain runs it keeps the last measured value; it is not saved, and a load measures it again in the loaded pose; null with floorSource none, floorSource map, feet, fixed or none, maps.live the maps that act and maps.dormant those kept for a part that is not there |

Example: `{"cmd": "wet.status"}`

<a id="cmd-wet-use"></a>

### `wet.use` · *HS2 parity* · *experimental*

The named stain becomes current; with start it runs from 0. What is on the cloth stays.

- flags `mutates`, `experimental`; owner `core`; since 1.3.0; timeout 60 s

| Argument | Type | Required | Default | Description |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `key` | `node` |  |  | a character; absent: the character of the last wet.attach, else the selection's first character |
| `name` | `string` | yes |  | a stain of the character by its name |
| `start` | `bool` |  | `true` | run it from 0 |

| Returns | Type | Always | Description |
|---|---|---|---|
| `status` | `string` | yes | ok |

Example: `{"cmd": "wet.use", "name": "first"}`
